### Pricing
Mix variant="outline" and variant="solid" to emphasize a highlighted plan.
```vue
<div style="display: grid; gap: 16px; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); max-width: min(100%, 720px)">
  <Card
    v-for="plan in [
      { name: 'Starter', price: '$0', highlighted: false, features: ['1 project', 'Community support', 'Plex UI core'] },
      { name: 'Pro', price: '$19', highlighted: true, features: ['Unlimited projects', 'Priority support', 'Custom domains', 'Early components'] },
    ]"
    :key="plan.name"
    :variant="plan.highlighted ? 'solid' : 'outline'"
  >
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>{{ plan.name }}</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>
        <span style="font-size: 28px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--color-text)">{{ plan.price }}</span>
        <span style="margin-left: 4px">/ month</span>
      </CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>
    <CardContent>
      <ul style="list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px">
        <li v-for="feature in plan.features" :key="feature" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px">
          <Check style="width: 16px; height: 16px; color: var(--color-text-success-soft)" />
          {{ feature }}
        </li>
      </ul>
    </CardContent>
    <CardFooter style="margin-top: auto; padding-top: 4px">
      <Button
        :color="plan.highlighted ? 'primary' : 'secondary'"
        :variant="plan.highlighted ? 'solid' : 'outline'"
        block
      >
        Choose {{ plan.name }}
      </Button>
    </CardFooter>
  </Card>
</div>
```

## Checkbox
Checkbox for binary on/off selection with solid, pill, and ghost variants, an indeterminate state, and rich labels with inline descriptions.
Import: import { Checkbox } from "@plexui/vue/components/Checkbox"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/checkbox
Props:
  - variant ("solid" | "ghost") default: "solid" — Visual style: solid (filled background) or ghost (checkmark only)
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded indicator corners
  - checked / v-model:checked (boolean | "indeterminate") — Controlled state
  - default-checked (boolean | "indeterminate") — Initial state (uncontrolled)
  - label (string) — Accessible label rendered next to the checkbox
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content; takes precedence over the label prop
  - description (string) — Second line below the label, linked via aria-describedby (or the description slot)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent interaction
  - required (boolean) — Required for form submission
  - name (string) — Form field name
  - value (string) default: "on" — Value when submitted
  - orientation ("left" | "right") default: "left" — Checkbox position relative to the label
  - class (string) — CSS classes applied to the wrapper node
  - id (string) — The id of the checkbox element (generated when omitted)
  - @update:checked ((next: boolean | "indeterminate") => void) — Emitted with the next state when it changes (a click always emits a boolean)
  - @focus ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the checkbox gains focus
  - @blur ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the checkbox loses focus

### Overview
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox label="Same as billing address" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" />
  <Checkbox label="Subscribe to newsletter" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" default-checked />
</div>
```

### With Radio Group
Place Checkbox alongside RadioGroup controls to verify that indicator sizes, label baselines, and spacing align consistently in both horizontal and vertical layouts.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-6 w-full max-w-md">
  <Field label="Notification channel" description="Choose how you'd like to be notified." v-slot="{ id }">
    <RadioGroup :direction="direction" default-value="email" aria-label="Notification channel">
      <RadioGroupItem
        v-for="channel in [
          { value: 'email', label: 'Email', description: 'Messages delivered to your inbox.' },
          { value: 'sms', label: 'SMS', description: 'Text messages to your phone number.' },
          { value: 'push', label: 'Push', description: 'Real-time alerts on your device.' },
        ]"
        :key="channel.value"
        :value="channel.value"
      >
        <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
          <span class="font-medium">{{ channel.label }}</span>
          <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ channel.description }}</span>
        </div>
        <template v-else>{{ channel.label }}</template>
      </RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
  <Field label="Topics" description="Select the topics you want to follow." v-slot="{ id }">
    <div :class="direction === 'row' ? 'flex flex-row gap-5' : 'flex flex-col gap-2.5'">
      <Checkbox
        v-for="topic in [
          { label: 'Product updates', description: 'New features and improvements.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Security alerts', description: 'Account activity and login notifications.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Community', description: 'Events, meetups, and discussions.', checked: false },
        ]"
        :key="topic.label"
        :default-checked="topic.checked"
      >
        <template #label>
          <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">{{ topic.label }}</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ topic.description }}</span>
          </div>
          <template v-else>{{ topic.label }}</template>
        </template>
      </Checkbox>
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Descriptions
Combine Field-level label and description with item-level descriptions for options that need additional context. Use a plain label without descriptions when the options are self-explanatory.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col max-w-sm w-full divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-5">
    <Field label="Email notifications" description="Configure which emails you receive." v-slot="{ id }">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
        <Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.marketing">
          <template #label>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="font-medium">Marketing</span>
              <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Receive emails about new products and features.</span>
            </div>
          </template>
        </Checkbox>
        <Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.security">
          <template #label>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="font-medium">Security</span>
              <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Receive emails about account activity and security.</span>
            </div>
          </template>
        </Checkbox>
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="pt-5">
    <Field label="Push notifications" v-slot="{ id }">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <Checkbox label="Everything" v-model:checked="settings.everything" />
        <Checkbox label="Direct messages and mentions" v-model:checked="settings.mentions" />
        <Checkbox label="Nothing" v-model:checked="settings.nothing" />
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Variants
The checkbox supports three visual styles: solid (default) with a filled background and standard radius, solid pill with fully rounded corners, and ghost, which renders only the checkmark without a border or background fill. Toggle disabled without resetting checked or indeterminate states. On a phone with touch.css a solid checkbox is the 22px selection circle of iOS with or without pill, so the phone view leaves out the solid pill group. A ghost checkbox is the checkmark of an iOS list row.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-x-12 gap-y-6">
  <div
    v-for="group in [
      { name: 'solid' },
      { name: 'solid pill', pill: true },
      { name: 'ghost', variant: 'ghost' },
    ]"
    :key="group.name"
    class="flex flex-col gap-3 items-start"
  >
    <span class="text-xs text-secondary font-mono mb-1">{{ group.name }}</span>
    <Checkbox label="Unchecked" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" :disabled="disabled" />
    <Checkbox label="Checked" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" :disabled="disabled" default-checked />
    <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" :disabled="disabled" checked="indeterminate" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Pill
Set pill to render the checkbox indicator with fully rounded corners (border-radius: full) instead of the default square radius. On a phone with touch.css every checkbox indicator is round, the 22px selection circle of iOS, so pill makes no difference there. The phone view leaves out the pill control here and in the overview.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox label="Unchecked" :pill="pill" />
  <Checkbox label="Checked" :pill="pill" default-checked />
  <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" :pill="pill" checked="indeterminate" />
</div>
```

### Ghost
Use variant="ghost" to render the checkbox without a border or background. The checkmark uses the text color instead of white, making it suitable for subtle inline selections or task lists.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox label="Unchecked" variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" />
  <Checkbox label="Checked" variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" default-checked />
  <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" checked="indeterminate" />
</div>
```

### With description
Use description (or the description slot) for a second line under the label. It sits outside the label, so the checkbox keeps the label as its name and gets the description through aria-describedby. The description renders only next to a label.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 max-w-sm">
  <Checkbox
    label="Accept terms and conditions"
    description="By checking this box, you agree to our Terms of Service and Privacy Policy."
    default-checked
  />
  <Checkbox
    label="Enable notifications"
    description="You can enable or disable notifications at any time from settings."
  />
</div>
```

### Indeterminate
Use checked="indeterminate" to indicate a partially selected state. This is typically used for "select all" controls where only some child items are checked. Here checked starts as "indeterminate" and each click moves it to the next state.
```vue
<Checkbox
  :checked="checked"
  @update:checked="checked = checked === 'indeterminate' ? true : checked ? false : 'indeterminate'"
  label="Select all"
/>
```

### Label orientation
By default the checkbox renders to the left of the label. Set orientation="right" to place the checkbox after the label.
```vue
<Checkbox label="Accept terms and conditions" :orientation="orientation" />
```

### Without label
When no label is provided, the checkbox renders as a standalone control, so pass aria-label to give it an accessible name. All three visual states are shown: unchecked, checked, and indeterminate.
```vue
<div class="flex gap-4">
  <Checkbox aria-label="Accept terms" />
  <Checkbox aria-label="Subscribe" default-checked />
  <Checkbox aria-label="Select all" checked="indeterminate" />
</div>
```

### Group with select all
Combine a parent checkbox with child checkboxes to create a "select all" pattern. The parent shows an indeterminate state when only some children are checked. Each child binds v-model:checked to its item in items.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox
    label="Permissions"
    :checked="items.every((i) => i.checked) ? true : items.some((i) => i.checked) ? 'indeterminate' : false"
    @update:checked="(next) => items.forEach((i) => (i.checked = next))"
  />
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 pl-6">
    <Checkbox
      v-for="item in items"
      :key="item.id"
      :label="item.label"
      v-model:checked="item.checked"
    />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Group with description
Wrap a group of checkboxes in a Field for a shared label and description. This is a common pattern for preference lists and settings panels.
```vue
<div class="max-w-sm w-full">
  <Field label="Show on desktop" description="Select the items you want to display on the desktop." v-slot="{ id }">
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <Checkbox v-for="item in items" :key="item.id" :label="item.label" v-model:checked="item.checked" />
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### With Field
Wrap checkboxes in a Field component for form layout with label, description, and error message support. A single control passed as the child gets aria-describedby and aria-invalid automatically; with v-slot the slot props carry them, so bind them onto the control that should carry them.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 max-w-sm w-full">
  <Field
    label="Terms"
    description="Please review and accept the terms before continuing."
    :error-message="showError ? 'You must accept the terms to continue.' : undefined"
    v-slot="{ id }"
  >
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 mt-1">
      <Checkbox label="I agree to the Terms of Service" v-model:checked="agreed" />
      <Checkbox label="Subscribe to product updates" />
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Grouped sections
Use dividers to separate checkbox groups into logical sections. Each section can be wrapped in a Field with its own label and description.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped" class="h-dvh" data-form-screen style="--page-content-padding-top:10px; --page-content-padding-bottom:16px; --page-footer-gutter:24px; --page-footer-padding-bottom:max(24px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))">
  <Toolbar>
    <ToolbarLeading><DemoBackButton /></ToolbarLeading>
    <ToolbarTitle>Notifications</ToolbarTitle>
  </Toolbar>
  <PageContent data-form-content>
    <form ref="screenForm" class="flex flex-col gap-6" @submit.prevent="" >
      <FieldGroup title="Email notifications" description="Configure which emails you receive.">
        <Field row label="Marketing" description="Receive emails about new products and features."><Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.emailMarketing" /></Field>
        <Field row label="Security" description="Receive emails about account activity and security."><Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.emailSecurity" /></Field>
      </FieldGroup>
      <FieldGroup title="Push notifications" description="Configure which push notifications you receive.">
        <Field row label="Everything"><Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.pushEverything" /></Field>
        <Field row label="Direct messages and mentions"><Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.pushMentions" /></Field>
        <Field row label="Nothing"><Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.pushNothing" /></Field>
      </FieldGroup>
    </form>
  </PageContent>

</Page>
```

## Code Block
Code block with syntax highlighting, a copy button, and collapsible long snippets with configurable expanded height limits.
Import: import { CodeBlock } from "@plexui/vue/components/CodeBlock"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/code-block
Props:
  - default slot (string) — Code content (text only; interpolate a string to keep its whitespace)
  - code (string) — Code content, used when the default slot is empty
  - language (string) — Prism language name
  - class (string) — Additional wrapper class
  CollapsibleCodeBlock:
    - default slot / code (string) — Code content
    - language (string) — Prism language name
    - default-expanded (boolean) default: false — Start expanded
    - collapsed-height (number) default: 140 — Height when collapsed (px)
    - max-expanded-height (number) — Max height when expanded (px); taller code scrolls
    - copyable (boolean) default: true — Show copy button
    - class (string) — Additional wrapper class

### Overview
The code is the text of the default slot. Interpolate a string ({"{{ code }}"}) so its whitespace is kept, or pass it with the code prop.
```vue
<CodeBlock language="javascript">{{ codeSnippet }}</CodeBlock>
```

### Collapsible
Use CollapsibleCodeBlock to display code in a compact view with gradient overlay and an expand action.
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="tsx">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Expanded by default
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="tsx" default-expanded>{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Custom collapsed height
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="typescript" :collapsed-height="60">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Max expanded height
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="typescript" default-expanded :max-expanded-height="200">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Without copy button
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="python" default-expanded :copyable="false">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Supported languages
A pragmatic set of Prism grammars are pre-registered out of the box.
```vue
<CodeBlock language="javascript">{{ code }}</CodeBlock>
```

## Color Picker
"The iOS 26 system color picker: Grid, Spectrum and Sliders, an Opacity slider and saved colors, and the color well that opens it in a popover or, on a phone, a sheet."
Import: import { ColorPicker, ColorWell } from "@plexui/vue/components/ColorPicker"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/color-picker
Props:
  - value (string) — The controlled color, `#rrggbb` or `#rrggbbaa`; bind with v-model:value
  - default-value (string) default: "#000000" — The color when it is initially rendered
  - supports-alpha (boolean) default: true — Shows the Opacity slider
  - mode ("grid" | "spectrum" | "sliders") — The controlled way to choose a color; bind with v-model:mode
  - default-mode ("grid" | "spectrum" | "sliders") default: "grid" — The way to choose a color when it is initially rendered
  - swatches (string[]) — The controlled saved colors; bind with v-model:swatches
  - default-swatches (string[]) default: [] — The saved colors when it is initially rendered
  - aria-label (string) default: "Colors" — Accessible name of the picker
  ColorWell:
    - value (string | null) — The controlled color, `#rrggbb` or `#rrggbbaa`; bind with v-model:value. `null` shows the well with no color
    - default-value (string | null) default: null — The color when it is initially rendered
    - size ("sm" | "lg") default: "sm" — 28px or 36px
    - title (string) default: "Colors" — The title of the picker
    - supports-eyedropper (boolean) default: true — Shows the eyedropper where the browser has the EyeDropper API
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevents opening the picker
    - name (string) — Submits the color with the owning form
    - aria-label (string) default: "Color" — Accessible name of the well
    - aria-labelledby (string) — The `id` of the visible label of the well

### Color well
The well shows the chosen color inside a rainbow ring, or the full rainbow when there is no color yet (null). It opens the picker in a popover from the well, and on a phone in a sheet. The eyedropper appears where the browser can pick a color from the screen. Both wells here share their saved colors. On a phone the rows sit on the grouped background of an iOS settings screen.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
const tint = ref('#af52de')
const swatches = ref(['#000000', '#007aff', '#34c759'])
</script>

<template>
  <FieldGroup>
    <Field row label="Tint">
      <ColorWell aria-label="Tint" v-model:value="tint" v-model:swatches="swatches" />
    </Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</template>
```

### Sizes and states
The kit's two sizes: sm 28px and lg 36px.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
  <ColorWell aria-label="Small, no color" />
  <ColorWell aria-label="Small" default-value="#af52de" />
  <ColorWell aria-label="Large, no color" size="lg" />
  <ColorWell aria-label="Large" size="lg" default-value="#af52de" />
  <ColorWell aria-label="Disabled" default-value="#007aff" disabled />
</div>
```

### Picker
The picker on its own. Grid shows the kit's 120 colors; Spectrum runs the hues from top to bottom, from white through the full hue to black across; Sliders sets red, green and blue, or a hex color. Add saves the current color; Delete or Backspace, or Delete in a saved color's context menu, removes one. Saved colors fill pages of ten that scroll sideways.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
const color = ref('#be38f3')
</script>

<template>
  <ColorPicker v-model:value="color" :default-swatches="['#000000', '#007aff', '#34c759']" />
</template>
```

### Without opacity
With :supports-alpha="false" there is no Opacity slider, and every color is opaque.
```vue
<ColorPicker default-value="#007aff" default-mode="sliders" :supports-alpha="false" />
```

## Context Menu
"A menu of actions for an element: a secondary click opens it at the pointer, a touch and hold opens it beside the element over a dimmed page, as iOS 26 context menus do. Its items are the ones of Menu."
Import: import { ContextMenu, ContextMenuContent, ContextMenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/ContextMenu"
Import: import { MenuItem, MenuSeparator } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/context-menu
  ContextMenuTrigger:
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — The element keeps the browser's own context menu
  ContextMenuContent:
    - width (number | string) — Defines the width of the menu
    - min-width (number | string) — Defines the min-width of the menu, in pixels
    - max-height (number) — Defines the max-height of the menu, in pixels
    - pill (boolean) default: false — Rounder list with fully rounded rows

### Overview
Wrap the element in ContextMenuTrigger: it keeps its own role and focus. On a touch screen, hold for half a second: the element stays clear above the dimmed, blurred page and the menu opens 18px below it, at the edge on the side of the touch. The press that opened the menu does not activate the element. On a phone the rows sit on the grouped background of an iOS list.
```vue
<ContextMenu>
  <ContextMenuTrigger>
    <div tabindex="0">Groceries</div>
  </ContextMenuTrigger>
  <ContextMenuContent>
    <MenuItem @select="copy"><Copy /> Copy</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="share"><Share /> Share</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="pin"><Pin /> Pin</MenuItem>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuItem danger @select="remove"><RemoveTrash /> Delete</MenuItem>
  </ContextMenuContent>
</ContextMenu>
```

### Rounded elements
The element is cut out of the dimming with its own corners. An element without corners of its own gets the 30px corner of the kit's preview, at most half its height.
```vue
<ContextMenu>
  <ContextMenuTrigger>
    <div tabindex="0" role="img" aria-label="Photos" class="rounded-2xl">
      <Images />
    </div>
  </ContextMenuTrigger>
  <ContextMenuContent>...</ContextMenuContent>
</ContextMenu>
```

## Copy Tooltip
A copy-to-clipboard tooltip that wraps any trigger element and confirms when the value has been copied.
Import: import { CopyTooltip } from "@plexui/vue/components/Tooltip"
Import: import { Button } from "@plexui/vue/components/Button"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/copy-tooltip
Props:
  - default slot (VNode) — Trigger element (for example TooltipTriggerDecorator)
  - copy-value (string) — Required. Content to copy to clipboard
  - open-delay (number) default: 150 — Tooltip open delay in milliseconds
  - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "center" — Preferred tooltip alignment against the trigger
  - align-offset (number) default: 0 — Offset from start/end alignment in pixels
  - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "top" — Preferred side of the trigger
  - side-offset (number) default: 5 — Distance from trigger in pixels
  - @copy ((copied: boolean) => void) — Emitted once the clipboard write settles, with whether it succeeded. The tooltip says Copied! only after a successful write

### Overview
```vue
<CopyTooltip
  copy-value="sess_12345abcdefg"
  :open-delay="openDelay"
  :side-offset="sideOffset"
  :align-offset="alignOffset"
  :side="side"
  :align="align"
>
  <Button color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="xl" aria-label="Copy session ID">
    sess_12345abcdefg
  </Button>
</CopyTooltip>
```

## Date Input
Masked text input for typing dates and times, with automatic formatting, min and max limits, and validation.
Import: import { DateInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/DateInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/date-input
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (DateTime | null) — Selected date (Luxon DateTime)
  - @update:value ((date: DateTime | null) => void) — Emitted when a complete valid date is entered or the field is cleared
  - @change ((date: DateTime | null) => void) — Same payload as update:value, named after React's onChange
  - mode (MaskitoDateMode) default: "dd/mm/yyyy" — Date format mode: dd/mm/yyyy, mm/dd/yyyy, yyyy/mm/dd, etc.
  - separator (string) default: "." — Character between date parts
  - time-mode (MaskitoTimeMode) — Enable time input: HH:MM, HH:MM:SS, etc.
  - date-time-separator (string) default: ", " — Separator between date and time parts
  - time-step (number) default: 0 — Arrow-key increment step for time segments
  - min (DateTime) — Earliest allowed date
  - max (DateTime) — Latest allowed date
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button when input has a value
  - size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Input size (3xs to 3xl)
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded corners
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable input
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark as invalid
  - placeholder (string) — Generated from the format if not set

### Overview
Bind a Luxon DateTime (or null) with v-model:value. It updates once a complete, valid date is typed, and becomes null when the field is emptied or cleared; @change carries the same value. Leaving the field with a partly typed date shows the value again.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import { DateInput } from '@plexui/vue/components/DateInput';

const date = ref<DateTime | null>(null);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" size="md" variant="outline" clearable :disabled="disabled" />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the date is the value of a row. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable :disabled="disabled" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
</template>
```

### With time
Pass time-mode="HH:MM" to enable date + time input in one field.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[260px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" time-mode="HH:MM" clearable />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Starts">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" time-mode="HH:MM" clearable />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### With seconds
Use time-mode="HH:MM:SS" for second-level precision.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[300px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" time-mode="HH:MM:SS" clearable />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Logged">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" time-mode="HH:MM:SS" clearable />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Limits
Use min and max to restrict the date range. The mask prevents entering values outside the allowed range. today is DateTime.local().
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput
    v-model:value="date"
    :min="today.minus({ days: 30 }).startOf('day')"
    :max="today.plus({ days: 30 }).endOf('day')"
    clearable
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DateInput
      v-model:value="date"
      :min="today.minus({ days: 30 }).startOf('day')"
      :max="today.plus({ days: 30 }).endOf('day')"
      clearable
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### US format
Set mode="mm/dd/yyyy" and separator="/" for US-style date formatting.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" clearable />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" clearable />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### ISO format
Set mode="yyyy/mm/dd" and separator="-" for ISO 8601 formatting.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" mode="yyyy/mm/dd" separator="-" clearable />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" mode="yyyy/mm/dd" separator="-" clearable />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### With Field
Wrap with Field to add a label, description, and automatic accessibility attributes.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <Field label="Date of birth" description="Enter your date of birth">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable />
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date of birth" description="Enter your date of birth">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Validation
Pass error-message to Field to display a validation error.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <Field label="Date of birth" :error-message="!date ? 'Date is required' : undefined">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable />
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date of birth" :error-message="!date ? 'Date is required' : undefined">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

## Date Picker
Date picker with an interactive calendar, time picker, year and month dropdowns, min/max limits, and date-of-birth presets.
Import: import { DatePicker } from "@plexui/vue/components/DatePicker"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/date-picker
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (DateTime | null) default: null — Selected date (luxon)
  - @change ((date: DateTime | null) => void) — Emitted when the date changes, with the same value as update:value
  - id (string) — Id of the trigger, for forms and labels
  - min (DateTime) — Earliest selectable date (inclusive)
  - max (DateTime) — Latest selectable date (inclusive). Must not be before min
  - caption-layout ("buttons" | "dropdown") default: "buttons" — Calendar header mode. Use dropdown for date of birth
  - show-time (boolean) default: false — Show time input below calendar
  - year-range ([number, number]) default: last 120 years — Min/max years for dropdown caption
  - size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Trigger size (3xs to 3xl)
  - variant ("outline" | "soft" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Trigger style
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Rounded trigger
  - block (boolean) default: false — Full-width trigger
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button
  - trigger-show-icon (boolean) default: true — Show calendar icon
  - trigger-date-format (string) default: "MM/dd/yy" — Date format in trigger. Auto-includes time when show-time is enabled and trigger-time-format is not set
  - trigger-time-format (string) — With show-time, the time format after the date in the trigger. The date and the time become two parts; in a phone row, two capsules
  - trigger-class-name (string) — Class applied to the trigger
  - dropdown-icon-type ("dropdown" | "chevronDown" | "none") default: "dropdown" — Icon at the end of the trigger
  - placeholder (string) default: "Select date..." — Trigger placeholder
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable picker
  - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "bottom" — Preferred side of the calendar popover. On phones, left and right prefer bottom so all seven day targets fit. Top and bottom keep their preference; collisions can still flip placement.
  - side-offset (number) default: 8 — Distance from the trigger in pixels
  - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "center" — Alignment against the trigger
  - align-offset (number) default: 0 (center), -5 (start, end) — Offset from start/end alignment in pixels

### Overview
Bind the date (a luxon DateTime, or null) with v-model:value. The @change event carries the same value, like React's onChange.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import { DatePicker } from '@plexui/vue/components/DatePicker';

const selectedDate = ref<DateTime | null>(null);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
<!-- Desktop -->
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-base"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  :disabled="disabled"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>

<!-- Phone: the date is the value of a row, in the compact date that opens the calendar. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-base"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      placeholder="Select date"
      :disabled="disabled"
      v-model:value="selectedDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
</template>
```

### Limits
Use min and max to limit selectable dates to a specific timeframe. Here minDate is {"today.minus({ days: 30 }).startOf('day')"} and maxDate is {"today.plus({ days: 30 }).endOf('day')"}. Equal bounds leave one selectable day.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-limits"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  pill
  trigger-show-icon
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
  :min="minDate"
  :max="maxDate"
/>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-limits"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      v-model:value="selectedDate"
      :min="minDate"
      :max="maxDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Date of birth
Set caption-layout="dropdown" to add month and year dropdowns inline with the arrow navigation. Ideal for selecting a date of birth where navigating month-by-month would be impractical.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-dob"
  trigger-date-format="MMMM d, yyyy"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  placeholder="Select your birthday"
  caption-layout="dropdown"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
  :max="DateTime.local()"
/>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Birthday">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-dob"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      placeholder="Select date"
      caption-layout="dropdown"
      v-model:value="selectedDate"
      :max="DateTime.local()"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Time picker
Enable show-time to add hour and minute inputs below the calendar. The popover stays open after selecting a date so the user can adjust the time. Use arrow keys to increment or decrement values. With trigger-time-format the trigger shows the date and the time as two parts: on Phone, in a row, they are two capsules, as iOS shows a date and a time.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-time"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy HH:mm"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  placeholder="Select date & time"
  show-time
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Starts">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-time"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      trigger-time-format="HH:mm"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      placeholder="Select date"
      show-time
      v-model:value="selectedDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Dropdown + time
Combine caption-layout="dropdown" with show-time for quick month/year jumps and time selection in a single picker, useful for scheduling events at a specific date and time.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-datetime-dropdown"
  trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy HH:mm"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  placeholder="Schedule event"
  caption-layout="dropdown"
  show-time
  :year-range="[2020, 2030]"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Event">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-datetime-dropdown"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      trigger-time-format="HH:mm"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      placeholder="Select date"
      caption-layout="dropdown"
      show-time
      :year-range="[2020, 2030]"
      v-model:value="selectedDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Two fields
Compose DatePicker and Input side by side for maximum flexibility in form layouts. Each field operates independently while sharing the same DateTime value. Here formatTime masks the typed time as HH:MM, applyTime moves the date to a complete typed time, and withTime gives a newly picked date the typed time.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center">
  <DatePicker
    id="date-picker-two-field-date"
    trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
    size="lg"
    pill
    caption-layout="dropdown"
    :trigger-show-icon="false"
    dropdown-icon-type="none"
    placeholder="Select date"
    :value="selectedDate"
    @update:value="selectedDate = withTime($event, time)"
  />
  <Input
    id="date-picker-two-field-time"
    size="lg"
    pill
    :value="time"
    @update:value="time = formatTime($event); selectedDate = applyTime(selectedDate, time)"
    placeholder="HH:MM"
    maxlength="5"
    style="width: 72px"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone: two rows, the compact date and the time typed at the row's end -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-two-field-date"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      caption-layout="dropdown"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      placeholder="Select date"
      :value="selectedDate"
      @update:value="selectedDate = withTime($event, time)"
    />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Time">
    <Input
      id="date-picker-two-field-time"
      :value="time"
      @update:value="time = formatTime($event); selectedDate = applyTime(selectedDate, time)"
      placeholder="HH:MM"
      maxlength="5"
      inputmode="numeric"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Date input
For typing a date directly instead of picking it, use DateInput, a masked input that auto-formats as you type.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="selectedDate" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" aria-label="Date" />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DateInput v-model:value="selectedDate" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### DateTime input
Pass time-mode="HH:MM" to DateInput to type the date and time in one field.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[260px]">
  <DateInput
    v-model:value="selectedDate"
    mode="mm/dd/yyyy"
    separator="/"
    time-mode="HH:MM"
    aria-label="Date and time"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Starts">
    <DateInput v-model:value="selectedDate" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" time-mode="HH:MM" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Without Icon
Remove the calendar icon from the trigger by setting trigger-show-icon to false. In a phone row the date never shows the icon, as the compact date of iOS does not, so this example is desktop only.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-no-icon"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  :trigger-show-icon="false"
  placeholder="Choose a date"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Date">
    <DatePicker
      id="date-picker-no-icon"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      placeholder="Choose a date"
      v-model:value="selectedDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

## Date Range Picker
Date range picker with an interactive calendar, min/max limits, preset shortcuts, and a month stepper for selecting start and end dates.
Import: import { DateRangePicker } from "@plexui/vue/components/DateRangePicker"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/date-range-picker
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (DateRange | null) — Selected range ([start, end], inclusive)
  - @change ((next: DateRange | null, shortcut?: DateRangeShortcut) => void) — Emitted when the range changes
  - min (DateTime) — Earliest selectable date (inclusive)
  - max (DateTime) — Latest selectable date (inclusive). Must not be before min
  - max-range-days (number) — Max length of range in days (inclusive)
  - week-starts-on (0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6) default: 0 — The day each week starts on, from 0 (Sunday) to 6 (Saturday)
  - shortcuts (DateRangeShortcut[]) — Quick selection shortcuts ({ label, getDateRange })
  - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "bottom" — Preferred side of the calendar popover. On phones, left and right prefer bottom so all seven day targets fit. Top and bottom keep their preference; collisions can still flip placement.
  - side-offset (number) default: 8 — Distance from trigger in pixels
  - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "center" — Preferred popover alignment
  - align-offset (number) default: 0 (center), -5 (start, end) — Offset from start/end alignment in pixels
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable picker interactions
  - placeholder (string) default: "Select date range..." — Placeholder in trigger when value is empty
  - variant ("soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Trigger style variant
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Rounded trigger shape
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Trigger size scale
  - dropdown-icon-type ("chevronDown" | "dropdown" | "none") default: "dropdown" — Icon shown at trigger end
  - trigger-class-name (string) — Custom class for trigger
  - trigger-show-icon (boolean) default: true — Show calendar icon at trigger start
  - trigger-stepper-unit ("month") — Enable month stepper mode in trigger
  - trigger-date-format (string) default: "MM/dd/yy" — Date formatting in trigger text
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button in trigger
  - block (boolean) default: false — Stretch trigger to full available width

### Overview
Bind the range ([start, end] luxon DateTimes, or null) with v-model:value. The @change event carries the same range.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    size="lg"
    pill
    clearable
    :disabled="disabled"
    v-model:value="value"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Dates">
    <DateRangePicker
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d"
      placeholder="None"
      :disabled="disabled"
      v-model:value="value"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Limits
Use min and max to limit the selectable range. Use max-range-days to limit the range length. Here minDate is 30 days ago (start of day) and maxDate is the end of today.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    size="lg"
    pill
    trigger-show-icon
    v-model:value="value"
    :min="minDate"
    :max="maxDate"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Dates">
    <DateRangePicker
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d"
      placeholder="None"
      v-model:value="value"
      :min="minDate"
      :max="maxDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Shortcuts
Use the shortcuts array to provide quick date range options. When a shortcut is selected, its label is shown in the trigger and @change carries that shortcut as its second argument, like React's onChange(range, shortcut). Each shortcut has a label and a getDateRange() function; dayShortcuts here holds Today, Yesterday, Last 7 days and Last 30 days. A shortcut whose range falls outside min and max, or is longer than max-range-days, is disabled.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    size="lg"
    pill
    clearable
    v-model:value="value"
    :shortcuts="dayShortcuts"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Period">
    <DateRangePicker
      :trigger-show-icon="false"
      trigger-date-format="MMM d"
      placeholder="None"
      v-model:value="value"
      :shortcuts="dayShortcuts"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Month stepper
Enable a stepper in the trigger with trigger-stepper-unit="month". When the selected range matches the unit, the trigger shows the stepper.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    :size="size"
    clearable
    :pill="pill"
    trigger-stepper-unit="month"
    v-model:value="value"
    :max="maxDate"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Month">
    <DateRangePicker
      trigger-stepper-unit="month"
      v-model:value="value"
      :max="maxDate"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

## Dialog
Modal dialog for confirmations, forms, and destructive alerts, with a focus trap, escape handling, and an overlay scrim.
Import: import { Dialog, DialogBody, DialogClose, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogHeaderAction, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Dialog"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/dialog
Props:
  - open / v-model:open (boolean) — Controlled open state; uncontrolled when omitted
  - @update:open ((open: boolean) => void) — Fires when the dialog opens or closes
  DialogContent:
    - surface ("plain" | "grouped") default: "plain" — Mobile panel and field-group palette. Dark dialogs use elevated surface levels; desktop colors stay unchanged
    - width (number | string) default: 480 — Panel width. Mobile alert uses its compact width; a bottom sheet fills the viewport width
    - show-close (boolean) default: true — Show the built-in close button. Mobile sheet places it at the leading edge; mobile alert hides it
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class on the panel
    - placement ("center" | "top" | "bottom" | "start" | "end" | "fullscreen") default: "center" — Where the panel sits: centered, near the top, a bottom sheet, a side panel, or the whole screen
    - mobile-variant ("alert" | "sheet") — Mobile appearance at 767px and below. Alert is compact and centered; sheet defaults to the bottom. Omit for the standard appearance
    - mobile-placement ("center" | "bottom" | "fullscreen") — Placement at 767px and below. Defaults to bottom for sheet, otherwise placement. Alert stays centered
    - @open-auto-focus ((event: Event) => void) — Called when focus moves into the dialog on open; call event.preventDefault() to place it yourself, for example on the panel instead of the first field
    - @close-auto-focus ((event: Event) => void) — Called when focus returns on close; call event.preventDefault() to keep it elsewhere
  DialogFooter:
    - background ("soft" | "hard" | "glass" | "solid") default: "soft" — How the phone Sheet's bar meets the body scrolled under it, as on a Toolbar. Also available on the header; desktop and Alert surfaces are unchanged
    - mobile-layout ("inline" | "stacked") — Action layout at 767px and below. Defaults to inline for alert, stacked otherwise. Inline actions wrap when they do not fit

### Overview
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Open dialog</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="alert" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })">
    <DialogHeader>
      <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Unsaved changes</DialogTitle></div>
      <DialogDescription>You have unsaved changes that will be lost if you close this page. Do you want to save before leaving?</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="primary" variant="soft">Cancel</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Save</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Destructive confirmation
Use color="danger" on the action button for destructive operations.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="danger" variant="outline">Delete</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="alert" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })">
    <DialogHeader>
      <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Delete assistant?</DialogTitle></div>
      <DialogDescription>
        This change will affect all API integrations using
        <span class="font-medium text-[var(--color-text)]">asst_NAM5K9a7loKZay9W8gtQVQ3H</span>.
        It cannot be undone.
      </DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Cancel</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="danger" @click="open = false">Delete</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Text field
An alert can ask for one short value. The field fills the alert like its actions, a 48px capsule on a phone, and its text is selected when the alert opens. Create is unavailable while the field is empty.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open" @update:open="(next) => { if (next) name = 'New folder' }">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">New folder</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="alert" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.field.$el.querySelector('input').select()">
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogTitle>New folder</DialogTitle>
      <DialogDescription>Enter a name for this folder.</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <Input ref="field" v-model="name" aria-label="Folder name" variant="soft" size="3xl" pill @clear="name = ''" />
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="primary" variant="soft">Cancel</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" :disabled="!name.trim()" @click="open = false">Create</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Metadata details
Use a definition list and CopyTooltip with a ghost Button. The full value is copied even when the visible ID is truncated. Focus starts at the heading; Tab reaches the controls.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { Badge } from '@plexui/vue/components/Badge';
import { Button } from '@plexui/vue/components/Button';
import { Dialog, DialogTrigger, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogHeaderAction, DialogTitle, DialogDescription, DialogBody, DialogFooter, DialogClose } from '@plexui/vue/components/Dialog';
import { DotsHorizontal } from '@plexui/vue/components/Icon';
import { Menu, MenuTrigger, MenuContent, MenuItem } from '@plexui/vue/components/Menu';
import { Separator } from '@plexui/vue/components/Separator';
import { CopyTooltip } from '@plexui/vue/components/Tooltip';
import { useBreakpoint } from '@plexui/vue/composables/useBreakpoints';

const wide = useBreakpoint('md');

const initialOpen = false;
const open = ref(initialOpen);
const showActions = ref(true);
const paused = ref(false);
const localhost = ref(true);
const sections = false;
const long = false;
const groups = [
  {
    "title": "Application",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Name",
        "values": [
          "Plex application"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Client ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "client_76fb07f7-78e2-4656-abdf-e77b5d7b1ed1"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Status",
        "values": [
          "Active"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Created",
        "values": [
          "Nov 3, 2026"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Last used",
        "values": [
          "Nov 4, 2026"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "Web",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Origins",
        "copy": true,
        "values": []
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "iOS",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Team ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "AB12CD34EF"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Bundle ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "com.plexui.application"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "Android",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Package name",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "com.plexui.application"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "SHA-256",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "144BA802A02D59A260D0021EC450552A4CA2E3496F4390A6D5A8556BF617420B"
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
];
const extraOrigins = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, index) => `https://preview-${index + 1}.plexui.com`);
</script>

<template>
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
  <Dialog v-model:open="open">
    <DialogTrigger v-if="!initialOpen || !open"><Button color="secondary" variant="outline">{{ long ? 'View long details' : sections ? 'View application' : 'View details' }}</Button></DialogTrigger>
    <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" :width="640" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })">
      <DialogHeader>
        <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Application details</DialogTitle></div>
        <DialogDescription class="sr-only">Application metadata and registered platforms.</DialogDescription>
        <DialogHeaderAction v-if="sections && showActions">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger><Button color="secondary" variant="soft" size="2xl" icon-size="2xl" uniform aria-label="Application actions"><DotsHorizontal /></Button></MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuItem @select="paused = !paused">{{ paused ? 'Resume application' : 'Pause application' }}</MenuItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </DialogHeaderAction>
      </DialogHeader>
      <DialogBody>
        <div class="details-content flex flex-col gap-5 py-2">
          <template v-for="(group, index) in (sections ? groups : groups.slice(0, 1))" :key="group.title">
            <Separator v-if="index > 0" />
            <section data-detail-section class="flex flex-col gap-4" :aria-label="group.title">
              <div v-if="sections" class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <h3 data-detail-heading class="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-tertiary">{{ group.title }}</h3>
                <div v-if="showActions && group.title === 'Web'" class="-my-2 flex shrink-0">
                  <Menu>
                    <MenuTrigger><Button color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="md" uniform :aria-label="group.title + ' actions'"><DotsHorizontal /></Button></MenuTrigger>
                    <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
                      <MenuItem @select="localhost = !localhost">{{ localhost ? 'Remove localhost' : 'Allow localhost' }}</MenuItem>
                    </MenuContent>
                  </Menu>
                </div>
              </div>
              <dl data-detail-list class="space-y-4 text-sm">
                <div data-detail-row v-for="row in group.rows" :key="row.label" class="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-x-4 gap-y-1 sm:grid-cols-4">
                  <dt class="text-secondary">{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd class="min-w-0 sm:col-span-3">
                    <Badge v-if="row.label === 'Status'" :color="paused ? 'secondary' : 'success'" size="sm">{{ paused ? 'Paused' : 'Active' }}</Badge>
                    <div data-detail-values v-else-if="row.copy" class="space-y-4">
                      <div v-for="value in (row.label === 'Origins' ? ['https://app.plexui.com', ...(localhost ? ['http://localhost:5173'] : []), ...(long ? extraOrigins : [])] : row.values)" :key="value">
                        <CopyTooltip :copy-value="value" :side-offset="wide ? 5 : 24" :align="wide ? 'center' : 'start'">
                          <Button data-detail-copy color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="sm" optically-align="start" class="-my-1 max-w-full" :aria-label="'Copy ' + row.label + ' ' + value">
                            <span data-detail-value class="flex min-w-0 font-mono text-xs"><span class="truncate">{{ value.slice(0, -6) }}</span><span class="shrink-0">{{ value.slice(-6) }}</span></span>
                          </Button>
                        </CopyTooltip>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <template v-else>{{ row.values[0] }}</template>
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
            </section>
          </template>
        </div>
      </DialogBody>
    </DialogContent>
  </Dialog>
</div>
</template>

<style>
/* Read-only metadata uses native Body and Footnote roles on phones. */
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .details-content [data-detail-section] {
    gap: 12px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-heading] {
    color: var(--color-text);
    font-size: 17px;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 22px;
    letter-spacing: normal;
    text-transform: none;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-list] {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0;
    font-size: 17px;
    line-height: 22px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-row] {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    row-gap: 4px;
    margin: 0;
    padding-block-end: 20px;
  }

  /* A copy target includes its own space below the text; targets never overlap. */
  .details-content [data-detail-row]:has([data-detail-copy]),
  .details-content [data-detail-row]:last-child {
    padding-block-end: 0;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-row] > dt {
    font-size: 13px;
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 18px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-row] > dd {
    grid-column: auto;
    color: var(--color-text);
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-values] > * {
    margin: 0;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-copy] {
    --button-radius: 8px;
    --button-gutter: 0px;
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    height: 44px;
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 100%;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    color: var(--color-text);
    vertical-align: top;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-copy] > span {
    align-items: flex-start;
    height: 22px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-copy]::before,
  .details-content [data-detail-copy]::after {
    inset-block-end: 22px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-value] {
    font-size: 17px;
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 22px;
  }
}
</style>
```

### Sections and actions
Separator has the same gap on either side. DialogHeaderAction keeps application actions on the same row as Close. Web actions stay beside the section heading. The menus update only this example: pause the application or remove and restore localhost. Turn off showActions to compare the read-only layout.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { Badge } from '@plexui/vue/components/Badge';
import { Button } from '@plexui/vue/components/Button';
import { Dialog, DialogTrigger, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogHeaderAction, DialogTitle, DialogDescription, DialogBody, DialogFooter, DialogClose } from '@plexui/vue/components/Dialog';
import { DotsHorizontal } from '@plexui/vue/components/Icon';
import { Menu, MenuTrigger, MenuContent, MenuItem } from '@plexui/vue/components/Menu';
import { Separator } from '@plexui/vue/components/Separator';
import { CopyTooltip } from '@plexui/vue/components/Tooltip';
import { useBreakpoint } from '@plexui/vue/composables/useBreakpoints';

const wide = useBreakpoint('md');

const initialOpen = false;
const open = ref(initialOpen);
const showActions = ref(true);
const paused = ref(false);
const localhost = ref(true);
const sections = true;
const long = false;
const groups = [
  {
    "title": "Application",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Name",
        "values": [
          "Plex application"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Client ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "client_76fb07f7-78e2-4656-abdf-e77b5d7b1ed1"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Status",
        "values": [
          "Active"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Created",
        "values": [
          "Nov 3, 2026"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Last used",
        "values": [
          "Nov 4, 2026"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "Web",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Origins",
        "copy": true,
        "values": []
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "iOS",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Team ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "AB12CD34EF"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Bundle ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "com.plexui.application"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "Android",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Package name",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "com.plexui.application"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "SHA-256",
        "copy": true,

  }
];

</script>

<template>
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">

    <DialogTrigger v-if="!initialOpen || !open"><Button color="secondary" variant="outline">{{ long ? 'View long details' : sections ? 'View application' : 'View details' }}</Button></DialogTrigger>
    <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" :width="640" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })">
      <DialogHeader>
        <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Application details</DialogTitle></div>
        <DialogDescription class="sr-only">Application metadata and registered platforms.</DialogDescription>
        <DialogHeaderAction v-if="sections && showActions">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger><Button color="secondary" variant="soft" size="2xl" icon-size="2xl" uniform aria-label="Application actions"><DotsHorizontal /></Button></MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuItem @select="paused = !paused">{{ paused ? 'Resume application' : 'Pause application' }}</MenuItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </DialogHeaderAction>
      </DialogHeader>
      <DialogBody>
        <div class="details-content flex flex-col gap-5 py-2">
          <template v-for="(group, index) in (sections ? groups : groups.slice(0, 1))" :key="group.title">
            <Separator v-if="index > 0" />
            <section data-detail-section class="flex flex-col gap-4" :aria-label="group.title">
              <div v-if="sections" class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <h3 data-detail-heading class="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-tertiary">{{ group.title }}</h3>
                <div v-if="showActions && group.title === 'Web'" class="-my-2 flex shrink-0">
                  <Menu>
                    <MenuTrigger><Button color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="md" uniform :aria-label="group.title + ' actions'"><DotsHorizontal /></Button></MenuTrigger>
                    <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
                      <MenuItem @select="localhost = !localhost">{{ localhost ? 'Remove localhost' : 'Allow localhost' }}</MenuItem>
                    </MenuContent>
                  </Menu>
                </div>
              </div>
              <dl data-detail-list class="space-y-4 text-sm">
                <div data-detail-row v-for="row in group.rows" :key="row.label" class="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-x-4 gap-y-1 sm:grid-cols-4">
                  <dt class="text-secondary">{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd class="min-w-0 sm:col-span-3">
                    <Badge v-if="row.label === 'Status'" :color="paused ? 'secondary' : 'success'" size="sm">{{ paused ? 'Paused' : 'Active' }}</Badge>
                    <div data-detail-values v-else-if="row.copy" class="space-y-4">
                      <div v-for="value in (row.label === 'Origins' ? ['https://app.plexui.com', ...(localhost ? ['http://localhost:5173'] : []), ...(long ? extraOrigins : [])] : row.values)" :key="value">

                          <Button data-detail-copy color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="sm" optically-align="start" class="-my-1 max-w-full" :aria-label="'Copy ' + row.label + ' ' + value">

                          </Button>
                        </CopyTooltip>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <template v-else>{{ row.values[0] }}</template>
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
            </section>
          </template>
        </div>
      </DialogBody>
    </DialogContent>
  </Dialog>
</div>
</template>

    font-size: 17px;
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 22px;
  }
}
</style>
```

### Long details
DialogBody scrolls while the header and Close button remain visible. Labels stack above values at narrow widths. Phone preview opens the dialog immediately so you can inspect the layout without resizing the page.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { Badge } from '@plexui/vue/components/Badge';
import { Button } from '@plexui/vue/components/Button';
import { Dialog, DialogTrigger, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogHeaderAction, DialogTitle, DialogDescription, DialogBody, DialogFooter, DialogClose } from '@plexui/vue/components/Dialog';
import { DotsHorizontal } from '@plexui/vue/components/Icon';
import { Menu, MenuTrigger, MenuContent, MenuItem } from '@plexui/vue/components/Menu';
import { Separator } from '@plexui/vue/components/Separator';
import { CopyTooltip } from '@plexui/vue/components/Tooltip';
import { useBreakpoint } from '@plexui/vue/composables/useBreakpoints';

const wide = useBreakpoint('md');

const initialOpen = false;
const open = ref(initialOpen);
const showActions = ref(true);
const paused = ref(false);
const localhost = ref(true);
const sections = true;
const long = true;
const groups = [
  {
    "title": "Application",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Name",
        "values": [
          "Plex application"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Client ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "client_76fb07f7-78e2-4656-abdf-e77b5d7b1ed1"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Status",
        "values": [
          "Active"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Created",
        "values": [
          "Nov 3, 2026"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Last used",
        "values": [
          "Nov 4, 2026"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "Web",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Origins",
        "copy": true,
        "values": []
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "iOS",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Team ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "AB12CD34EF"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "Bundle ID",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "com.plexui.application"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "title": "Android",
    "rows": [
      {
        "label": "Package name",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "com.plexui.application"
        ]
      },
      {
        "label": "SHA-256",
        "copy": true,
        "values": [
          "144BA802A02D59A260D0021EC450552A4CA2E3496F4390A6D5A8556BF617420B"
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
];
const extraOrigins = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, index) => `https://preview-${index + 1}.plexui.com`);
</script>

<template>
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
  <Dialog v-model:open="open">
    <DialogTrigger v-if="!initialOpen || !open"><Button color="secondary" variant="outline">{{ long ? 'View long details' : sections ? 'View application' : 'View details' }}</Button></DialogTrigger>
    <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" :width="640" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })">
      <DialogHeader>
        <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Application details</DialogTitle></div>
        <DialogDescription class="sr-only">Application metadata and registered platforms.</DialogDescription>
        <DialogHeaderAction v-if="sections && showActions">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger><Button color="secondary" variant="soft" size="2xl" icon-size="2xl" uniform aria-label="Application actions"><DotsHorizontal /></Button></MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuItem @select="paused = !paused">{{ paused ? 'Resume application' : 'Pause application' }}</MenuItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </DialogHeaderAction>
      </DialogHeader>
      <DialogBody>
        <div class="details-content flex flex-col gap-5 py-2">
          <template v-for="(group, index) in (sections ? groups : groups.slice(0, 1))" :key="group.title">
            <Separator v-if="index > 0" />
            <section data-detail-section class="flex flex-col gap-4" :aria-label="group.title">
              <div v-if="sections" class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <h3 data-detail-heading class="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-tertiary">{{ group.title }}</h3>
                <div v-if="showActions && group.title === 'Web'" class="-my-2 flex shrink-0">
                  <Menu>
                    <MenuTrigger><Button color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="md" uniform :aria-label="group.title + ' actions'"><DotsHorizontal /></Button></MenuTrigger>
                    <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
                      <MenuItem @select="localhost = !localhost">{{ localhost ? 'Remove localhost' : 'Allow localhost' }}</MenuItem>
                    </MenuContent>
                  </Menu>
                </div>
              </div>
              <dl data-detail-list class="space-y-4 text-sm">
                <div data-detail-row v-for="row in group.rows" :key="row.label" class="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-x-4 gap-y-1 sm:grid-cols-4">
                  <dt class="text-secondary">{{ row.label }}</dt>
                  <dd class="min-w-0 sm:col-span-3">
                    <Badge v-if="row.label === 'Status'" :color="paused ? 'secondary' : 'success'" size="sm">{{ paused ? 'Paused' : 'Active' }}</Badge>
                    <div data-detail-values v-else-if="row.copy" class="space-y-4">
                      <div v-for="value in (row.label === 'Origins' ? ['https://app.plexui.com', ...(localhost ? ['http://localhost:5173'] : []), ...(long ? extraOrigins : [])] : row.values)" :key="value">
                        <CopyTooltip :copy-value="value" :side-offset="wide ? 5 : 24" :align="wide ? 'center' : 'start'">
                          <Button data-detail-copy color="secondary" variant="ghost" size="sm" optically-align="start" class="-my-1 max-w-full" :aria-label="'Copy ' + row.label + ' ' + value">
                            <span data-detail-value class="flex min-w-0 font-mono text-xs"><span class="truncate">{{ value.slice(0, -6) }}</span><span class="shrink-0">{{ value.slice(-6) }}</span></span>
                          </Button>
                        </CopyTooltip>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <template v-else>{{ row.values[0] }}</template>
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
            </section>
          </template>
        </div>
      </DialogBody>
    </DialogContent>
  </Dialog>
</div>
</template>

<style>
/* Read-only metadata uses native Body and Footnote roles on phones. */
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .details-content [data-detail-section] {
    gap: 12px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-heading] {

    padding-block-end: 20px;
  }

  /* A copy target includes its own space below the text; targets never overlap. */
  .details-content [data-detail-row]:has([data-detail-copy]),
  .details-content [data-detail-row]:last-child {
    padding-block-end: 0;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-row] > dt {

    grid-column: auto;

  }

    margin: 0;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-copy] {
    --button-radius: 8px;
    --button-gutter: 0px;
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: flex-start;
    height: 44px;
    min-width: 0;
    max-width: 100%;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    color: var(--color-text);
    vertical-align: top;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-copy] > span {
    align-items: flex-start;
    height: 22px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-copy]::before,
  .details-content [data-detail-copy]::after {
    inset-block-end: 22px;
  }

  .details-content [data-detail-value] {
    font-size: 17px;
    font-weight: 400;
    line-height: 22px;
  }
}
</style>
```

### Scrolling body
Put long content in DialogBody: only the body scrolls, while the header and footer stay in view. Without it the whole panel scrolls. On a phone, background on DialogHeader and DialogFooter sets how the Sheet's bars meet the body scrolled under them, as a Toolbar's do: soft by default, or hard, glass or solid.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Review terms</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })">
    <DialogHeader>
      <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Terms of service</DialogTitle></div>
      <DialogDescription>Please read them before you continue.</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-4 text-sm text-secondary max-md:text-[length:var(--dialog-mobile-type-size)] max-md:leading-[var(--dialog-mobile-type-leading)]">
        <p v-for="(term, index) in [...terms, ...terms]" :key="index">{{ term }}</p>
      </div>
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Decline</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Accept</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Placement
Use placement to move the panel: top for search and command lists, bottom for a sheet along the bottom edge, start and end for a full-height side panel, fullscreen for the whole screen. mobile-placement applies at 767px and below, so a centered dialog can become a bottom sheet on phones: {''}.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Open filters</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" :mobile-placement="placement === 'fullscreen' ? 'fullscreen' : 'bottom'" @open-auto-focus="$event.preventDefault(); $refs.heading.focus({ preventScroll: true })" :placement="placement" :width="placement === 'start' || placement === 'end' ? 360 : 480">
    <DialogHeader>
      <div ref="heading" tabindex="-1" class="outline-none"><DialogTitle>Filters</DialogTitle></div>
      <DialogDescription>Narrow the list down.</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <FieldGroup columns>
        <Field row label="Status"><Input placeholder="Any status" /></Field>
        <Field row label="Owner"><Input placeholder="Anyone" /></Field>
        <Field row label="Created after"><Input placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD" /></Field>
      </FieldGroup>
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Clear filters</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Done</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ViewOptionsSheetDemo } from './vue/ViewOptionsSheet';
</script>

<template>
<ViewOptionsSheetDemo />
</template>
```

```vue
</template>
```
