## File Upload
Mobile-first file upload with MIME-type and size validation, multi-file selection, error states, and read-only file cards.
Import: import { FileUpload, FileCard, FileCardGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/FileUpload"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/file-upload
Props:
  - variant ("compact" | "area") default: "compact" — Visual variant: compact button or larger tappable area
  - label (string) — Button label text
  - description (string) — Helper text below the icon (area variant only)
  - accept (string) — Accepted file types (e.g. ".pdf,.png,image/*")
  - multiple (boolean) default: false — Allow selecting multiple files. Without it, a new pick replaces the current file
  - max-files (number) — Maximum number of files. In multiple mode, the button disables at the limit. Single-file mode keeps the button available for replacement.
  - max-size (number) — Maximum file size in bytes
  - value / v-model:value (File[]) — Controlled file list; `update:value` fires when files are added or removed
  - @file-reject ((file: File, reason: string) => void) — Fires when a file is rejected (wrong type, too large, over limit)
  - error-message (string) — Error message; sets the invalid visual state
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid state (auto-set when error-message is provided)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable all interactions
  - id (string) — Id of the hidden file input
  - class (string) — Additional CSS class
  FileCard:
    - file (File) — The file to display
    - @remove (() => void) — Remove callback. When omitted, the delete button is hidden.
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the remove button
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class
  FileCardGroup:
    - default slot (VNode[]) — FileCard elements
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class

### Overview
Toggle errorMessage and disabled while keeping the selected files. The error control demonstrates a supplied message; it does not validate the number of files. Accepted types and size limits still apply.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { FileUpload } from '@plexui/vue/components/FileUpload';

const files = ref<File[]>([]);
const errorMessage = ref(false);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
  <FileUpload
    v-model:value="files"
    accept=".pdf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg"
    multiple
    :max-files="5"
    :max-size="10 * 1024 * 1024"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Please upload at least 2 documents.' : undefined"
    :disabled="disabled"
  />
</template>
```

### With files
File list renders below the button in a bordered pill container. Each file shows a type icon, name with middle truncation, size, and a remove button. files starts with three File objects.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileUpload
    v-model:value="files"
    accept=".pdf,.png"
    multiple
    :max-files="5"
  />
</div>
```

### Read-only
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup>
    <FileCard :file="file" />
  </FileCardGroup>
</div>
```

### With remove
Removing the focused file moves focus to the existing File removed status without scrolling. Pointer removal does not move focus if the remove button was not focused.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue';

function removeFileWithFocus(root, index, remove) {
  const buttons = Array.from(root?.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled)') ?? []);
  const source = buttons[index];
  const target = buttons[index + 1] ?? buttons[index - 1] ?? root?.querySelector('[data-file-removal-status]');
  const doc = source?.ownerDocument;
  const restore = source !== undefined && doc?.activeElement === source;
  remove();
  if (!restore || !source || !doc || !(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
  queueMicrotask(() => {
    if (!source.isConnected && target.isConnected && (doc.activeElement === doc.body || doc.activeElement === source)) {
      target.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
  });
}

const file = ref(initialFile);
</script>

<template>
<div ref="fileCards" class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup v-if="file">
    <FileCard :file="file" @remove="removeFileWithFocus($refs.fileCards, 0, () => { file = null })" />
  </FileCardGroup>
  <div role="status" tabindex="-1" data-file-removal-status :class="file ? 'sr-only' : 'text-center text-sm text-tertiary'">
    {{ file ? '' : 'File removed' }}
  </div>
</div>
</template>
```

### Multiple files
After keyboard removal, focus moves to the next file, then the previous one, or the Files removed status when the list becomes empty. The same helper preserves the scroll position.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue';

function removeFileWithFocus(root, index, remove) {
  const buttons = Array.from(root?.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled)') ?? []);
  const source = buttons[index];
  const target = buttons[index + 1] ?? buttons[index - 1] ?? root?.querySelector('[data-file-removal-status]');
  const doc = source?.ownerDocument;
  const restore = source !== undefined && doc?.activeElement === source;
  remove();
  if (!restore || !source || !doc || !(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
  queueMicrotask(() => {
    if (!source.isConnected && target.isConnected && (doc.activeElement === doc.body || doc.activeElement === source)) {
      target.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
  });
}

const files = ref(initialFiles);
</script>

<template>
<div ref="fileCards" class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup v-if="files.length > 0">
    <FileCard
      v-for="(file, index) in files"
      :key="file.name + '-' + file.size"
      :file="file"
      @remove="removeFileWithFocus($refs.fileCards, index, () => { files = files.filter((_, i) => i !== index) })"
    />
  </FileCardGroup>
  <div role="status" tabindex="-1" data-file-removal-status :class="files.length > 0 ? 'sr-only' : 'text-center text-sm text-tertiary'">
    {{ files.length > 0 ? '' : 'Files removed' }}
  </div>
</div>
</template>
```

### Read-only list
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup>
    <FileCard v-for="file in files" :key="file.name + '-' + file.size" :file="file" />
  </FileCardGroup>
</div>
```

## Float Input
Text input whose label floats above the value, with error and helper text, a clear button, a password reveal toggle, and a multi-line variant.
Import: import { FloatingLabelInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/FloatingLabelInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/floating-label-input
  FloatingLabelInput:
    - label (string) — Label text for the floating label
    - value / v-model:value (string) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on input
    - default-value (string) — Initial value when uncontrolled
    - error-message (string) — Error message to display below the input
    - invalid (boolean) default: false (true if error-message) — Mark the input as invalid
    - description (string | slot) — Helper text below the input
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the input
    - read-only / readonly (boolean) default: false — Makes the input read-only (both spellings work)
    - type (string) default: "text" — Input type; password gets a reveal toggle
    - revealable (boolean) default: true for password without an end adornment — Built-in reveal toggle
    - multiline (boolean) default: false — Render an auto-growing textarea
    - rows (number) default: 2 — Visible lines when multiline
    - label-placement ("border" | "inside") default: "border" — Where the label rests once it floats. inside keeps it in the box, with the value under it, and always takes the rounded box
    - pill (boolean) default: true — Pill shape; false takes the rounded box of the other fields
    - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: false — Let password-manager extensions decorate the input
    - #startAdornment / #endAdornment (slots) — Content before or after the input (camelCase slot names). A start adornment is always shown and keeps the label floated
    - @clear (() => void) — Shows the clear button and fires when it is clicked; focus returns to the input
    - @revealed-change ((revealed: boolean) => void) — Fires when the reveal toggle is clicked
    - @autofill (() => void) — Fires when the browser autofills the input
  FloatingLabelSelect:
    - label (string) — Label text for the floating label
    - selected (boolean) default: false — Whether a value is selected: floats the label and shows the default slot
    - open (boolean) default: false — Whether the attached list is open
    - error-message (string) — Error message to display below the field
    - invalid (boolean) default: false (true if error-message) — Mark the field as invalid
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the field
    - label-placement ("border" | "inside") default: "border" — Where the label rests once it floats. inside keeps it in the box, with the value under it, and always takes the rounded box
    - pill (boolean) default: true — Pill shape; false takes the rounded box of the other fields
    - @clear-click (() => void) — Shows a clear button when a value is selected
    - @interact (() => void) — Fires on pointer down and on the keys that open the field. Without it those keys click the field, so a wrapping trigger opens

### Overview
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" />
</div>
```

### Types
Two things set the type: where the label rests, and the shape of the field. An empty field shows the label as a placeholder, so the difference appears once it has a value or the focus. Text fields, textareas and selects work the same in each.
```vue
<div class="grid w-[44rem] max-w-[calc(100vw-5rem)] grid-cols-1 items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-6 py-8 sm:grid-cols-[10rem_1fr_1fr]">
  <div class="max-sm:hidden"></div>
  <div class="text-sm text-secondary max-sm:hidden">Empty</div>
  <div class="text-sm text-secondary max-sm:hidden">Filled</div>
  <div class="text-sm">
    Label on the border
    <div class="text-secondary">Pill</div>
  </div>
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" />
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@example.com" />
  <div class="text-sm">
    Label on the border
    <div class="text-secondary">Rounded box</div>
  </div>
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" :pill="false" />
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@example.com" :pill="false" />
  <div class="text-sm">
    Label inside
    <div class="text-secondary">Rounded box</div>
  </div>
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" label-placement="inside" />
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@example.com" label-placement="inside" />
</div>
```

### Label inside
Use label-placement="inside" on FloatingLabelInput and FloatingLabelSelect for long forms such as an address: the label stays inside the box, with the value under it. It works with multiline, adornments, errors and the clear button, and always takes the rounded box.
```vue
<div class="flex w-[360px] flex-col gap-3">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Full name" label-placement="inside" default-value="Jane Doe" />
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countries"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Country or region"
        label-placement="inside"
        :selected="!!country"
        :open="open"
      >
        {{ countries.find((option) => option.value === country)?.label }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Address line 1" label-placement="inside" />
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Delivery notes" label-placement="inside" multiline />
</div>
```

### Rounded box
Set :pill="false" to take the rounded box of Input and Select, with the label on the border. Both floating fields follow the same two tokens, so a form can set its radius and gutter in one place.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" :pill="false" />
</div>
```

### Textarea
Use multiline to render an auto-growing textarea with the same floating-label treatment. The textarea starts at 2 rows and grows automatically as content is added. The border radius adjusts to half the input height.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    :key="String(multiline)"
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Message"
    :multiline="multiline"
  />
</div>
```

### Password input
A type="password" field ships a built-in reveal toggle: click the eye to show or hide the value. Set revealable to false to remove it.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Password" type="password" />
</div>
```

### SSN input
A Social Security Number input combining three techniques: an input mask that auto-inserts dashes (XXX-XX-XXXX) with highlighted segment, value masking that hides digits behind bullets (•••-••-••••), and a built-in revealable toggle to reveal the real value. formatSSN, nextSSNDigits and ssnMask are plain helper functions of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <FloatingLabelInput
      label="Social Security Number"
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="off"
      :style="{
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        ...(!visible && digits
          ? { color: 'transparent', caretColor: 'transparent' }
          : focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix
            ? { caretColor: 'transparent' }
            : {}),
      }"
      revealable
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @revealed-change="(revealed) => (visible = revealed)"
    />
    <div
      v-if="(!visible && digits) || (focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix)"
      aria-hidden="true"
      style="position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 0 calc(var(--floating-input-gutter) + 1px); pointer-events: none; font: inherit; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5rem; z-index: 2; font-family: var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace); overflow: hidden"
    >
      <span v-if="!visible && digits" style="white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits).replace(/[0-9]/g, '•') }}</span>
      <span v-else style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits) }}</span>
      <template v-if="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix">
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="ssnMask(digits).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ ssnMask(digits).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).after }}</span>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday input
A date-of-birth input with an inline MM / DD / YYYY mask. The copied example includes its formatting and mask helpers.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { FloatingLabelInput } from '@plexui/vue/components/FloatingLabelInput';

const birthday = ref('');
const focused = ref(false);
const invalid = ref(false);
const disabled = ref(false);
const readOnly = ref(false);

const BIRTHDAY_MASK = 'MM / DD / YYYY';

type MaskParts = { suffix: string; before: string; highlight: string; after: string };

const splitMask = (suffix: string, pattern: RegExp): MaskParts => {
  if (!suffix) return { suffix, before: '', highlight: '', after: '' };
  const match = suffix.match(pattern);
  if (!match) return { suffix, before: suffix, highlight: '', after: '' };
  return { suffix, before: match[1] || '', highlight: match[2] ?? '', after: match[3] || '' };
};

const getBirthdayDigits = (value: string) => value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8);

/** `01151995` → `01 / 15 / 1995`, with the separator added as soon as a part is full */
const formatBirthday = (rawValue: string) => {
  const digits = getBirthdayDigits(rawValue);
  const month = digits.slice(0, 2);
  const day = digits.slice(2, 4);
  const year = digits.slice(4, 8);
  if (digits.length < 2) return month;
  if (digits.length === 2) return `${month} / `;
  if (digits.length < 4) return `${month} / ${day}`;
  if (digits.length === 4) return `${month} / ${day} / `;
  return `${month} / ${day} / ${year}`;
};

/** Formatted value after an edit; backspacing over a separator removes the digit before it */
const nextBirthday = (raw: string, value: string) => {
  const newDigits = getBirthdayDigits(raw);
  if (raw.length < value.length && newDigits.length === getBirthdayDigits(value).length) {
    return formatBirthday(newDigits.slice(0, -1));
  }
  return formatBirthday(raw);
};

/** The rest of `MM / DD / YYYY` after the typed value, split around the next part */
const birthdayMask = (value: string) => {
  const suffix = value.length >= BIRTHDAY_MASK.length ? '' : BIRTHDAY_MASK.slice(value.length);
  return splitMask(suffix, /^(\s*\/\s*)?([A-Z]+)([\s\S]*)/);
};

</script>

<template>
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[360px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <FloatingLabelInput
      label="Birthday"
      :invalid="invalid"
      :disabled="disabled"
      :read-only="readOnly"
      :value="birthday"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
    />
    <div
      v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
      aria-hidden="true"
      style="position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 0 calc(var(--floating-input-gutter) + 1px); pointer-events: none; font: inherit; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5rem; z-index: 2; overflow: hidden"
    >
      <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
      <span
        v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
        style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
      >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</template>
```

### Birthday with validation
Use invalid and an error message to display the birthday validation state. Here the masked input of the Birthday input example is a component, BirthdayMaskedInput, and the error shows in a FieldError below it.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <component
      :is="BirthdayMaskedInput"
      v-model:value="birthday"
      :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
      :aria-describedby="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday) ? 'birthday-validation-demo-error' : undefined"
    />
    <FieldError v-if="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)" id="birthday-validation-demo-error">
      Please enter a valid date.
    </FieldError>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Phone input
A country code selector paired with a FloatingLabelInput that uses the startAdornment slot to show the dial code as a non-editable prefix. The prefix is always shown, and it keeps the label floated.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px] flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="phoneCountryOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :search-predicate="phoneCountrySearchPredicate"
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    size="3xl"
    check-position="end"
    @change="focusLater($refs.phoneInput)"
  >
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
    </template>
    <template #trigger="{ open, onToggle }">
      <component :is="PhoneCountryTrigger" :country="country" :open="open" @click="onToggle" />
    </template>
  </Select>
  <FloatingLabelInput
    ref="phoneInput"
    label="Phone number"
    :value="phone"
    @update:value="(raw) => (phone = formatPhoneNumber(raw.replace(/\D/g, ''), country))"
    type="tel"
    inputmode="tel"
  >
    <template #startAdornment>
      <span class="text-tertiary whitespace-nowrap select-none mr-2">{{ dialCodeOf(country) }}</span>
    </template>
  </FloatingLabelInput>
</div>
```

### Select
Use FloatingLabelSelect for plain text dropdowns. The label floats up when a value is selected, matching FloatingLabelInput styling.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="role"
    :options="roleOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search roles..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Role"
        :selected="!!role"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="role ? () => (role = '') : undefined"
      >
        {{ roleOptions.find((option) => option.value === role)?.label }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Multiple selection
Pair FloatingLabelSelect with a multiple Select to collect several values while keeping the floating-label treatment. The trigger shows the selected count and exposes a clear button that resets the whole list.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    multiple
    clearable
    v-model:value="skills"
    :options="skillOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search skills..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Skills"
        :selected="skills.length > 0"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="skills.length > 0 ? () => (skills = []) : undefined"
      >
        {{
          skills.length === 1
            ? skillOptions.find((option) => option.value === skills[0])?.label
            : skills.length > 1
              ? skills.length + ' skills'
              : ''
        }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Country select
Use FloatingLabelSelect alongside FloatingLabelInput for consistent form styling. The select trigger shares the same floating-label visual shell. The label floats up when a value is selected.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countries"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
    </template>
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Country"
        :selected="!!countryOf(country)"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="countryOf(country) ? () => (country = '') : undefined"
      >
        <span
          v-if="countryOf(country)"
          :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"
        ><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="country" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ countryOf(country).label }}</span></span>
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### States
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { FloatingLabelInput } from '@plexui/vue/components/FloatingLabelInput';

const value = ref('');
const invalid = ref(false);
const errorMessage = ref(false);
const description = ref(false);
const disabled = ref(false);
const readOnly = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Email address"
    :invalid="invalid || errorMessage"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Email is not valid.' : undefined"
    :description="description ? `We'll never share your email with anyone else.` : undefined"
    :disabled="disabled"
    :read-only="readOnly"
  />
</div>
</template>
```

### Clear button
Listen to @clear to display a built-in clear button. The button appears when the input has a value and is not disabled or read-only.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Email address"
    :disabled="disabled"
    :read-only="readOnly"
    @clear="value = ''"
  />
</div>
```

## Indicators
Loading and progress indicators, from circular spinners and determinate progress rings to animated dots, with configurable color and stroke.
Import: import { LoadingIndicator, CircularProgress, LoadingDots } from "@plexui/vue/components/Indicator"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/indicators
  LoadingIndicator:
    - size (number | string) default: "1em" — Size in px or any CSS length. Defaults to 30px with the optional touch stylesheet, 22px inside a field. Also accepts --indicator-size.
    - stroke-width (number) default: 2 — Ring stroke or mobile spoke width in px. Mobile defaults to 4px at 30px and 3px at 22px.
  CircularProgress:
    - progress (number) — Static progress 0-100 (disables the simulated animation)
    - done (boolean) default: false — Fills the ring and ends the simulated progress
    - max-duration (number) default: 15000 — Duration of the simulated progress in ms
    - size (number | string) default: "28px" — Diameter in px or any CSS length
    - stroke-width (number) default: 2 — Stroke thickness in px, capped at 4
    - track-color (string) — Track color (CSS variable or value)
    - track-active-color (string) — Active segment color

### Overview
The demos restart every few seconds through a done flag that fills the ring before the simulation starts over.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-6">
  <LoadingIndicator :size="20" />
  <CircularProgress :size="24" :done="done" />
  <LoadingDots />
</div>
```

### LoadingIndicator
Simple spinning indicator for general loading states.
```vue
<LoadingIndicator />
```

### Sizing & stroke
By default, the indicator adapts to the surrounding font-size and color of text. You can control the size of the LoadingIndicator using the --indicator-size CSS custom property.
```vue
<LoadingIndicator :size="size" :stroke-width="strokeWidth" />
```

### Color
The indicator defaults to currentcolor, inheriting the surrounding text color. This can also be customized by altering the --indicator-color variable around the DOM scope of the component.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-danger">
  <LoadingIndicator :size="16" /> <span>Destroying forever...</span>
</div>
```

### Duration
You can control the rotation speed of the spinner with --indicator-rotate-duration.
```vue
<div style="--indicator-rotate-duration: 3s">
  <LoadingIndicator />
</div>
```

### CircularProgress
Circular progress ring with simulated progress animation.
```vue
<CircularProgress :done="done" />
```

### Progress
Use progress to show static progress and prevent the animation.
```vue
<CircularProgress :progress="25" />
```

### Sizing & stroke
Use size and stroke-width. Or use --circular-progress-size and --circular-progress-stroke CSS variables.
```vue
<CircularProgress :size="size" :stroke-width="strokeWidth" :done="done" />
```

### Color
Customize the color of the indicator with the --circular-progress-track-color and --circular-progress-track-active-color variables.
```vue
<CircularProgress
  track-color="var(--alpha-15)"
  track-active-color="var(--gray-1000)"
  :done="done"
/>
```

### LoadingDots
Indicate typing progress with staggered pulsing dot animation.
```vue
<LoadingDots />
```

### Color
The indicator defaults to currentcolor, inheriting the surrounding text color. You can customize the color by providing a different color value or context.
```vue
<div class="text-danger">
  <LoadingDots />
</div>
```

### Phone spinner
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-6">
<LoadingIndicator />
<LoadingIndicator :size="22" />
</div>
```

## Input
Text input with start and end adornments, a clear button, a password visibility toggle, and presets for SSN and birthday.
Import: import { Input, InputAdornmentButton } from "@plexui/vue/components/Input"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/input
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (string | number) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on every input
  - default-value (string | number) — Initial value when uncontrolled
  - variant ("outline" | "soft" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Visual style of the input; ghost has no border and no fill
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Controls the size of the input
  - gutter-size (string) — Controls gutter on the edges; defaults to value from size
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the input visually and from interactions
  - read-only / readonly (boolean) default: false — Makes the input read-only and hides the clear button
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark the input as invalid
  - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: false (true with name or type="password") — Allow autofill extensions to appear in the input
  - auto-select (boolean) default: false — Select all contents of the input when mounted
  - startAdornment / endAdornment (slots) — Content rendered at the start or end of the input
  - @clear ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — When set, displays a clear button that appears when the input has a value
  - @autofill (() => void) — Fires when the browser autofills the input
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded pill shape
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Applies a negative margin using the current gutter to optically align with surrounding content
  InputAdornmentButton:
    - color (SemanticColor) — Tint of the pressed (aria-pressed="true") state; the neutral primary-soft tint when unset
    - type (string) default: "button" — Button type

### Overview
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Enter text..." />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the input is the value of a row. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Email">
    <Input type="email" placeholder="name@example.com" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the input. ghost has no border and no fill, for a field its container already frames, such as the search line at the top of a dialog; it still turns red when invalid. The state control shows each variant empty, with a value, invalid and disabled. You decide how the invalid state is removed, whether that's through @focus, @update:value, a submit handler, etc.; [data-invalid] is applied to the container for custom styling. disabled greys the input out and prevents user interactions. On Phone the row is the frame, and the value stands in one of three places: at the end of the row, at one column for the whole group with FieldGroup columns, or under the label with orientation="vertical" and a ghost input. A row has no frame to color, so an error is the row's error-message under it.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[320px]">
  <Input
    v-for="variant in ['outline', 'soft', 'ghost']"
    :key="variant + state"
    type="email"
    :variant="variant"
    placeholder="name@example.com"
    :default-value="state === 'empty' ? undefined : state === 'invalid' ? 'jane@example' : 'jane@example.com'"
    :invalid="state === 'invalid'"
    :disabled="state === 'disabled'"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the value at the end of the row, at one column, or under the label. -->
<FieldGroup
  v-for="place in [
    { title: 'Value at the end', columns: false, vertical: false },
    { title: 'Values in a column', columns: true, vertical: false },
    { title: 'Value under the label', columns: false, vertical: true },
  ]"
  :key="place.title"
  :title="place.title"
  :columns="place.columns"
>
  <Field row :orientation="place.vertical ? 'vertical' : undefined" label="Name">
    <Input
      :key="state"
      :variant="place.vertical ? 'ghost' : undefined"
      placeholder="Required"
      :default-value="state === 'empty' ? undefined : 'Jane Appleseed'"
      :disabled="state === 'disabled'"
    />
  </Field>
  <Field
    row
    :orientation="place.vertical ? 'vertical' : undefined"
    label="Email"
    :error-message="state === 'invalid' ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : undefined"
  >
    <Input
      :key="state"
      type="email"
      :variant="place.vertical ? 'ghost' : undefined"
      placeholder="name@example.com"
      :default-value="state === 'empty' ? undefined : state === 'invalid' ? 'jane@example' : 'jane@example.com'"
      :invalid="state === 'invalid'"
      :disabled="state === 'disabled'"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Sizing & roundness
Sizing of the input is controlled with the size and gutter-size props. size sets the input to a specific control height, and gutter-size sets the horizontal padding. Each size provides default values for gutter-size, as well as font-size. Each of these values can be overridden independently. On Phone, a row sets the input's height, insets and type, and with touch.css the 3xs to xl sizes are all 44px, so this example has no Phone switch.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 justify-start items-start w-[320px]">
  <Input
    placeholder="Enter text..."
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  />
  <Input
    placeholder="Search..."
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #startAdornment>
      <Search class="fill-tertiary" />
    </template>
  </Input>
  <Input
    v-model:value="username"
    placeholder="Username"
    maxlength="16"
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #endAdornment>
      <span class="mt-[1.5px] tabular-nums text-tertiary text-[.875em]">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the row sets the height, insets and type. -->
<FieldGroup columns>
  <Field row label="Name">
    <Input placeholder="Enter text..." />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Username">
    <Input v-model:value="username" placeholder="Required" maxlength="16">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <span class="tabular-nums text-secondary">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Adornments
Add arbitrary content to the beginning of an input with the startAdornment slot, like a search icon, or to its end with the endAdornment slot, like a character counter or custom icon; position switches between the two. Ideally, the spacing will look pretty good by default. If you need to customize the spacing: gutter-size determines the amount of spacing on the edges of the container, which you can fine-tune as needed (if you need precise spacing not supported by gutter-size, use a negative margin on your adornment directly, e.g. -ml-2). The spacing between the adornment and the input is controlled with gap on the container, which you can customize as needed with Tailwind classes. On Phone, search is the Toolbar search field; in a row a start adornment is a prefix of the value, such as a currency, and trailing text-secondary text takes the row's accessory color.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input v-if="position === 'start'" placeholder="Search">
    <template #startAdornment>
      <Search class="fill-tertiary" />
    </template>
  </Input>
  <Input v-else v-model:value="username" placeholder="Username" maxlength="16">
    <template #endAdornment>
      <span class="mt-[1.5px] tabular-nums text-tertiary text-[.875em]">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>

<!-- Phone: a prefix of the value, which starts at the column, or trailing text. -->
<FieldGroup v-if="position === 'start'" columns>
  <Field row label="Amount">
    <Input placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal">
      <template #startAdornment>
        <span class="text-secondary">$</span>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup v-else>
  <Field row label="Username">
    <Input v-model:value="username" placeholder="Required" maxlength="16">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <span class="tabular-nums text-secondary">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Clear button
Listen to @clear to display a built-in clear button. The button appears when the input has a value and is not disabled or read-only. Clicking it keeps focus in the input.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    v-model:value="value"
    placeholder="Enter text..."
    :pill="pill"
    :size="size"
    @clear="value = ''"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Name">
    <Input v-model:value="value" placeholder="Enter text..." @clear="value = ''" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Password with visibility toggle
Put an InputAdornmentButton in the endAdornment slot to switch between type="password" and type="text". Works across all sizes, variants, and with pill. On Phone, the button stands at the end of the row.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    placeholder="Password"
    :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'"
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #endAdornment>
      <InputAdornmentButton
        :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'"
        :aria-pressed="visible"
        @click="visible = !visible"
      >
        <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
        <Eye v-else />
      </InputAdornmentButton>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Password">
    <Input placeholder="Required" :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'" :aria-pressed="visible" @click="visible = !visible">
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### SSN input
A masked SSN field that formats input as XXX-XX-XXXX, hides digits behind bullets when masked, and reveals the value via an InputAdornmentButton in the endAdornment slot. An inline mask suffix highlights the next expected segment. Adapts to all sizes automatically. formatSSN, nextSSNDigits, ssnMask and measureInput are plain helper functions of the example. On Phone the row is the input's frame, and the overlay needs a wrapper that the row cannot frame: the field formats as you type and hides as a secure field.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <Input
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      :placeholder="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix ? undefined : 'Social Security Number'"
      :size="size"
      :variant="variant"
      :pill="pill"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="{
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        ...(!visible && digits
          ? { color: 'transparent', caretColor: 'transparent' }
          : focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix
            ? { caretColor: 'transparent' }
            : {}),
      }"
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
    >
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton
          :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'"
          :aria-pressed="visible"
          @click="visible = !visible"
        >
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
    <div
      v-if="(!visible && digits) || (focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix)"
      aria-hidden="true"
      :style="{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '0',
        left: '0',
        right: '0',
        bottom: '0',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
        lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
        zIndex: 2,
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }"
    >
      <span v-if="!visible && digits" style="white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits).replace(/[0-9]/g, '•') }}</span>
      <span v-else style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits) }}</span>
      <template v-if="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix">
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="ssnMask(digits).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ ssnMask(digits).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).after }}</span>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="SSN">
    <Input
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      placeholder="XXX-XX-XXXX"
      :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="off"
      :allow-autofill-extensions="false"
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
    >
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'" :aria-pressed="visible" @click="visible = !visible">
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Birthday input
A date-of-birth input with inline formatting that inserts separators as you type (MM / DD / YYYY). An inline mask suffix highlights the next expected segment. Works across all sizes, variants, and with pill. nextBirthday, birthdayMask and measureInput are plain helper functions of the example. On Phone the field formats as you type and the pattern is its placeholder, as in the About you form.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      :placeholder="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? undefined : 'Birthday (MM / DD / YYYY)'"
      :size="size"
      :variant="variant"
      :pill="pill"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
    />
    <div
      v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
      aria-hidden="true"
      :style="{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '0',
        left: '0',
        right: '0',
        bottom: '0',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
        lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
        zIndex: 2,
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }"
    >
      <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
      <span
        v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
        style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
      >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Birthday">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      placeholder="MM / DD / YYYY"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="bday"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Birthday with validation
Use invalid and FieldError to display a validation state when the entered date is not valid. On Phone, give the message to the row's Field as error-message: it stands under the row.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <div style="position: relative">
      <Input
        :value="birthday"
        :placeholder="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? undefined : 'Birthday (MM / DD / YYYY)'"
        :size="size"
        :variant="variant"
        :pill="pill"
        maxlength="14"
        inputmode="numeric"
        :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
        :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
        @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
        @focus="focused = true"
        @blur="focused = false"
        @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
        @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      />
      <div
        v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
        aria-hidden="true"
        :style="{
          position: 'absolute',
          top: '0',
          left: '0',
          right: '0',
          bottom: '0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
          pointerEvents: 'none',
          fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
          lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
          zIndex: 2,
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }"
      >
        <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
        <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <FieldError v-if="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)" id="input-birthday-validation-demo-error">
      Please enter a valid date.
    </FieldError>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Birthday" :error-message="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday) ? 'Please enter a valid date.' : undefined">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      placeholder="MM / DD / YYYY"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="bday"
      :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Behavior
Pass auto-select to select all text in the input, useful when pairing with other copy actions. This prop behaves like an imperative action when toggled and allows selection to change. By default, autofill extensions are prevented by Input. For the occasions where you want browser extensions to be present, pass allow-autofill-extensions to remove the disabling. When name is passed, or type is set to "password", this value will default to true. Both behave the same in a phone row, so this example has no Phone switch.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Add text..." :auto-select="autoSelect" default-value="Toggle to auto select" />
  <Input
    :key="String(allowAutofillExtensions)"
    name="email"
    :allow-autofill-extensions="allowAutofillExtensions"
    :placeholder="allowAutofillExtensions ? 'Allowed' : 'Not allowed'"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Name">
    <Input placeholder="Add text..." :auto-select="autoSelect" default-value="Toggle to auto select" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Email">
    <Input
      :key="String(allowAutofillExtensions)"
      name="email"
      :allow-autofill-extensions="allowAutofillExtensions"
      :placeholder="allowAutofillExtensions ? 'Allowed' : 'Not allowed'"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### With button
You can pair Input and Button components together by using the same size value. On Phone, the button is the 50px main action under the group, size="3xl".
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex w-[320px] gap-2">
  <Input placeholder="jane.doe@gmail.com" :size="size" :variant="variant" />
  <Button color="primary" :size="size" :variant="variant">Subscribe</Button>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Email">
    <Input type="email" placeholder="jane.doe@gmail.com" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Subscribe</Button>
```

### Optical alignment
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-6 px-8">
    <div class="mb-5 text-secondary text-sm">
      {{ opticallyAlign ? 'optically-align="start"' : 'Default' }}
    </div>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <Field label="Full name" :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined">
        <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
      <Field
        label="Email"
        description="We'll never share your email."
        error-message="Please enter a valid email address."
        required
        :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined"
      >
        <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
    </div>
    <Button class="mt-6" color="primary" variant="soft" :pill="pill">Submit</Button>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the help is the group's footer. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Full name">
    <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Email" error-message="Please enter a valid email address.">
    <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" />
  </Field>
  <template #footer>We'll never share your email.</template>
</FieldGroup>
<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Submit</Button>
```

## Input Group
Input or textarea with icons, text, buttons and menus attached inside one frame, inline or as header and footer rows, in every input size and style.
Import: import { InputGroup, InputGroupAddon, InputGroupButton, InputGroupInput, InputGroupText, InputGroupTextarea } from "@plexui/vue/components/InputGroup"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/input-group
Props:
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height, on the same scale as Input (22px to 48px)
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style of the frame
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded frame, with pill buttons inside
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the control and the buttons inside
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid border; sets aria-invalid on the control
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>
  InputGroupAddon:
    - align ("inline-start" | "inline-end" | "block-start" | "block-end") default: "inline-start" — Before or after the control on its line, or a row above or below it
    - class (string) — Applied to the addon <div>

### Overview
Put an InputGroupInput and one or more InputGroupAddons inside an InputGroup. The group draws the frame and passes size, pill, disabled and invalid down to the control and the buttons.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search..." />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>12 results</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="example" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Ask anything..." />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupButton variant="solid" color="primary" uniform aria-label="Send">
        <ArrowUpSm />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the group is the value of a row; with a prefix the rows take columns. -->
<FieldGroup columns>
  <Field row label="Website" :error-message="invalid ? 'Enter a valid address.' : undefined">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="acme.com" inputmode="url" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Amount">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup :disabled="disabled">
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>$</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the group, like Input. On Phone the row is the frame, so a group in a row keeps the default outline, which the row draws without a border, and the row places the value: at its end, or, with columns, at one column shared by the group's rows.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup variant="outline">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Outline" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup variant="soft">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Soft" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup title="Value at the end">
  <Field row label="Distance">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="5" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <InputGroupText>km</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup title="Values in a column" columns>
  <Field row label="Handle">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="username" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>@</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Price">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>€</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Sizes
Every Input size from 3xs to 3xl. Icons, text and InputGroupButton scale with the group: buttons in inline addons fit the control height and keep a 4px inset. On a phone with touch.css, 3xs to xl are all 44px, and in a row the row sets the height: the iOS 26 field is one 52px row, so this example is desktop only.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[360px]">
  <div v-for="size in sizes" :key="size" class="flex items-center gap-4">
    <span class="w-8 shrink-0 text-right text-sm text-tertiary">{{ size }}</span>
    <InputGroup :size="size" :pill="pill">
      <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search..." />
      <InputGroupAddon>
        <Search />
      </InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
        <InputGroupButton>Search</InputGroupButton>
      </InputGroupAddon>
    </InputGroup>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup columns>
  <Field row label="Domain">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="plexui.com" inputmode="url" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Align
Use align on InputGroupAddon to place it before (inline-start, the default) or after (inline-end) the control, or as a row above (block-start) or below (block-end) it. Place addons after the control in the markup so the tab order starts with the field; align handles the visual position. On Phone the inline addons stay in the row (see Variants), a block-start text becomes the row's label and a block-end text the group's footer.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="inline-start" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-start">
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="inline-end" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InfoCircle />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Ada Lovelace" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-start">
      <InputGroupText>Full name</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="0.00" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-end">
      <InputGroupText>Amount in USD</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Full name">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="Ada Lovelace" />
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup>
  <template #footer>Amount in USD</template>
  <Field row label="Amount">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Text
Use InputGroupText for prefixes, suffixes, units and counters. Clicking text or icons in an addon focuses the field.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>$</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>

    </InputGroupAddon>

    <InputGroupInput placeholder="example" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>

    <InputGroupInput placeholder="username" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Email />
    </InputGroupAddon>

    </InputGroupAddon>

      <InputGroupText>{{ 280 - post.length }} characters left</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>

</div>

<!-- Phone: a counter is the group's footer. -->

      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup>
  <template #footer>{{ 280 - post.length }} characters left</template>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Post">
    <Textarea v-model:value="post" variant="ghost" placeholder="What's happening?" maxlength="280" auto-resize :rows="2" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Button
InputGroupButton is a Button that defaults to ghost and secondary and takes its size from the group. Pass uniform for icon buttons.
```vue

<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">

    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search the docs..." />

</FieldGroup>
<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Pay</Button>
```

### Dropdown
Wrap an InputGroupButton in a Menu to pick a unit of measurement next to the value.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from "vue"
import { ChevronDownMd } from "@plexui/vue/components/Icon"
import {
  InputGroup,
  InputGroupAddon,
  InputGroupButton,
  InputGroupInput,
  InputGroupText,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/InputGroup"
import { Menu, MenuCheckboxItem, MenuContent, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"

const UNITS = [
  { value: "kg", label: "Kilograms" },
  { value: "lb", label: "Pounds" },
  { value: "st", label: "Stones" },
]
const unit = ref("kg")
</script>

<template>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="72" inputmode="decimal" aria-label="Weight" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>Weight</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger>
          <InputGroupButton aria-label="Weight unit">
            {{ unit }}<ChevronDownMd />
          </InputGroupButton>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent align="end" :min-width="160">
          <MenuCheckboxItem
            v-for="item in UNITS"
            :key="item.value"
            :checked="unit === item.value"
            indicator-variant="ghost"
            @update:checked="unit = item.value"
          >
            {{ item.label }} ({{ item.value }})
          </MenuCheckboxItem>
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</template>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Weight">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="72" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger>
              <InputGroupButton :aria-label="'Unit: ' + weightUnit" class="text-secondary font-normal">
                {{ weightUnit }}<span class="ms-1"><DropdownIcon icon-type="dropdown" /></span>
              </InputGroupButton>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuCheckboxItem
                v-for="unit in weightUnits"
                :key="unit.value"
                :checked="weightUnit === unit.value"
                indicator-position="start"
                indicator-variant="ghost"
                @update:checked="weightUnit = unit.value"
              >
                {{ unit.label }}
              </MenuCheckboxItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Height">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="180" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger>
              <InputGroupButton :aria-label="'Unit: ' + lengthUnit" class="text-secondary font-normal">
                {{ lengthUnit }}<span class="ms-1"><DropdownIcon icon-type="dropdown" /></span>
              </InputGroupButton>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuCheckboxItem
                v-for="unit in lengthUnits"
                :key="unit.value"
                :checked="lengthUnit === unit.value"
                indicator-position="start"
                indicator-variant="ghost"
                @update:checked="lengthUnit = unit.value"
              >
                {{ unit.label }}
              </MenuCheckboxItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Spinner
Show a LoadingIndicator in an addon while a value is searched, checked or saved. On a phone with touch.css an indicator in a field is 22px, the iOS 26 small indicator, instead of the standalone 30px.
```vue

<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Searching..." readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Processing..." readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Saving changes" readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>Saving...</InputGroupText>
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <template #footer>Checking availability...</template>
  <Field row label="Username">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="ada" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <LoadingIndicator />
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Textarea
With InputGroupTextarea, use block-start and block-end addons for a header and a footer. Utility classes such as ml-auto push a button to the far side.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupTextarea placeholder="console.log('Hello, world!');" :rows="4" class="font-mono" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-start">
      <InputGroupText>
        <FileCode />script.js
      </InputGroupText>
      <InputGroupButton uniform aria-label="Copy" class="ml-auto">
        <Copy />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-end">
      <InputGroupText>Line 1, Column 1</InputGroupText>
      <InputGroupButton variant="solid" color="primary" class="ml-auto">
        Run<Play />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the header is the row's label, the status line the group's footer. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <template #footer>Line 1, Column 1</template>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="script.js">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" placeholder="console.log('Hello, world!');" :rows="4" class="font-mono" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Run</Button>
```

## Kbd
A key or a keyboard shortcut, such as ⌘K, set inline in text, in a tooltip or at the end of a menu item.
Import: import { Kbd, KbdGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/Kbd"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/kbd
Props:
  - size ("xs" | "sm" | "md") default: "sm" — Height and text size: 16px, 20px or 24px
  - variant ("soft" | "outline") default: "soft" — soft fills the key, outline draws its edge; both mix the text color
  - class (string) — Applied to the kbd element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — The key

### Overview
Kbd renders a {''} element: the semantic tag for keyboard input. It sits on the text baseline, so it can stand in a sentence.
```vue
<p class="text-sm text-secondary">
  Press <Kbd>/</Kbd> to search, <Kbd>Esc</Kbd> to close.
</p>
```

### Shortcut
Use KbdGroup for keys pressed together. Each key keeps its own frame.
```vue
<KbdGroup>
  <Kbd>⌘</Kbd>
  <Kbd>K</Kbd>
</KbdGroup>
<KbdGroup>
  <Kbd>Ctrl</Kbd>
  <Kbd>Shift</Kbd>
  <Kbd>P</Kbd>
</KbdGroup>
```

### Sizes and variants
Three sizes: xs (16px) for tooltips and menu items, sm (20px, default) for text, md (24px) next to larger controls. soft (default) fills the key, outline draws its edge. Both mix the surrounding text color, so a key reads on a page, in a dark tooltip and in a highlighted menu item alike.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
  <div v-for="variant in ['soft', 'outline']" :key="variant" class="flex items-center gap-4">
    <Kbd size="xs" :variant="variant">⌘K</Kbd>
    <Kbd size="sm" :variant="variant">⌘K</Kbd>
    <Kbd size="md" :variant="variant">⌘K</Kbd>
  </div>
</div>
```

### In a tooltip and a menu
Show a control's shortcut in its tooltip, or at the end of a menu item. Kbd only shows the keys: announce the shortcut to assistive technology with aria-keyshortcuts on the control that has it.
```vue
<Tooltip>
  <template #content>
    <span class="flex items-center gap-2">
      Search
      <KbdGroup>
        <Kbd size="xs">⌘</Kbd>
        <Kbd size="xs">K</Kbd>
      </KbdGroup>
    </span>
  </template>
  <Button color="secondary" variant="outline" uniform aria-label="Search" aria-keyshortcuts="Meta+K">
    <Search />
  </Button>
</Tooltip>
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Edit</Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :min-width="220">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1">Undo</span>
      <Kbd size="xs" variant="outline">⌘Z</Kbd>
    </MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1">Redo</span>
      <Kbd size="xs" variant="outline">⇧⌘Z</Kbd>
    </MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

## Label
Accessible form label that points at any control by its id, so clicking it focuses or toggles the control, with consistent typography across forms.
Import: import { Label } from "@plexui/vue/components/Label"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/label
Props:
  - for (string) — id of the associated control. Clicks on the label focus or activate the control.
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <label> element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Label content

### Overview
Clicking the label toggles the checkbox it points at. Here accepted starts as true.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px">
  <Checkbox id="label-demo-terms" v-model:checked="accepted" />
  <Label for="label-demo-terms">Accept terms and conditions</Label>
</div>
```

### Label in Field
For stacked form rows with descriptions, errors, and horizontal layouts, use the Field component. It renders the label internally, hooks up for automatically, and handles the surrounding spacing.
```vue
<div style="width: 100%; max-width: 420px">
  <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px">
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Payment Method</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>All transactions are secure and encrypted</CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>

    <Field label="Name on Card">
      <Input placeholder="Aisha Cooper" />
    </Field>

    <Field label="Card Number" description="Enter your 16-digit card number">
      <Input placeholder="1234 5678 9012 3456" inputmode="numeric" />
    </Field>

    <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr; gap: 16px">
      <Field label="Month">
        <Select v-model:value="month" :options="monthOptions" placeholder="MM" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Year">
        <Select v-model:value="year" :options="yearOptions" placeholder="YYYY" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="CVV">
        <Input placeholder="123" inputmode="numeric" />
      </Field>
    </div>

    <Separator />

    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Billing Address</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>The billing address associated with your payment method</CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>

    <Checkbox v-model:checked="sameAsShipping" label="Same as shipping address" />

    <Separator />

    <Field label="Comments">
      <Textarea placeholder="Add any additional comments" :rows="3" />
    </Field>

    <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px">
      <Button color="primary" variant="solid">Submit</Button>
      <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Cancel</Button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Markdown
Markdown renderer for rich formatted content, with headings, lists, tables, code blocks, and KaTeX math expressions out of the box.
Import: import { Markdown } from "@plexui/vue/components/Markdown"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/markdown
Props:
  - default slot (string) — Markdown source (text only; interpolate a string to keep its newlines)
  - source (string) — Markdown source, used when the default slot is empty
  - include-math (boolean) default: false — Enable LaTeX math rendering
  - break-new-lines (boolean) default: false — Turn single newlines into line breaks
  - copyable-code-blocks (boolean) default: true — Show a copy button on code blocks
  - collapsible-code-blocks (boolean) default: true — Render code blocks in the collapsible view with a View Code button
  - components (Record<string, MarkdownComponent>) — Tag name to component overrides, applied over the built-in ones
  - directives (MarkdownDirective[]) — Custom directives, each rendered by its own component
  - remark-plugins (PluggableList) — Extra remark plugins, after the built-in ones
  - rehype-plugins (PluggableList) — Extra rehype plugins
  - allowed-elements (string[]) — Only render these elements
  - disallowed-elements (string[]) — Never render these elements
  - skip-html (boolean) default: false — Ignore raw HTML in the source
  - url-transform ((url: string) => string) — Rewrites URLs; the default drops unsafe protocols (javascript:) and keeps tel: and sms:
  - class (string) — Applied to the wrapping element

### Overview
Use Markdown to render rich markdown text. The markdown is the text of the default slot: interpolate a string ({"{{ markdown }}"}) so its newlines are kept, or pass it with the source prop.
```vue
<Markdown>{{ markdownContent }}</Markdown>
```

### Math
Use include-math to enable LaTeX rendering.
```vue
<Markdown include-math>{{ markdownWithLatex }}</Markdown>
```

## Markdown Editor
Compact rich text editor powered by Tiptap, embedded in a form field, with a markdown formatting toolbar, variants, and an invalid state.
Import: import { MarkdownEditor } from "@plexui/vue/components/MarkdownEditor"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/markdown-editor
Props:
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style of the editor
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the editor
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark as invalid
  - placeholder (string) — Placeholder text when empty
  - value / v-model:value (string) — Controlled markdown value; `update:value` fires on every change
  - default-value (string) — Initial markdown for uncontrolled mode
  - @change ((markdown: string) => void) — Called on content change
  - toolbarEnd slot (VNode[]) — Content rendered at the end of the toolbar
  - rows (number) default: 1 — Minimum visible rows
  - id (string) — id of the editable element (Field sets it)
  - aria-label (string) — Accessible name of the editable element
  - aria-labelledby (string) — Id of the element that names the editor, such as a visible label
  - aria-describedby (string) — aria-describedby of the editable element (Field sets it)
  - aria-invalid (boolean) — Sets aria-invalid on the editable element and the invalid style (Field sets it with error-message)
  - class (string) — Applied to the container

### Placeholder
Use placeholder for empty content. Toggle invalid and disabled without replacing the entered markdown. The invalid state is controlled by this example, not by a validator.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { MarkdownEditor } from '@plexui/vue/components/MarkdownEditor';

const value = ref('');
const invalid = ref(false);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
  <MarkdownEditor aria-label="Markdown content" placeholder="Enter text..."
    v-model:value="value" :invalid="invalid" :disabled="disabled" />
</template>
```

### All formatting
Example with all supported formatting: bold, italic, links, ordered lists, and unordered lists. Click inside formatted text to see toolbar buttons toggle.
```vue
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[500px]">
  <MarkdownEditor :default-value="allFormattingMd" />
</div>
```

### With Field
Wrap with Field to add a description and a linked error message. The editor receives id, aria-describedby and aria-invalid automatically. Set an accessible name with aria-label. The errorMessage control demonstrates the error state without validating or replacing the content.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { Field } from '@plexui/vue/components/Field';
import { MarkdownEditor } from '@plexui/vue/components/MarkdownEditor';

const value = ref('');
const errorMessage = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
  <Field label="Description" description="Supports bold, italic, lists, and links."
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'This field is required.' : undefined">
    <MarkdownEditor aria-label="Description" placeholder="Enter description..." v-model:value="value" />
  </Field>
</template>
```

### Toolbar end slot
Use the #toolbarEnd slot to render custom content at the end of the toolbar, for example an &ldquo;Edit translations&rdquo; button. markdownEditorStyles.toolbarButton gives it the style of the built-in toolbar buttons.
```vue
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[500px]">
  <Field label="Text markdown">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Enter text...">
      <template #toolbarEnd>
        <button
          type="button"
          :class="markdownEditorStyles.toolbarButton"
          aria-label="Edit translations"
          title="Edit translations"
        >
          <Translate />
        </button>
      </template>
    </MarkdownEditor>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Variants
Use variant to switch between outline (default) and soft styles.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 w-full min-w-0 max-w-[500px]">
  <Field label="Outline">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Outline variant..." variant="outline" />
  </Field>
  <Field label="Soft">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Soft variant..." variant="soft" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

## Menu
Dropdown menu for structured actions with grouped items, submenus, checkbox and radio items, and horizontal layouts.
Import: import { Menu, MenuContent, MenuItem, MenuLink, MenuSeparator, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/menu
Props:
  - force-open (boolean) — Keep menu open (controlled)
  - modal (boolean) default: false — Whether menu is modal (focus trap)
  - @open (() => void) — Emitted when menu opens
  - @close (() => void) — Emitted when menu closes
  MenuTrigger:
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent the menu from opening
  MenuContent:
    - width (number | "auto") default: "auto" — Panel width (numbers are pixels)
    - min-width (number | "auto") default: 300 — Minimum panel width
    - max-height (number) — Maximum panel height, in pixels
    - pill (boolean) default: false — Rounder list with fully rounded rows, for menus beside pill controls. MenuSubContent takes it too
    - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "bottom" — Preferred placement
    - side-offset (number) default: 5 — Distance from the trigger, in pixels
    - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "center" — Alignment relative to trigger
