Validation runs on submit: the phone field shows an error state with FieldError when the number is too short or too long.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped" class="h-dvh" data-form-screen style="--page-content-padding-top:10px; --page-content-padding-bottom:16px; --page-footer-gutter:24px; --page-footer-padding-bottom:max(24px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))">
  <Toolbar>
    <ToolbarLeading><DemoBackButton /></ToolbarLeading>
    <ToolbarTitle>Phone number</ToolbarTitle>
  </Toolbar>
  <PageContent data-form-content>
    <form ref="screenForm" class="flex flex-col gap-6" @submit.prevent="submitted = true" novalidate>
      <FieldGroup title="Phone number" description="Choose your country and enter the number you want to use." columns>
  <Field row label="Country">
    <Select v-model:value="country" :options="countryOptions" search-placeholder="Search countries..."
      :search-predicate="countrySearchPredicate" :list-min-width="300" :option-class-name="countryOptionClass"
      @change="() => { phone = ''; submitted = false; focusLater($refs.phoneInput) }">
      <template #optionView="{ value, label }"><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" />{{ label }}</span></template>
    </Select>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Phone" :error-message="submitted && !isPhoneValid(phone) ? 'Phone number is not valid.' : undefined">
    <Input ref="phoneInput" :value="phone" @update:value="(raw) => { phone = formatPhoneNumber(raw, country); submitted = false }"
      placeholder="Phone number" type="tel" inputmode="tel" autocomplete="tel-national">
      <template #startAdornment><span class="text-tertiary whitespace-nowrap select-none mr-2">{{ countryOf(country)?.dialCode ?? '+1' }}</span></template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
    </form>
  </PageContent>
  <PageFooter data-form-footer><Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block @click="$refs.screenForm.requestSubmit()">Continue</Button></PageFooter>
  <Toolbar placement="bottom"><ToolbarContent fill><Button color="primary" pill block @click="$refs.screenForm.requestSubmit()">Continue</Button></ToolbarContent></Toolbar>
</Page>
```

### Optical alignment
Use optically-align with either "start" or "end" to offset the gutter of the control. In a phone row the row aligns the value with its inset, so optically-align is for desktop, and so is this example.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3 w-[280px]">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-4 px-6">
    <div class="mb-2 text-secondary text-sm">Default</div>
    <SelectControl class="w-full" selected variant="ghost">Ghost control</SelectControl>
  </div>
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-4 px-6">
    <div class="mb-2 text-secondary text-sm">optically-align="{{ opticallyAlign }}"</div>
    <SelectControl class="w-full" selected variant="ghost" :optically-align="opticallyAlign">Ghost control</SelectControl>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Theme">
    <SelectControl selected>System</SelectControl>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

## Separator
A thin divider between content, horizontal or vertical, decorative by default or exposed to assistive technology when it marks a real grouping.
Import: import { Separator } from "@plexui/vue/components/Separator"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/separator
Props:
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Direction of the divider
  - decorative (boolean) default: true — When true, the separator is ignored by assistive technologies. Set false when the divider carries semantic grouping (renders role="separator").
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>

### Overview
```vue
<div style="display: flex; max-width: 384px; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size); line-height: var(--font-text-sm-line-height)">
  <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; line-height: 1.3">
    <div style="font-weight: 500">Aisha Cooper</div>
    <div style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">Engineering · Platform team</div>
  </div>
  <Separator />
  <div>Shipped 24 pull requests this week across CI/CD and developer tooling.</div>
</div>
```

### Vertical
Use orientation="vertical" between inline items. Works for meta-strips, breadcrumbs, and dot-less separators.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; height: 20px; align-items: center; gap: 16px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size)">
  <div>MIT license</div>
  <Separator orientation="vertical" />
  <div>12k weekly downloads</div>
  <Separator orientation="vertical" />
  <div>48 kB bundle</div>
</div>
```

### Menu
Vertical separators between menu items with a short caption underneath each label.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size)">
  <template v-for="(plan, i) in [
    { name: 'Starter', meta: 'Free · 1 project' },
    { name: 'Pro', meta: '$19/mo · Unlimited' },
    { name: 'Enterprise', meta: 'Custom · Priority SLA' },
  ]" :key="plan.name">
    <Separator v-if="i > 0" orientation="vertical" />
    <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; line-height: 1.2">
      <span style="font-weight: 500">{{ plan.name }}</span>
      <span style="font-size: var(--font-text-xs-size); color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ plan.meta }}</span>
    </div>
  </template>
</div>
```

### List
Horizontal separators between rows in a definition list.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; width: 100%; max-width: 384px; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size); line-height: var(--font-text-sm-line-height)">
  <template v-for="(row, i) in [
    ['Version', '0.9.0'],
    ['Published', '2 days ago'],
    ['Bundle', '48 kB'],
    ['License', 'MIT'],
  ]" :key="row[0]">
    <Separator v-if="i > 0" />
    <dl style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0">
      <dt>{{ row[0] }}</dt>
      <dd style="margin: 0; color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ row[1] }}</dd>
    </dl>
  </template>
</div>
```

## Shimmer Text
Text with a shimmer animation for loading and in-progress states, always on or toggled with a graceful stop.
Import: import { ShimmerText, ShimmerableText } from "@plexui/vue/components/ShimmerText"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/shimmer-text
Props:
  - as ("p" | "span" | "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" | "div") default: "div" — HTML element to render
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Text content to shimmer
  - class (string) — Additional CSS classes
  ShimmerableText:
    - as ("p" | "span" | "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" | "div") default: "div" — HTML element to render
    - shimmer (boolean) default: false — Whether the shimmer animation is active
    - default slot (VNode[]) — Text content to shimmer
    - class (string) — Additional CSS classes

### Overview
ShimmerText applies a continuous shimmer animation to text content. Useful for loading states, AI-generated content indicators, or visual emphasis.
```vue
<ShimmerText class="text-lg font-semibold">
  Thinking...
</ShimmerText>
```

### Semantic elements
Use the as prop to render as any text element: p, span, h1 to h6, or div.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
  <ShimmerText as="h2" class="text-2xl font-bold">Heading shimmer</ShimmerText>
  <ShimmerText as="p" class="text-base">Paragraph shimmer effect</ShimmerText>
  <ShimmerText as="span" class="text-sm text-secondary">Inline shimmer</ShimmerText>
</div>
```

### Controllable shimmer
ShimmerableText allows toggling the shimmer on and off. When shimmer is set to false, the animation finishes its current iteration and then pauses gracefully, with no abrupt cut. Use this variant when text transitions between a loading and idle state.
```vue
<ShimmerableText :shimmer="shimmer" class="text-lg font-semibold">
  Generating response...
</ShimmerableText>
```

## Sidebar
Composable navigation sidebar for app shells with collapsible sections, search and filter, tree views with hover actions, and a mobile menu
Import: import { SidebarProvider, SidebarLayout, Sidebar, SidebarHeader, SidebarContent, SidebarFooter, SidebarGroup, SidebarGroupLabel, SidebarMenu, SidebarMenuItem, SidebarMenuButton, SidebarInset, SidebarTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Sidebar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/sidebar
Props:
  - variant ("sidebar" | "docs") default: "sidebar" — Visual variant
  - side ("left" | "right") default: "left" — Which side the sidebar is on
  SidebarProvider:
    - collapsible ("offcanvas" | "icon" | "none") default: "offcanvas" — Collapse behavior: offcanvas (drawer on mobile, full collapse on desktop), icon (icon rail), or none
    - variant ("sidebar" | "app") default: "sidebar" — Look of the whole shell. "app" is the sidebar of a product: 260px, pill rows on a grey ground, a page with no inset, and on a phone a menu under the page
    - default-open (boolean) default: true — Initial open state (uncontrolled)
    - open (boolean) — Controlled open state. Bind with v-model:open
    - @update:open ((open: boolean) => void) — Emitted when the open state changes; powers v-model:open. Without open the provider keeps its own state and still emits
    - class (string) — Class of the provider's wrapper
    - style (StyleValue) — Style of the provider's wrapper, e.g. --sidebar-width
    - default slot ({ state, open, setOpen, openMobile, setOpenMobile, isMobile, collapsible, toggleSidebar }) — Receives the sidebar context, the object useSidebar() returns
  SidebarGroup:
    - default-open (boolean) default: true — Whether the group is open initially. A closed group hides its SidebarGroupContent
    - open (boolean) — Controlled open state, also exposed as data-state. The group has no toggle of its own: change open from your own control
  SidebarGroupLabel:
    - size ("sm" | "lg") default: "lg" — sm: compact, tertiary. lg: larger, primary, more spacing
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the single child element as the label
  SidebarMenuItem:
    - expanded (boolean) — Marks the item expanded, which turns its SidebarMenuChevron. Pair it with open on its SidebarMenuSub
  SidebarMenuButton:
    - is-active (boolean) default: false — Highlight the current page (aria-current="page")
    - tooltip (string) — Label shown on hover when collapsed (icon mode). Hidden on mobile. The tooltip slot takes rich content
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the single child element (e.g. a RouterLink) as the button. The button attributes go on the child and its content is wrapped like the button's
  SidebarMenuSub:
    - open (boolean) default: true — Whether submenu is expanded. A closed submenu is inert, so its links and buttons leave the tab order and the accessibility tree
    - has-icons (boolean) default: false — Align child items with parent text that has icons
  SidebarMenuSubButton:
    - is-active (boolean) default: false — Highlight the current page (aria-current="page")
    - indent (0 | 1 | 2 | 3) default: 0 — Nesting level; each level adds 12px of left margin
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the single child element (e.g. a RouterLink) as the button
  SidebarCard:
    - variant ("default" | "elevated") default: "default" — Visual variant
    - dismissible (boolean) default: false — Shows dismiss button
    - @dismiss ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted when the dismiss button is clicked

### App Shell
A multi-section app shell with a workspace selector, expandable parent sections with sub-items, and a footer with a documentation link plus a user menu. Inbox carries a quiet count Badge, as the inbox of a platform does. When collapsed (icon mode), hovering a parent icon opens a Popover flyout listing the section&apos;s items so users keep one-click access without expanding the rail.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider"
  v-model:open="open"
  v-slot="sidebar"
  :class="['h-full console-demo', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]"
  collapsible="icon"
>
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">Console</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>

      <SidebarContent class="console-content">
        <!-- Workspace selector -->
        <SidebarGroup class="console-group">
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem>
                <Menu>
                  <MenuTrigger>
                    <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button" tooltip="Default">
                      <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><Cube /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                      <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Default</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                      <ChevronUpDown class="ml-auto console-workspace-chevron" style="width: 14px; height: 14px" />
                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                  </MenuTrigger>
                  <MenuContent :min-width="200" align="start">
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Default</MenuItem>
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Production</MenuItem>
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Staging</MenuItem>
                    <MenuSeparator />
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Plus /> New workspace</MenuItem>
                  </MenuContent>
                </Menu>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>

        <SidebarGroup class="console-group">
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <template v-for="section in consoleSections" :key="section.id">
                <!-- Collapsed rail: hovering a parent opens a flyout with its items -->
                <SidebarMenuItem v-if="section.items && sidebar.state === 'collapsed' && !sidebar.isMobile">
                  <Popover show-on-hover :hover-open-delay="80">
                    <PopoverTrigger>
                      <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button">
                        <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                        <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                        <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="section.badge">
                          <Badge size="sm" color="info">{{ section.badge }}</Badge>
                        </SidebarMenuBadge>
                        <SidebarMenuChevron class="ml-auto transition-transform" />
                      </SidebarMenuButton>
                    </PopoverTrigger>
                    <PopoverContent side="right" :side-offset="8" :min-width="220" align="start" :auto-focus="false">
                      <div class="p-1">
                        <div class="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-tertiary">{{ section.label }}</div>
                        <button
                          v-for="item in section.items"
                          :key="item.id"
                          type="button"
                          :data-active="active === item.id ? 'true' : undefined"
                          class="flex items-center w-full text-left rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-primary cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 appearance-none select-none transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--sidebar-accent)] data-[active=true]:bg-[var(--sidebar-accent)] data-[active=true]:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--color-ring)]"
                          @click="active = item.id"
                        >{{ item.label }}</button>
                      </div>
                    </PopoverContent>
                  </Popover>
                </SidebarMenuItem>

                <!-- Expanded: the parent toggles its sub items; a row without items opens its page -->
                <SidebarMenuItem v-else :expanded="!!expanded[section.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuButton
                    class="console-menu-button"
                    :is-active="!section.items && active === section.id"
                    :tooltip="section.items ? undefined : section.label"
                    @click="section.items ? (expanded[section.id] = !expanded[section.id]) : (active = section.id)"
                  >
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="section.badge">
                      <Badge size="sm" color="info">{{ section.badge }}</Badge>
                    </SidebarMenuBadge>
                    <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="section.count">
                      <Badge size="sm" color="secondary">{{ section.count }}</Badge>
                    </SidebarMenuBadge>
                    <SidebarMenuChevron
                      v-if="section.items"
                      class="ml-auto transition-transform"
                      :style="{ transform: expanded[section.id] ? 'rotate(90deg)' : undefined }"
                    />
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                  <SidebarMenuSub v-if="section.items" :open="!!expanded[section.id]" has-icons>
                    <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="item in section.items" :key="item.id">
                      <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active === item.id" @click="active = item.id">{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                    </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </template>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>

      <SidebarFooter class="console-footer">
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button" tooltip="Documentation">
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><Book /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Documentation</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
          <SidebarSeparator class="console-footer-separator" />
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <Menu>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button console-account-button" tooltip="Alex · Acme Inc">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <Avatar name="Alex" :size="24" color="secondary" variant="solid" />
                  </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    <span class="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                      <span class="font-medium text-primary text-sm">Alex</span>
                      <span class="text-tertiary text-xs">Admin</span>
                    </span>
                  </SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <ChevronDownMd class="ml-auto" style="width: 14px; height: 14px" />
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent :min-width="260" align="start" side="top">
                <MenuItem disabled>
                  <span class="text-tertiary text-sm">alex@example.com</span>
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
                  <BuildingWorkspace /> Acme Inc
                  <span class="ml-auto text-xs text-tertiary">API plan</span>
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><SettingsCog /> Organization settings</MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Chat /> Feedback</MenuItem>
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Help /> Get help</MenuItem>
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
                  <InfoCircle /> Legal center
                  <ChevronRightMd class="ml-auto" />
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Logout /> Log out</MenuItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarFooter>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="consoleSections.flatMap((s) => [s, ...(s.items ?? [])]).find((i) => i.id === active)?.label ?? 'Workbench'">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          Hover the icons when collapsed to reveal a flyout with the section's items.
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### App
The sidebar of a product: 260px, pill rows on a grey ground and a page with no inset. Rows keep their place as it folds, the labels fade, and along the rail the next tooltip opens at once. A Badge in SidebarMenuBadge sits as far from the row's edge as from its top and bottom, and its digits end where plain counts and the chevron end; in the folded rail it becomes a dot of its color above the icon, while plain text such as a count fades. A group lists its items in a Menu beside the icon when folded. The person row in the footer is 40px tall, so the round end of its highlight is concentric with the avatar on the icons' axis. In these examples, the phone toolbar opens the same navigation tree in a full-screen drawer. Set --sidebar-menu-sub-max-height to a group's real height for an even fold.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" v-model:open="open" v-slot="sidebar" class="h-full" collapsible="icon" variant="app">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">App</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem v-for="row in appTopRows" :key="row.id">
            <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="active === row.id" :tooltip="row.label" @click="active = row.id">
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="row.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ row.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.alert">
                <Badge color="danger" variant="solid" pill>{{ row.alert }}</Badge>
              </SidebarMenuBadge>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.count">{{ row.count }}</SidebarMenuBadge>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>

          <!-- Collapsed rail: the group's items in a menu beside its icon -->
          <SidebarMenuItem v-if="sidebar.state === 'collapsed' && !sidebar.isMobile">
            <Menu>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="appDocumentItems.some((item) => item.id === active)" aria-label="Documents">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><FileDocument /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Documents</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent side="right" align="start" :min-width="220">
                <MenuCheckboxItem
                  v-for="item in appDocumentItems"
                  :key="item.id"
                  :checked="item.id === active"
                  indicator-position="start"
                  indicator-variant="ghost"
                  @select="active = item.id"
                >
                  {{ item.label }}
                  <span class="ml-auto text-xs text-secondary tabular-nums">{{ item.count }}</span>
                </MenuCheckboxItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>

          <!-- Expanded: the group opens in place, each item with its count -->
          <SidebarMenuItem v-else :expanded="documentsOpen">
            <SidebarMenuButton
              :is-active="!documentsOpen && appDocumentItems.some((item) => item.id === active)"
              :aria-expanded="documentsOpen"
              @click="documentsOpen = !documentsOpen"
            >
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><FileDocument /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Documents</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
              <SidebarMenuChevron />
            </SidebarMenuButton>
            <SidebarMenuSub :open="documentsOpen" has-icons>
              <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="item in appDocumentItems" :key="item.id">
                <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active === item.id" @click="active = item.id">
                  {{ item.label }}
                  <SidebarMenuBadge>{{ item.count }}</SidebarMenuBadge>
                </SidebarMenuSubButton>
              </SidebarMenuSubItem>
            </SidebarMenuSub>
          </SidebarMenuItem>

          <SidebarMenuItem v-for="row in appBottomRows" :key="row.id">
            <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="active === row.id" :tooltip="row.label" @click="active = row.id">
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="row.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ row.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.alert">
                <Badge color="danger" variant="solid" pill>{{ row.alert }}</Badge>
              </SidebarMenuBadge>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.count">{{ row.count }}</SidebarMenuBadge>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarContent>

      <SidebarFooter>
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <Menu>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <SidebarMenuButton tooltip="Sam Lee">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><Avatar name="Sam Lee" :size="24" color="secondary" variant="solid" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Sam Lee</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent side="top" align="start" :min-width="220">
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Members /> Account</MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Logout /> Sign out</MenuItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarFooter>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="appLabels.find((item) => item.id === active)?.label">
          <p class="text-secondary">Fold the sidebar: the Notifications badge turns into a dot above its icon, the counts fade.</p>
        </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Basic
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider"
  :key="collapsible + '-' + icons"
  v-slot="sidebar"
  :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]"
  :collapsible="icons ? collapsible : 'none'"
>
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">App</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="group in navGroups" :key="group.label">
          <SidebarGroupLabel :size="labelSize">{{ group.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in group.items" :key="item.label">
                <SidebarMenuButton
                  :is-active="activeItem === item.label"
                  :tooltip="item.label"
                  @click="activeItem = item.label"
                >
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon v-if="icons"><component :is="item.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="badges && item.badge">
                    <Badge size="sm" color="info">{{ item.badge }}</Badge>
                  </SidebarMenuBadge>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
      <SidebarFooter v-if="footerCard">
        <SidebarCard dismissible>
          <SidebarCardTitleLink href="#" @click.prevent>Upgrade to Pro</SidebarCardTitleLink>
          <SidebarCardContent>
            Unlock higher rate limits, priority support, and advanced features.
          </SidebarCardContent>
          <SidebarCardFooter>
            <Button size="sm" pill color="primary">View Plans</Button>
          </SidebarCardFooter>
        </SidebarCard>
      </SidebarFooter>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="activeItem">
        <p class="text-secondary">Main content area.</p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Collapsible Sections
Build collapsible nested menus with chevron toggles for documentation and API reference sidebars. A parent is a SidebarMenuItem whose SidebarMenuSub holds the next level; expanded on the item turns its SidebarMenuChevron and open on the submenu shows it. Each level of the tree is one v-for.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" :key="icons ? 'icons' : 'no-icons'" class="h-full" collapsible="none">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" :variant="icons ? undefined : 'docs'">
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-if="icons">
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="section in iconSections" :key="section.id" :expanded="!!expanded[section.id]">
                <SidebarMenuButton @click="expanded[section.id] = !expanded[section.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <SidebarMenuChevron />
                </SidebarMenuButton>
                <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[section.id]" has-icons>
                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="child in section.items" :key="child.id">
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="appPage.id === child.id" @click="appPage = child">{{ child.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                </SidebarMenuSub>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
        <template v-else>
          <SidebarGroup v-for="section in docsSections" :key="section.id">
            <SidebarGroupLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
            <SidebarGroupContent>
              <SidebarMenu>
                <template v-for="a in section.items" :key="a.id">
                  <SidebarMenuItem v-if="a.items" :expanded="!!expanded[a.id]">
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" @click="expanded[a.id] = !expanded[a.id]">
                      {{ a.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                    </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                    <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[a.id]">
                      <template v-for="b in a.items" :key="b.id">
                        <SidebarMenuItem v-if="b.items" :expanded="!!expanded[b.id]">
                          <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" @click="expanded[b.id] = !expanded[b.id]">
                            {{ b.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                          </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                          <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[b.id]">
                            <template v-for="c in b.items" :key="c.id">
                              <SidebarMenuItem v-if="c.items" :expanded="!!expanded[c.id]">
                                <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" @click="expanded[c.id] = !expanded[c.id]">
                                  {{ c.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                                </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                                <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[c.id]">
                                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="d in c.items" :key="d.id">
                                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="3" :is-active="docsPage.id === d.id" @click="docsPage = d">{{ d.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                                </SidebarMenuSub>
                              </SidebarMenuItem>
                              <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                                <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" :is-active="docsPage.id === c.id" @click="docsPage = c">{{ c.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                              </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                            </template>
                          </SidebarMenuSub>
                        </SidebarMenuItem>
                        <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                          <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="docsPage.id === b.id" @click="docsPage = b">{{ b.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                        </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                      </template>
                    </SidebarMenuSub>
                  </SidebarMenuItem>
                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" :is-active="docsPage.id === a.id" @click="docsPage = a">{{ a.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                </template>
              </SidebarMenu>
            </SidebarGroupContent>
          </SidebarGroup>
        </template>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="(icons ? appPage : docsPage).label">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          Documentation page for {{ (icons ? appPage : docsPage).label }}. This is where the content would be displayed.
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Search and Filter
Combine search and type-based filtering with nested tree navigation. Each item has an icon based on its type. A Menu with checkbox items filters items by type; filterWorkflowTree and flattenByType are plain functions in the setup scope. Create a unique treeId with Vue useId() to connect each disclosure to its sublist.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]" collapsible="none">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 w-full [&>div:first-child>div]:!p-0" style="padding: 16px 0 8px">
          <div class="flex-1">
            <SidebarInput
              v-model:value="search"
              size="md"
              :pill="false"
              variant="soft"
              placeholder="Search"
              @clear="search = ''"
            />
          </div>
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger>
              <Button variant="soft" size="md" color="secondary" :pill="false" aria-label="Filter by type" uniform>
                <FilterBadge v-if="typeFilters.length > 0" />
                <Filter v-else />
              </Button>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
              <MenuCheckboxItem
                v-for="option in stepFilterOptions"
                :key="option.value"
                :checked="typeFilters.includes(option.value)"
                indicator-position="end"
                indicator-variant="ghost"
                @update:checked="(checked) => (typeFilters = checked ? [...typeFilters, option.value] : typeFilters.filter((v) => v !== option.value))"
                @select="(event) => event.preventDefault()"
              >{{ option.label }}</MenuCheckboxItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </div>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <!-- Filtered by type: a flat list -->
            <SidebarMenu v-if="typeFilters.length > 0">
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="step in flattenByType(workflowSteps, typeFilters, search)" :key="step.id">
                <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="activeStep === step.id" @click="activeStep = step.id">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                    <component :is="stepTypes[step.type].icon" />
                  </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ step.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>

            <!-- Otherwise the tree, narrowed by the search -->
            <SidebarMenu v-else>
              <template v-for="step in filterWorkflowTree(workflowSteps, search)" :key="step.id">
                <SidebarMenuItem v-if="step.children" :expanded="!!expanded[step.id]">
                  <div class="flex items-center">
                  <SidebarMenuButton
                    class="!w-auto min-w-0 flex-1"
                    :aria-expanded="expand === 'row' ? !!expanded[step.id] : undefined"
                    :aria-controls="expand === 'row' ? treeId + '-' + step.id : undefined"
                    :is-active="activeStep === step.id"
                    @click="expand === 'chevron' ? (activeStep = step.id) : (expanded[step.id] = !expanded[step.id])"
                  >
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                      <component :is="stepTypes[step.type].icon" />
                    </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ step.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    <SidebarMenuChevron v-if="expand === 'row'" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                  <Button v-if="expand === 'chevron'" variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                    class="!size-[26px] max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                    :aria-label="(expanded[step.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + step.label"
                    :aria-expanded="!!expanded[step.id]" :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + step.id"
                    @click="expanded[step.id] = !expanded[step.id]">
                    <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[step.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                  </Button>
                  </div>
                  <SidebarMenuSub :id="treeId + '-' + step.id" :open="!!expanded[step.id]" has-icons>
                    <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="child in step.children" :key="child.id">
                      <SidebarMenuSubButton
                        class="gap-2"
                        :indent="1"
                        :is-active="activeStep === child.id"
                        @click="activeStep = child.id"
                      >
                        <span
                          class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"
                          style="width: 16px; height: 16px; flex-shrink: 0"
                        >
                          <component :is="stepTypes[child.type].icon" />
                        </span>{{ child.label }}
                      </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                    </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuItem v-else>
                  <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="activeStep === step.id" @click="activeStep = step.id">
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                      <component :is="stepTypes[step.type].icon" />
                    </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ step.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </template>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="findStepLabel(activeStep) || 'Start'">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          {{ typeFilters.length > 0 ? 'Filtered results shown as a flat list.' : 'Use the search and filter controls to narrow the navigation tree.' }}
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Tree with Hover Actions
Tree navigation with chevrons on the left and actions on the right. Clicking a row selects the item and its chevron toggles expand/collapse. On hover, a row shows a + menu (top-level rows and nested groups) and nested rows show a drag handle for reordering. The actions sit next to the row button, not inside it, so no button is nested in another.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// Local responsive helpers from the Responsive composition example below.
import { ResponsiveSidebarProvider, ResponsiveSidebar, SidebarDemoPage } from "./responsive-sidebar"
import { computed, ref, useId, watch, type Component } from "vue"
import { Button } from "@plexui/vue/components/Button"
import { Menu, MenuCheckboxItem, MenuContent, MenuItem, MenuSeparator, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"
import {
  Sidebar,
  SidebarContent,
  SidebarGroup,
  SidebarGroupContent,
  SidebarHeader,
  SidebarInput,
  SidebarInset,
  SidebarLayout,
  SidebarMenu,
  SidebarMenuButton,
  SidebarMenuButtonIcon,
  SidebarMenuButtonLabel,
  SidebarMenuItem,
  SidebarMenuSub,
  SidebarMenuSubButton,
  SidebarMenuSubItem,
  SidebarProvider,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/Sidebar"
import {
  // Row types
  Abc, AllProductsExplore, Bolt, ButtonMousePointer, Calendar, CheckboxChecked, CheckCheck, CheckMd,
  Clip, Commit, Desktop, Globe, GroupCheck, GroupCheckCircle, ImageSquare, LinkExternal, MapPin,
  Number123, PanelBottom, Phone, SpacingVertical, SquareAsterisk, SquareDashed, TextAlignStart,
  TextInitial, TextSelect,
  // Header and row actions
  ChevronDownMd, Filter, FilterBadge, GripVertical, ListChevronsDownUp, Plus,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/Icon"

type NodeType =
  | "action"
  | "condition"
  | "module"
  | "screen"
  | "container"
  | "footer"
  | "button"
  | "image"
  | "text"
  | "title"
  | "spacer"
  | "address-input"
  | "checkbox"
  | "checkbox-group"
  | "country-select"
  | "date"
  | "file-upload"
  | "masked-text"
  | "multi-select"
  | "number-input"
  | "phone-number"
  | "radio-group"
  | "select"
  | "text-area"
  | "text-input"
  | "url-input"

type ActionNode = { id: string; label: string; type: NodeType; children?: ActionNode[] }

const nodeTypes: Record<NodeType, { icon: Component; label: string }> = {
  action: { icon: Bolt, label: "Action" },
  condition: { icon: Commit, label: "Condition" },
  module: { icon: AllProductsExplore, label: "Module" },
  screen: { icon: Desktop, label: "Screen" },
  container: { icon: SquareDashed, label: "Container" },
  footer: { icon: PanelBottom, label: "Footer" },
  button: { icon: ButtonMousePointer, label: "Button" },
  image: { icon: ImageSquare, label: "Image" },
  text: { icon: TextAlignStart, label: "Text" },
  title: { icon: TextInitial, label: "Title" },
  spacer: { icon: SpacingVertical, label: "Spacer" },
  "address-input": { icon: MapPin, label: "Address input" },
  checkbox: { icon: CheckboxChecked, label: "Checkbox" },
  "checkbox-group": { icon: GroupCheck, label: "Checkbox group" },
  "country-select": { icon: Globe, label: "Country select" },
  date: { icon: Calendar, label: "Date" },
  "file-upload": { icon: Clip, label: "File upload" },
  "masked-text": { icon: SquareAsterisk, label: "Masked text" },
  "multi-select": { icon: CheckCheck, label: "Multi select" },
  "number-input": { icon: Number123, label: "Number input" },
  "phone-number": { icon: Phone, label: "Phone number" },
  "radio-group": { icon: GroupCheckCircle, label: "Radio group" },
  select: { icon: CheckMd, label: "Select" },
  "text-area": { icon: TextSelect, label: "Text area" },
  "text-input": { icon: Abc, label: "Text input" },
  "url-input": { icon: LinkExternal, label: "URL input" },
}

const filterOptions = (["action", "condition", "module", "screen", "container", "footer"] as NodeType[]).map((type) => ({
  value: type,
  label: nodeTypes[type].label,
}))

const elementTypes: NodeType[] = ["button", "image", "text", "title", "spacer"]
const formTypes: NodeType[] = [
  "address-input", "checkbox", "checkbox-group", "country-select", "date",
  "file-upload", "masked-text", "multi-select", "number-input", "phone-number",
  "radio-group", "select", "text-area", "text-input", "url-input",
]

/** The + menu: a screen adds a container or a footer, any other row adds elements */
const addMenu = (node: ActionNode): NodeType[][] =>
  node.type === "screen" ? [["container", "footer"]] : [elementTypes, formTypes]

const treeId = useId()

// Demo controls
const action = ref<"collapse" | "filter">("collapse")
const bordered = ref(true)

const steps = ref<ActionNode[]>([
  { id: "action", label: "Action", type: "action" },
  { id: "condition", label: "Condition", type: "condition" },
  {
    id: "screen", label: "Screen", type: "screen",
    children: [
      {
        id: "container-elements", label: "Container 1", type: "container",
        children: [
          { id: "el-button", label: "Button", type: "button" },
          { id: "el-image", label: "Image", type: "image" },
          { id: "el-text", label: "Text", type: "text" },
          { id: "el-title", label: "Title", type: "title" },
          { id: "el-spacer", label: "Spacer", type: "spacer" },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: "container-forms", label: "Container 2", type: "container",
        children: [
          { id: "fm-address", label: "Address input", type: "address-input" },
          { id: "fm-checkbox", label: "Checkbox", type: "checkbox" },
          { id: "fm-checkgroup", label: "Checkbox group", type: "checkbox-group" },
          { id: "fm-country", label: "Country select", type: "country-select" },
          { id: "fm-date", label: "Date", type: "date" },
          { id: "fm-file", label: "File upload", type: "file-upload" },
          { id: "fm-masked", label: "Masked text", type: "masked-text" },
          { id: "fm-multi", label: "Multi select", type: "multi-select" },
          { id: "fm-number", label: "Number input", type: "number-input" },
          { id: "fm-phone", label: "Phone number", type: "phone-number" },
          { id: "fm-radio", label: "Radio group", type: "radio-group" },
          { id: "fm-select", label: "Select", type: "select" },
          { id: "fm-textarea", label: "Text area", type: "text-area" },
          { id: "fm-textinput", label: "Text input", type: "text-input" },
          { id: "fm-url", label: "URL input", type: "url-input" },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: "footer", label: "Footer", type: "footer",
        children: [
          { id: "ft-text", label: "Text", type: "text" },
          { id: "ft-button", label: "Button", type: "button" },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "module", label: "Module", type: "module",
    children: [
      { id: "mod-screen-1", label: "Screen 1", type: "screen" },
      { id: "mod-screen-2", label: "Screen 2", type: "screen" },
      { id: "mod-screen-3", label: "Screen 3", type: "screen" },
    ],
  },
])

const search = ref("")
const typeFilters = ref<NodeType[]>([])
const activeItem = ref("screen")

const groupIds = ["screen", "container-elements", "container-forms", "footer", "module"]
const expandAll = (): Record<string, boolean> => Object.fromEntries(groupIds.map((id) => [id, true]))
const expanded = ref<Record<string, boolean>>(expandAll())

function toggle(id: string) {
  expanded.value[id] = !expanded.value[id]
}

// Collapse mode: one button folds every group, or opens them all again
const hasAnyExpanded = computed(() => groupIds.some((id) => expanded.value[id]))
function toggleCollapseAll() {
  expanded.value = hasAnyExpanded.value ? {} : expandAll()
}

// Filter mode: a search or a type filter lists the matching rows flat
const isFlattening = computed(
  () => action.value === "filter" && (typeFilters.value.length > 0 || search.value.length > 0),
)
const visibleSteps = computed(() =>
  isFlattening.value
    ? flattenNodes(steps.value, typeFilters.value, search.value)
    : filterTree(steps.value, search.value),
)
const activeLabel = computed(() => findLabel(steps.value, activeItem.value) || "Screen")

// Switching modes clears the search and the type filters
watch(action, (mode) => {
  typeFilters.value = []
  search.value = ""
  if (mode === "collapse") expanded.value = expandAll()
})

/** The tree, keeping the parents of matching rows */
function filterTree(items: ActionNode[], query: string): ActionNode[] {
  if (!query) return items
  return items.reduce<ActionNode[]>((acc, item) => {
    const match = item.label.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
    const children = item.children ? filterTree(item.children, query) : undefined
    const childMatch = !!children && children.length > 0
    if (match || childMatch) acc.push(childMatch ? { ...item, children } : item)
    return acc
  }, [])
}

/** Every row of the checked types (all types when none is) matching the search, as a flat list */
function flattenNodes(items: ActionNode[], types: NodeType[], query: string): ActionNode[] {
  const result: ActionNode[] = []
  const collect = (list: ActionNode[]) => {
    for (const item of list) {
      const matchType = types.length === 0 || types.includes(item.type)
      const matchSearch = !query || item.label.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
      if (matchType && matchSearch) result.push(item)
      if (item.children) collect(item.children)
    }
  }
  collect(items)
  return result
}

/** The label of a row anywhere in the tree */
function findLabel(items: ActionNode[], id: string): string {
  for (const item of items) {
    if (item.id === id) return item.label
    if (item.children) {
      const found = findLabel(item.children, id)
      if (found) return found
    }
  }
  return ""
}

// Drag and drop (native HTML5): pressing a row's grip makes the row
// draggable; it drops on the rows of its own list and takes their place.

/** Containers in a screen (not its Footer) and elements reorder; a module's screens do not */
const isSortable = (parent: ActionNode, node: ActionNode) => parent.type !== "module" && node.type !== "footer"

const grabbed = ref<string | null>(null) // the row whose grip is pressed
const dragId = ref<string | null>(null)
const overId = ref<string | null>(null) // the drop target, drawn hovered
let dropIds: string[] = []

/** Moves `activeId` to the place of `targetId` in the list holding both; the Footer never moves */
function reorderTree(items: ActionNode[], activeId: string, targetId: string): ActionNode[] {
  return items.map((item) => {
    if (!item.children) return item
    const from = item.children.findIndex((child) => child.id === activeId)
    const to = item.children.findIndex((child) => child.id === targetId)
    if (from !== -1 && to !== -1) {
      if (item.children[from].type === "footer" || item.children[to].type === "footer") return item
      const children = [...item.children]
      children.splice(to, 0, ...children.splice(from, 1))
      return { ...item, children }
    }
    return { ...item, children: reorderTree(item.children, activeId, targetId) }
  })
}

function startDrag(event: DragEvent, parent: ActionNode, node: ActionNode) {
  dragId.value = node.id
  dropIds = (parent.children ?? []).filter((sibling) => isSortable(parent, sibling)).map((sibling) => sibling.id)
  if (event.dataTransfer) {
    event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "move"
    event.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", node.id)
  }
}

function dragOver(event: DragEvent, id: string) {
  if (!dragId.value || !dropIds.includes(id)) return
  event.preventDefault() // accepts the drop
  overId.value = id === dragId.value ? null : id
}

/** Anywhere else in the sidebar there is no drop target */
function clearDropTarget(event: DragEvent) {
  if (!event.defaultPrevented) overId.value = null
}

function endDrag() {
  grabbed.value = null
  dragId.value = null
  overId.value = null
  dropIds = []
}

function dropOn(event: DragEvent, id: string) {
  if (!dragId.value || !dropIds.includes(id)) return
  event.preventDefault()
  if (id !== dragId.value) steps.value = reorderTree(steps.value, dragId.value, id)
  endDrag()
}

/** Indent each level; leaf rows reserve the disclosure column without adding a control. */
const rowStyle = (depth: number, node: ActionNode) => ({
  paddingLeft: "calc(" + (4 + depth * 24) + "px + " + (node.children ? "0px" : "var(--disclosure-width)") + ")",
})
</script>

<template>
  <component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]" collapsible="none">
    <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
      <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" class="!py-4">
        <SidebarHeader>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 w-full [&>div:first-child>div]:!p-0">
            <div class="flex-1">
              <SidebarInput
                v-model:value="search"
                size="md"
                :pill="false"
                variant="soft"
                placeholder="Search..."
                @clear="search = ''"
              />
            </div>
            <!-- collapse: folds every group, or opens them all again -->
            <Button
              v-if="action === 'collapse'"
              variant="soft"
              size="md"
              color="secondary"
              :pill="false"
              uniform
              aria-label="Collapse all"
              :aria-pressed="!hasAnyExpanded"
              @click="toggleCollapseAll"
            >
              <ListChevronsDownUp />
            </Button>
            <!-- filter: lists the rows of the checked types -->
            <Menu v-else>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <Button variant="soft" size="md" color="secondary" :pill="false" aria-label="Filter by type" uniform>
                  <FilterBadge v-if="typeFilters.length > 0" />
                  <Filter v-else />
                </Button>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                <MenuCheckboxItem
                  v-for="option in filterOptions"
                  :key="option.value"
                  :checked="typeFilters.includes(option.value)"
                  indicator-position="end"
                  indicator-variant="ghost"
                  @update:checked="(checked) => (typeFilters = checked ? [...typeFilters, option.value] : typeFilters.filter((v) => v !== option.value))"
                  @select="(event) => event.preventDefault()"
                >{{ option.label }}</MenuCheckboxItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </div>
        </SidebarHeader>
        <SidebarContent @dragover="clearDropTarget">
          <SidebarGroup>
            <SidebarGroupContent>
              <!-- Filter mode, searching or filtering by type: a flat list -->
              <SidebarMenu v-if="isFlattening">
                <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in visibleSteps" :key="item.id">
                  <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="activeItem === item.id" @click="activeItem = item.id">
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                      <component :is="nodeTypes[item.type].icon" />
                    </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </SidebarMenu>

              <!-- Otherwise the tree, narrowed by the search -->
              <SidebarMenu v-else>
                <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in visibleSteps" :key="item.id" :expanded="!!expanded[item.id]">
                  <div class="group/atp relative flex items-center pl-1">
                    <Button
                      v-if="item.children"
                      variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                      class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                      :aria-label="(expanded[item.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + item.label"
                      :aria-expanded="!!expanded[item.id]"
                      :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + item.id"
                      @click="toggle(item.id)"
                    >
                      <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[item.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                    </Button>
                    <span v-else class="w-6 max-md:w-11 shrink-0" />
                    <SidebarMenuButton
                      :is-active="activeItem === item.id"
                      class="!w-auto min-w-0 flex-1 group-hover/atp:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] group-hover/atp:[&::before]:!opacity-50 group-hover/atp:[&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]"
                      @click="activeItem = item.id"
                    >
                      <span class="flex items-center shrink-0" style="margin-left: 4px">
                        <span
                          class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1]"
                          style="width: 24px; height: 24px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 6px"
                        >
                          <component :is="nodeTypes[item.type].icon" />
                        </span>
                      </span>
                      <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                    <span
                      class="item-actions flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5 opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover/atp:opacity-100 group-hover/atp:pointer-events-auto group-focus-within/atp:opacity-100 group-focus-within/atp:pointer-events-auto max-md:opacity-100 max-md:pointer-events-auto"
                    >
                      <Menu>
                        <MenuTrigger>
                          <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11" :aria-label="'Add to ' + item.label"><Plus /></Button>
                        </MenuTrigger>
                        <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                          <template v-for="(types, index) in addMenu(item)" :key="index">
                            <MenuSeparator v-if="index > 0" />
                            <MenuItem v-for="type in types" :key="type" @select="() => {}">
                              <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 shrink-0 [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"><component :is="nodeTypes[type].icon" /></span> {{ nodeTypes[type].label }}
                            </MenuItem>
                          </template>
                        </MenuContent>
                      </Menu>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <SidebarMenuSub :id="treeId + '-' + item.id" v-if="item.children" :open="!!expanded[item.id]" has-icons>
                    <SidebarMenuSubItem
                      v-for="child in item.children"
                      :key="child.id"
                      :style="dragId === child.id ? 'opacity: 0.7' : undefined"
                    >
                      <div
                        class="group/ati relative flex items-center [--disclosure-width:24px] max-md:[--disclosure-width:44px]"
                        :style="rowStyle(1, child)"
                        :draggable="grabbed === child.id"
                        @dragstart="startDrag($event, item, child)"
                        @dragover="dragOver($event, child.id)"
                        @drop="dropOn($event, child.id)"
                        @dragend="endDrag"
                      >
                        <Button
                          v-if="child.children"
                          variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                          class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                          :aria-label="(expanded[child.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + child.label"
                          :aria-expanded="!!expanded[child.id]"
                          :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + child.id"
                          @click="toggle(child.id)"
                        >
                          <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[child.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                        </Button>
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton
                          :indent="1"
                          :is-active="activeItem === child.id"
                          :class="[
                            'gap-0.5 !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1 !h-8 !px-1 !py-0 max-md:!h-11',
                            dragId === child.id || overId === child.id
                              ? 'text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] [&::before]:!opacity-50 [&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]'
                              : 'group-hover/ati:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] group-hover/ati:[&::before]:!opacity-50 group-hover/ati:[&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]',
                          ]"
                          @click="activeItem = child.id"
                        >
                          <span class="flex items-center shrink-0">
                            <span
                              class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1]"
                              style="width: 24px; height: 24px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 6px"
                            >
                              <component :is="nodeTypes[child.type].icon" />
                            </span>
                          </span>
                          <span class="flex-1 min-w-0 truncate text-left relative z-[1]">{{ child.label }}</span>
                        </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                        <!-- Hover actions: + on parents, the drag grip on rows that reorder -->
                        <span
                          v-if="child.children || isSortable(item, child)"
                          class="item-actions flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5"
                          :class="
                            dragId === child.id
                              ? 'opacity-100 pointer-events-auto'
                              : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover/ati:opacity-100 group-hover/ati:pointer-events-auto group-focus-within/ati:opacity-100 group-focus-within/ati:pointer-events-auto max-md:opacity-100 max-md:pointer-events-auto'
                          "
                        >
                          <Menu v-if="child.children">
                            <MenuTrigger>
                              <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11" :aria-label="'Add to ' + child.label"><Plus /></Button>
                            </MenuTrigger>
                            <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                              <template v-for="(types, index) in addMenu(child)" :key="index">
                                <MenuSeparator v-if="index > 0" />
                                <MenuItem v-for="type in types" :key="type" @select="() => {}">
                                  <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 shrink-0 [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"><component :is="nodeTypes[type].icon" /></span> {{ nodeTypes[type].label }}
                                </MenuItem>
                              </template>
                            </MenuContent>
                          </Menu>
                          <span
                            v-if="isSortable(item, child)"
                            class="inline-flex size-6 max-md:size-11 items-center justify-center shrink-0 [&>svg]:block [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1] pointer-events-auto text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)]"
                            style="border-radius: 6px; touch-action: none"
                            :style="{ cursor: dragId === child.id ? 'grabbing' : 'grab' }"
                            @pointerdown="grabbed = child.id"
                            @pointerup="grabbed = null"
                          >
                            <GripVertical />
                          </span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <SidebarMenuSub :id="treeId + '-' + child.id" v-if="child.children && dragId !== child.id" :open="!!expanded[child.id]" has-icons>
                        <SidebarMenuSubItem
                          v-for="grandchild in child.children"
                          :key="grandchild.id"
                          :style="dragId === grandchild.id ? 'opacity: 0.7' : undefined"
                        >
                          <div
                            class="group/ati relative flex items-center [--disclosure-width:24px] max-md:[--disclosure-width:44px]"
                            :style="rowStyle(2, grandchild)"
                            :draggable="grabbed === grandchild.id"
                            @dragstart="startDrag($event, child, grandchild)"
                            @dragover="dragOver($event, grandchild.id)"
                            @drop="dropOn($event, grandchild.id)"
                            @dragend="endDrag"
                          >
                            <Button
                              v-if="grandchild.children"
                              variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                              class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                              :aria-label="(expanded[grandchild.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + grandchild.label"
                              :aria-expanded="!!expanded[grandchild.id]"
                              :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + grandchild.id"
                              @click="toggle(grandchild.id)"
                            >
                              <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[grandchild.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                            </Button>
                            <SidebarMenuSubButton
                              :indent="1"
                              :is-active="activeItem === grandchild.id"
                              :class="[
                                'gap-0.5 !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1 !h-8 !px-1 !py-0 max-md:!h-11',
                                dragId === grandchild.id || overId === grandchild.id
                                  ? 'text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] [&::before]:!opacity-50 [&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]'
                                  : 'group-hover/ati:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] group-hover/ati:[&::before]:!opacity-50 group-hover/ati:[&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]',
                              ]"
                              @click="activeItem = grandchild.id"
                            >
                              <span class="flex items-center shrink-0">
                                <span
                                  class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1]"
                                  style="width: 24px; height: 24px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 6px"
                                >
                                  <component :is="nodeTypes[grandchild.type].icon" />
                                </span>
                              </span>
                              <span class="flex-1 min-w-0 truncate text-left relative z-[1]">{{ grandchild.label }}</span>
                            </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                            <!-- Hover actions: + on parents, the drag grip on rows that reorder -->
                            <span
                              v-if="grandchild.children || isSortable(child, grandchild)"
                              class="item-actions flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5"
                              :class="
                                dragId === grandchild.id
                                  ? 'opacity-100 pointer-events-auto'
                                  : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover/ati:opacity-100 group-hover/ati:pointer-events-auto group-focus-within/ati:opacity-100 group-focus-within/ati:pointer-events-auto max-md:opacity-100 max-md:pointer-events-auto'
                              "
                            >
                              <Menu v-if="grandchild.children">
                                <MenuTrigger>
                                  <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11" :aria-label="'Add to ' + grandchild.label"><Plus /></Button>
                                </MenuTrigger>
                                <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                                  <template v-for="(types, index) in addMenu(grandchild)" :key="index">
                                    <MenuSeparator v-if="index > 0" />
                                    <MenuItem v-for="type in types" :key="type" @select="() => {}">
                                      <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 shrink-0 [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"><component :is="nodeTypes[type].icon" /></span> {{ nodeTypes[type].label }}
                                    </MenuItem>
                                  </template>
                                </MenuContent>
                              </Menu>
                              <span
                                v-if="isSortable(child, grandchild)"
                                class="inline-flex size-6 max-md:size-11 items-center justify-center shrink-0 [&>svg]:block [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1] pointer-events-auto text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)]"
                                style="border-radius: 6px; touch-action: none"
                                :style="{ cursor: dragId === grandchild.id ? 'grabbing' : 'grab' }"
                                @pointerdown="grabbed = grandchild.id"
                                @pointerup="grabbed = null"
                              >
                                <GripVertical />
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                      </SidebarMenuSub>
                    </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </SidebarMenu>
            </SidebarGroupContent>
          </SidebarGroup>
        </SidebarContent>
      </component>

      <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="activeLabel">
          <p class="text-secondary">
            {{ isFlattening ? 'Filtered results shown as a flat list.' : 'Screen → Container / Footer → Elements. Containers and elements are drag-reorderable; Footer stays pinned.' }}
          </p>
        </component>
    </SidebarLayout>
  </component>
</template>
```

### Mobile Menu
Use Phone preview, then open the menu in the page toolbar. The same navigation tree renders in a desktop rail or a full-screen mobile drawer. Selecting a leaf closes the drawer; expanding a group keeps it open.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider"
  v-slot="sidebar"
  :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]"
  :collapsible="icons ? 'icon' : 'none'"
>
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" v-if="!nested">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">App</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="group in navGroups" :key="group.label">
          <SidebarGroupLabel size="sm">{{ group.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in group.items" :key="item.label">
                <SidebarMenuButton
                  :is-active="active.label === item.label"
                  :tooltip="item.label"
                  @click="active = { id: item.label.toLowerCase(), label: item.label }; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)"
                >
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon v-if="icons"><component :is="item.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" v-else-if="icons" style="width: 280px">
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="section in iconSections" :key="section.id" :expanded="!!expanded[section.id]">
                <SidebarMenuButton @click="expanded[section.id] = !expanded[section.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <SidebarMenuChevron />
                </SidebarMenuButton>
                <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[section.id]" has-icons>
                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="child in section.items" :key="child.id">
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active.id === child.id" @click="active = child; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ child.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                </SidebarMenuSub>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" v-else variant="docs" style="width: 280px">
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="section in docsSections" :key="section.id">
          <SidebarGroupLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <template v-for="a in section.items" :key="a.id">
                <SidebarMenuItem v-if="a.items" :expanded="!!expanded[a.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" @click="expanded[a.id] = !expanded[a.id]">
                    {{ a.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                  </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[a.id]">
                    <template v-for="b in a.items" :key="b.id">
                      <SidebarMenuItem v-if="b.items" :expanded="!!expanded[b.id]">
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" @click="expanded[b.id] = !expanded[b.id]">
                          {{ b.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                        </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                        <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[b.id]">
                          <template v-for="c in b.items" :key="c.id">
                            <SidebarMenuItem v-if="c.items" :expanded="!!expanded[c.id]">
                              <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" @click="expanded[c.id] = !expanded[c.id]">
                                {{ c.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                              </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                              <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[c.id]">
                                <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="d in c.items" :key="d.id">
                                  <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="3" :is-active="active.id === d.id" @click="active = d; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ d.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                                </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                              </SidebarMenuSub>
                            </SidebarMenuItem>
                            <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                              <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" :is-active="active.id === c.id" @click="active = c; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ c.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                            </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                          </template>
                        </SidebarMenuSub>
                      </SidebarMenuItem>
                      <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active.id === b.id" @click="active = b; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ b.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                      </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                    </template>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                  <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" :is-active="active.id === a.id" @click="active = a; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ a.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                </SidebarMenuSubItem>
              </template>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="active.label">
      <p class="text-secondary">Open the menu to choose a different page.</p>
    </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Search Input
The sidebar provides a dedicated SidebarInput component for search functionality. It forwards everything to Input, so bind it with v-model:value and clear it with @clear.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" class="h-full" collapsible="none">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" variant="docs">
      <SidebarHeader>
        <div style="padding: 12px 0 8px; width: 100%">
          <SidebarInput v-model:value="searchValue" variant="soft" placeholder="Search" @clear="searchValue = ''" />
        </div>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="section in searchSections" :key="section.label">
          <SidebarGroupLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in section.items" :key="section.label + '-' + item">
                <SidebarMenuSubButton
                  :indent="0"
```
