```vue
                  :is-active="activeItem === section.label + '-' + item"
                  @click="activeItem = section.label + '-' + item"
                >{{ item }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="activeItem">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          Search input stays fixed at the top while sidebar content scrolls.
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

## Skeleton
Loading placeholders that match the shape of the content they stand in for, from avatars and text to cards, forms, and tables.
Import: import { Skeleton } from "@plexui/vue/components/Skeleton"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/skeleton
Props:
  - circle (boolean) default: false — Render a circular skeleton
  - class (string) — Set dimensions and custom styling

### Overview
Use a circular skeleton to represent loading avatars, combined with text placeholders.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
  <Skeleton circle class="size-12" />
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[200px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[150px]" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Card
Compose multiple skeletons to represent loading cards with images and text content.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3 w-[300px] p-4 border border-default rounded-xl">
  <Skeleton class="h-[150px] w-full rounded-lg" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-3/4" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-1/2" />
</div>
```

### Text
Multiple text lines with varying widths create natural-looking paragraph placeholders.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[300px]">
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-full" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-full" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-4/5" />
</div>
```

### Form
Form skeletons use Plex UI control sizing (32px height for inputs and buttons).
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 w-[300px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[80px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-8 w-full rounded-lg" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[100px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-8 w-full rounded-lg" />
  </div>
  <Skeleton class="h-8 w-[120px] rounded-lg" />
</div>
```

### Table
Table skeletons show header and row structure while data loads.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <div class="flex gap-4 pb-2 border-b border-default">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[100px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[150px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[80px]" />
  </div>
  <div v-for="i in 4" :key="i" class="flex gap-4 py-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[100px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[150px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[80px]" />
  </div>
</div>
```

## Slider
Slider for fine-tuning numeric values with single or range thumbs, marks, an editable value input, and vertical orientation.
Import: import { Slider } from "@plexui/vue/components/Slider"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/slider
Props:
  - value (number | number[]) — Required. Current value; an array when range is set. Bind with v-model:value
  - min (number) — Required. Minimum value
  - max (number) — Required. Maximum value
  - step (number) — Required. Step increment
  - range (boolean) default: false — Enable multi-thumb range mode
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Layout direction of the slider
  - reset-value (number) — Value for the reset option (single mode only)
  - reset-tooltip (string) default: "Reset to default" — Tooltip and accessible name of the reset button (single mode only)
  - unit (string) — Unit displayed after the value (e.g. ms, px)
  - prefix-unit (string) — Unit displayed before the value (e.g. $)
  - label (string) — Label text. Also names the slider thumbs for assistive technology
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content (e.g. a tooltip); takes precedence over the label prop
  - aria-label (string) — Accessible name when there is no visible label. Range thumbs add Minimum / Maximum (or Value n of m)
  - aria-labelledby (string) — Id of an element that names the slider; takes precedence over label
  - marks (Array<{ value: number, label: string }>) — Marks below the track (horizontal only)
  - track-color (string) — Color of the slider track
  - range-color (string) — Color of the filled part of the track
  - min-steps-between-thumbs (number) — Minimum steps between thumbs (range mode only)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the slider
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the wrapper element
  - @update:value ((value: number | number[]) => void) — Emitted when the value changes; an array when range is set; powers v-model:value
  - @change ((value: number | number[]) => void) — Emitted with update:value, named after the React onChange prop
  - @focus ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the value input or a thumb gains focus
  - @blur ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the value input or a thumb loses focus

### Overview
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { Slider } from '@plexui/vue/components/Slider';

const value = ref(1000);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
<div style="width: 300px">
  <Slider
    label="Example field"
    :min="0"
    :max="2000"
    :reset-value="1000"
    :step="10"
    unit="ms"
    v-model:value="value"
    :disabled="disabled"
  />
</div>
</template>
```

### Adding a tooltip
A tooltip can easily be added to the Slider label by passing rich content through the label slot instead of a simple string.
```vue
<div style="width: 300px">
  <Slider :min="0" :max="2000" :reset-value="1000" :step="10" unit="ms" v-model:value="value">
    <template #label>
      <Tooltip content="More details about this field">
        <TooltipTriggerDecorator>Example field</TooltipTriggerDecorator>
      </Tooltip>
    </template>
  </Slider>
</div>
```

### Marks
Slider marks can be easily added by passing an array of values to the marks prop. Responsive behaviors are handled automatically.
```vue
<div style="width: 300px">
  <Slider
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Passing grade"
    :min="1"
    :max="5"
    :step="1"
    :marks="[
      { value: 1, label: '1' },
      { value: 2, label: '2' },
      { value: 3, label: '3' },
      { value: 4, label: '4' },
      { value: 5, label: '5' },
    ]"
    track-color="#61C454"
    range-color="#EF4146"
  />
</div>
```

### Range
Set range to enable a dual-thumb slider for selecting a value range. v-model:value then binds an array instead of a single number.
```vue
<div style="width: 300px">
  <Slider
    range
    label="Price range"
    :min="0"
    :max="100"
    :step="1"
    v-model:value="value"
    prefix-unit="$"
  />
</div>
```

### Multiple thumbs
Pass three or more values to create a multi-thumb slider. Use min-steps-between-thumbs to enforce minimum spacing between thumbs.
```vue
<div style="width: 300px">
  <Slider
    range
    label="Breakpoints"
    :min="0"
    :max="100"
    :step="1"
    v-model:value="value"
    :min-steps-between-thumbs="5"
  />
</div>
```

### Vertical
Set orientation="vertical" to render a vertical slider. Marks are not supported in vertical orientation.
```vue
<Slider
  label="Volume"
  :min="0"
  :max="100"
  :step="1"
  v-model:value="value"
  orientation="vertical"
/>
```

## Sortable
"Reorders a list by dragging: rows of a group, table rows, images, tags and cards. The order changes on the drop."
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/sortable
Props:
  - value (unknown[]) — The list, in the order of the children. Bind with v-model:value
  - layout ('list' | 'grid') default: list — list for rows one under another, grid for items that stand side by side
  - as (string | Component) default: div — Element to render, such as TableBody for table rows
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Turns dragging off
  - handles (boolean) default: true in a table, otherwise false — Shows the grips of SortableHandle. A hidden grip still takes the keyboard, and the item drags from anywhere but a control
  - group (string) — Lists of one group trade items, and a SortableTarget of that group takes them. Give each list a name
  - name (string) — Names the list in move
  - aria-label (string) default: Reorder — Name of the handle for assistive technology

### Rows
Every direct child is an item, in the order of value. A row drags from anywhere but a control, so the switch keeps working. Its grip is hidden and still takes the keyboard: Tab to the row and press the arrow keys. On a touch screen a row lifts after a short hold, and a swipe still scrolls the page. Esc cancels a drag. A wrapper with data-row keeps the lines of the group, and rows that touch hold still: the line alone marks the gap. On a phone the rows sit on the grouped background of an iOS settings screen.
```vue
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Home screen">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows">
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.label" />
        <Field row :label="row.label" :description="row.description">
          <Switch default-checked />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Rows with a handle
A row that is mostly a field needs a grip: handles shows it, and then only the grip drags the row.
```vue
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Contact details">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows" handles>
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.label" />
        <Field row :label="row.label">
          <Input variant="ghost" :default-value="row.value" />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Stacked rows
A long value under its label sorts the same way.
```vue
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Questions">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows">
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.question" />
        <Field row orientation="vertical" :label="row.question">
          <Textarea variant="ghost" :rows="1" auto-resize :default-value="row.answer" />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Different controls
The kind of control does not matter: each row keeps its state when it moves.
```vue
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Preferences">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows">
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.label" />
        <Field row :label="row.label">
          <Select
            v-if="row.id === 'language'"
            v-model:value="language"
            :options="languages"
            variant="soft"
            :block="false"
            align="end"
          />
          <SegmentedControl v-else-if="row.id === 'theme'" v-model:value="theme" aria-label="Theme">
            <SegmentedControlOption value="light">Light</SegmentedControlOption>
            <SegmentedControlOption value="dark">Dark</SegmentedControlOption>
          </SegmentedControl>
          <DatePicker
            v-else-if="row.id === 'start'"
            v-model:value="start"
            trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
            placeholder="Select date"
            variant="soft"
          />
          <Switch v-else-if="row.id === 'summary'" default-checked />
          <Input v-else variant="ghost" default-value="Weekly report" />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Table
Pass :as="TableBody" and the rows sort inside the table. Columns keep their width. Grips show here without handles.
```vue
<div class="w-[640px] max-w-full">
  <Table>
    <TableHeader>
      <TableRow>
        <TableHead class="w-10" aria-label="Order" />
        <TableHead>Product</TableHead>
        <TableHead>SKU</TableHead>
        <TableHead>Stock</TableHead>
        <TableHead class="text-end">Price</TableHead>
      </TableRow>
    </TableHeader>
    <Sortable :as="TableBody" v-model:value="rows">
      <TableRow v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id">
        <TableCell><SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.name" /></TableCell>
        <TableCell>{{ row.name }}</TableCell>
        <TableCell>{{ row.sku }}</TableCell>
        <TableCell>{{ row.stock }}</TableCell>
        <TableCell class="text-end">{{ row.price }}</TableCell>
      </TableRow>
    </Sortable>
  </Table>
</div>
```

### Images
layout="grid" for items side by side: the line stands between two items, and the chip shows the picture. With tabindex an item takes the arrow keys itself.
```vue
<Sortable v-model:value="pictures" layout="grid" class="grid w-[420px] max-w-full grid-cols-3 gap-3">
  <div
    v-for="picture in pictures"
    :key="picture.id"
    tabindex="0"
    class="aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-xl"
  >
    <img :src="picture.src" :alt="picture.alt" class="block size-full object-cover" />
  </div>
</Sortable>
```

### Tags
The grid layout also fits a line that wraps: the end of one line and the start of the next are separate places to land.
```vue
<Sortable v-model:value="tags" layout="grid" class="flex w-[420px] max-w-full flex-wrap gap-2">
  <div v-for="tag in tags" :key="tag" tabindex="0" class="rounded-full">
    <Badge color="secondary" size="lg" pill>{{ tag }}</Badge>
  </div>
</Sortable>
```

### Cards
Any element can be an item, such as a StatCard.
```vue
<Sortable v-model:value="cards" layout="grid" class="grid w-[480px] max-w-full grid-cols-2 gap-3">
  <div v-for="card in cards" :key="card.label" tabindex="0" class="rounded-xl">
    <StatCard :label="card.label" :value="card.value" />
  </div>
</Sortable>
```

### Between lists
Lists of one group trade items, and an empty list takes a drop. Each list has a name. move fires for every move, also inside one list, and the handler updates both lists. Past the end of a list the arrow keys go on to the next one. A data-sortable-static child, such as a head or a message, stays out of the items.
```vue
<div class="grid w-[640px] max-w-full gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
  <div v-for="(tasks, name) in lists" :key="name">
    <p class="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary">{{ name }}</p>
    <Sortable group="tasks" :name="name" :value="tasks" class="rounded-xl border border-default" @move="move">
      <div v-for="task in tasks" :key="task" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-primary">{{ task }}</div>
      <p v-if="!tasks.length" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-secondary" data-sortable-static>Nothing here</p>
    </Sortable>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Stat Card
Stat card for dashboards displaying KPIs with trend deltas, an info tooltip, an icon, a progress bar, and accent border styling.
Import: import { StatCard } from "@plexui/vue/components/StatCard"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/stat-card
Props:
  - label (string) — Required. Label describing the metric
  - value (string | number) — The primary metric value
  - value slot (VNode[]) — Rich value content; takes precedence over the value prop
  - description (string) — Subtitle below the value
  - trend ({ value: number; label?: string }) — Trend indicator with percentage
  - invert-trend (boolean) default: false — Invert trend colors (positive = bad)
  - trend-position ("value" | "header") default: "value" — Where the trend indicator is rendered
  - trend-variant ("text" | "badge") default: "text" — Render trend as text or badge
  - size ("default" | "sm") default: "default" — Card size; sm for compact analytics layouts
  - icon slot (VNode[]) — Icon displayed on the left with a soft background
  - sparkline slot (VNode[]) — Chart rendered at the bottom
  - progress (number) — Progress bar value (0-100)
  - progress-label (string) — Label next to progress bar
  - tooltip (string) — Shows an info icon next to the label with tooltip text
  - variant ("default" | "accent") default: "default" — Visual variant
  - accent-color ("primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "info" | "discovery" | "success" | "caution" | "warning") default: "primary" — Left border color (accent variant)
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the card element

### Overview
Dashboard-ready stat cards with trends, icons, and progress bars. Use the controls below to explore all available props.
```vue
<div style="max-width: 320px; width: 100%" class="mx-auto">
  <StatCard
    label="Total Revenue"
    value="$45,231"
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    accent-color="primary"
    :trend-position="trendPosition"
    :trend-variant="trendVariant"
    :trend="trend ? { value: 12.5, label: 'vs last month' } : undefined"
    :tooltip="tooltip ? 'Sum of all revenue streams' : undefined"
    :invert-trend="invertTrend"
  />
</div>
```

### Sizes
Use size="sm" for compact analytics rows with denser spacing and smaller typography. The small size also pairs well with the tooltip prop for info icons.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-6 w-full">
  <!-- Default size -->
  <div class="grid w-full min-w-0 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4" style="max-width: 720px">
    <StatCard label="Total Revenue" value="$45,231" :trend="{ value: 12.5 }" />
    <StatCard label="Active Users" value="2,847" :trend="{ value: 8.2 }" />
    <StatCard label="Conversion Rate" value="3.24%" :trend="{ value: -1.8 }" />
  </div>
  <!-- Small size -->
  <div class="grid w-full min-w-0 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3" style="max-width: 720px">
    <StatCard size="sm" label="Total Inquiries" value="3,544" :trend="{ value: -2.6 }" tooltip="Total inquiries in period" />
    <StatCard size="sm" label="Completion Rate" value="89.2%" :trend="{ value: 0.5 }" tooltip="Average across templates" />
    <StatCard size="sm" label="Approval Rate" value="84.6%" :trend="{ value: 1.8 }" tooltip="Average across templates" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Trend position
Control where the trend appears: next to the value (default) or right-aligned in the header row.
```vue
<div class="grid w-full min-w-0 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4" style="max-width: 480px">
  <StatCard label="Revenue" value="$45,231" :trend="{ value: 12.5 }" trend-position="value" />
  <StatCard label="Revenue" value="$45,231" :trend="{ value: 12.5 }" trend-position="header" />
</div>
```

### Trend variant
Display the trend as plain text or wrapped in a badge.
```vue
<div class="grid w-full min-w-0 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4" style="max-width: 480px">
  <StatCard label="Users" value="2,847" :trend="{ value: 8.2 }" trend-variant="text" />
  <StatCard label="Users" value="2,847" :trend="{ value: 8.2 }" trend-variant="badge" />
</div>
```

### Info tooltip
Pass a tooltip string to render an info icon next to the label with a tooltip on hover.
```vue
<div style="max-width: 320px; width: 100%; margin: 0 auto">
  <StatCard
    label="Avg Completion Rate"
    value="89.2%"
    :trend="{ value: 0.5 }"
    tooltip="Percentage of inquiries reaching final state"
  />
</div>
```

### With icon
Pass an icon through the icon slot to display it on the left side with a soft background. The layout automatically switches to a horizontal icon + content arrangement. Works with both default and sm sizes.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-6 w-full">
  <!-- Default size -->
  <div class="grid w-full min-w-0 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
    <StatCard label="Total Revenue" value="$45,231" :trend="{ value: 12.5 }">
      <template #icon><DollarCircle /></template>
    </StatCard>
    <StatCard label="Active Users" value="2,847" :trend="{ value: 8.2 }">
      <template #icon><Users /></template>
    </StatCard>
  </div>
  <!-- Small size -->
  <div class="grid w-full min-w-0 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
    <StatCard size="sm" label="Revenue" value="$45,231" :trend="{ value: 12.5 }">
      <template #icon><DollarCircle /></template>
    </StatCard>
    <StatCard size="sm" label="Users" value="2,847" :trend="{ value: 8.2 }">
      <template #icon><Users /></template>
    </StatCard>
    <StatCard size="sm" label="Orders" value="1,234" :trend="{ value: -3.1 }">
      <template #icon><ShoppingBag /></template>
    </StatCard>
  </div>
</div>
```

### With progress
Progress bars show a visual representation of completion or usage percentage.
```vue
<StatCard label="Storage Used" value="7.2 GB" :progress="72" progress-label="72%" description="of 10 GB" />
```

### Accent border
The accent variant adds a colored left border. Choose from eight semantic colors.
```vue
<div style="max-width: 320px; width: 100%" class="mx-auto">
  <StatCard
    label="Total Revenue"
    value="$45,231"
    variant="accent"
    :accent-color="accentColor"
    :trend="{ value: 12.5, label: 'vs last month' }"
  />
</div>
```

### Inverted trend
Use invert-trend for metrics where positive values are bad (e.g., error rate, response time). Toggle the switch to see the color flip.
```vue
<div style="max-width: 320px; width: 100%" class="mx-auto">
  <StatCard
    label="Avg Response Time"
    value="234ms"
    :trend="{ value: 15.3, label: 'vs last hour' }"
    :invert-trend="invertTrend"
  />
</div>
```

## Stepper
Two segments that decrease and increase a number in steps, within a range or wrapping around, as the iOS 26 stepper does.
Import: import { Stepper } from "@plexui/vue/components/Stepper"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/stepper
Props:
  - value (number) — Controlled value; bind with v-model:value
  - default-value (number) default: min — Initial value (uncontrolled)
  - min (number) default: 0 — Lowest value
  - max (number) default: 100 — Highest value
  - step (number) default: 1 — Amount each step adds or removes
  - wrap (boolean) default: false — A step past max continues from min, and one below min from max
  - auto-repeat (boolean) default: true — Holding a segment keeps stepping: after 400ms, every 100ms
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent interaction and focus
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Fully rounded ends
  - decrement-label (string) default: "Decrease" — Accessible name of the minus segment
  - increment-label (string) default: "Increase" — Accessible name of the plus segment
  - name (string) — Submits the value with the owning form through a hidden input
  - aria-label (string) — Name of the value, when no visible label names it
  - aria-labelledby (string) — The id of the visible label of the value
  - aria-describedby (string) — The id of an element that describes the stepper
  - id (string) — The id of the stepper

### Overview
Name the stepper with aria-labelledby, the visible label of its value. A segment at the end of the range turns tertiary and stops stepping. Holding a segment keeps stepping, as a native stepper does.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
  <span id="stepper-guests-label">Guests</span>
  <span class="min-w-5 text-end font-medium tabular-nums" aria-hidden="true">{{ guests }}</span>
  <Stepper aria-labelledby="stepper-guests-label" v-model:value="guests" :min="1" :max="8" />
</div>
```

### In rows
In a Field row the label names the stepper. Put the value in the label, such as 3 copies. On a phone the rows sit on the grouped background of an iOS settings screen.
```vue
<FieldGroup class="w-full max-w-[400px]">
  <template #footer>Up to 20 copies and 14 nights.</template>
  <Field row :label="copies === 1 ? '1 copy' : copies + ' copies'">
    <Stepper v-model:value="copies" :min="1" :max="20" />
  </Field>
  <Field row :label="nights === 1 ? '1 night' : nights + ' nights'">
    <Stepper v-model:value="nights" :min="1" :max="14" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Range, step and wrap
Decimal steps add without rounding drift. With wrap a step past max continues from min, so the hour goes from 23 to 0, and neither segment stops.
```vue
<div class="grid grid-cols-[auto_auto_auto] items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-3">
  <span id="stepper-opacity-label">Opacity</span>
  <span class="text-end font-medium tabular-nums" aria-hidden="true">{{ Math.round(opacity * 100) }}%</span>
  <Stepper aria-labelledby="stepper-opacity-label" v-model:value="opacity" :min="0" :max="1" :step="0.1" />
  <span id="stepper-hour-label">Hour</span>
  <span class="text-end font-medium tabular-nums" aria-hidden="true">{{ String(hour).padStart(2, '0') }}:00</span>
  <Stepper aria-labelledby="stepper-hour-label" v-model:value="hour" :min="0" :max="23" wrap />
</div>
```

### States
At the lowest value the minus segment is tertiary. A disabled stepper takes no focus. On a desktop set :pill="false" for square ends, as other controls in a dense layout have; on a phone the stepper stays the capsule of iOS.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
  <Stepper aria-label="At the lowest value" :default-value="0" />
  <Stepper aria-label="Disabled" :default-value="3" disabled />
  <Stepper aria-label="Square ends" :default-value="3" :pill="false" />
</div>
```

## Switch
Toggle switch for turning settings on or off, with an optional label, a supporting description linked for screen readers, and a configurable label position.
Import: import { Switch } from "@plexui/vue/components/Switch"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/switch
Props:
  - checked / v-model:checked (boolean) — Controlled state
  - default-checked (boolean) — Initial state (uncontrolled)
  - label (string) — Accessible label rendered next to the switch
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content; takes precedence over the label prop
  - description (string) — Secondary text below the label, linked via aria-describedby
  - description slot (VNode[]) — Rich description content; takes precedence over the description prop
  - label-position ("start" | "end") default: "end" — Label on the left (start) or right (end)
  - color ("primary" | "accent") default: "primary" — Checked track color. Accent reads --switch-accent-color, with platform green as its fallback. Disabled checked controls retain a muted accent.
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent interaction
  - required (boolean) — Required for form submission
  - name (string) — Form field name
  - value (string) default: "on" — Value when submitted
  - class (string) — CSS classes applied to the wrapper node
  - id (string) — The id of the switch element (generated when omitted)
  - @update:checked ((next: boolean) => void) — Emitted with the next state when toggled
  - @focus ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the switch gains focus
  - @blur ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the switch loses focus

### Overview
Compare off and on across enabled and disabled states. Enabled controls are interactive. Choose accent for the configured application accent (platform green by default), or primary for the default Switch style. Disabled on retains a muted accent; disabled off uses the neutral track.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<table class="w-full max-w-sm border-separate border-spacing-x-4 border-spacing-y-4 text-sm">
  <caption class="sr-only">Initial switch states. Enabled switches are interactive.</caption>
  <thead>
    <tr>
      <th scope="col" class="text-left font-normal text-secondary">State</th>
      <th scope="col" class="text-center font-normal text-secondary">Off</th>
      <th scope="col" class="text-center font-normal text-secondary">On</th>
    </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
    <tr v-for="disabled in [false, true]" :key="String(disabled)">
      <th scope="row" class="text-left font-normal">{{ disabled ? 'Disabled' : 'Enabled' }}</th>
      <td v-for="checked in [false, true]" :key="String(checked)">
        <div class="flex justify-center">
          <Switch
            :color="color"
            :disabled="disabled"
            :default-checked="checked"
            :aria-label="(disabled ? 'Disabled' : 'Enabled') + ', initially ' + (checked ? 'on' : 'off')"
          />
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>
  </tbody>
</table>

<!-- Phone -->
<div data-switch-phone-rows class="flex w-full grow flex-col gap-6 bg-[var(--mobile-surface-grouped-primary)] px-4 py-6 [--field-group-background:var(--mobile-surface-grouped-secondary)]">
  <FieldGroup v-for="disabled in [false, true]" :key="String(disabled)" :title="disabled ? 'Disabled' : 'Enabled'">
    <Field row label="Initially off"><Switch :color="color" :disabled="disabled" /></Field>
    <Field row label="Initially on"><Switch :color="color" :disabled="disabled" default-checked /></Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Label and description
Click either the label or the control. Descriptions are linked with aria-describedby. Desktop supports both label positions; Phone keeps the label on the left. Use default-checked for an initial value and name for form submission.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col gap-6">
  <Switch color="accent" label="Notifications" :label-position="labelPosition" name="notifications" default-checked />
  <Switch color="accent" label="Weekly summary" description="Sent on Mondays." :label-position="labelPosition" name="summary" />
</div>

<!-- Phone: Field associates the external label and description with the switch. -->
<div data-switch-phone-rows class="w-full grow bg-[var(--mobile-surface-grouped-primary)] px-4 py-6 [--field-group-background:var(--mobile-surface-grouped-secondary)]">
  <FieldGroup>
    <Field row label="Notifications"><Switch color="accent" name="notifications" default-checked /></Field>
    <Field row label="Weekly summary" description="Sent on Mondays."><Switch color="accent" name="summary" /></Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Controlled state
Change checked above or toggle the setting. Both update the same state. Use v-model:checked when your application owns the value.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
const checked = ref(false)
</script>

<!-- Desktop -->
<Switch color="accent" label="Airplane mode" v-model:checked="checked" />

<!-- Phone -->
<div data-switch-phone-rows class="w-full grow bg-[var(--mobile-surface-grouped-primary)] px-4 py-6 [--field-group-background:var(--mobile-surface-grouped-secondary)]">
  <FieldGroup><Field row label="Airplane mode"><Switch color="accent" v-model:checked="checked" /></Field></FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Grouped settings
Group related settings with FieldGroup. Use a row description to explain a locked setting and a footer for help that applies to the group. Phone shows the same groups in a full Page with navigation.
```vue
<!-- Place these groups inside PageContent on Phone. -->
<div class="flex w-full max-w-lg flex-col gap-6"><FieldGroup title="Notifications">
  <Field row label="Push notifications"><Switch color="accent" default-checked /></Field>
  <Field row label="Weekly summary" description="Sent on Mondays."><Switch color="accent" /></Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup title="Privacy">
  <Field row label="Share usage data"><Switch color="accent" /></Field>
  <Field row label="Security alerts" description="Required for your account."><Switch color="accent" default-checked disabled /></Field>
  <template #footer>You can change your data sharing preference at any time.</template>
</FieldGroup></div>
```

## Tab Bar
"The tab bar of a phone screen: two to five sections at the bottom in a floating glass capsule, an optional search tab, minimizing while the content scrolls. Desktop layouts navigate with a Sidebar or Tabs, so a tab bar never renders from 768px up."
Import: import { TabBar, TabBarItem } from "@plexui/vue/components/TabBar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/tab-bar
Props:
  - value (string) — Controlled value of the selected tab; bind with v-model:value
  - default-value (string) — Selected tab (uncontrolled)
  - search ({ value: string; label?: string }) — A search tab in a glass circle of its own at the trailing end; label defaults to Search
  - minimize-behavior ("never" | "onScrollDown") default: "never" — Minimize to the selected tab while the Page content scrolls down
  - minimized (boolean) — Controlled minimized state; bind with v-model:minimized
  - default-minimized (boolean) default: false — Minimized state (uncontrolled)
  - background ("soft" | "hard" | "glass" | "solid") default: "soft" — How the bar meets the content scrolled under it, see Background
  - aria-label (string) default: "Tabs" — Name of the navigation
  TabBarItem:
    - value (string) — Value of the tab
    - label (string) — Label under the symbol and the name of the tab
    - href (string) — Makes the tab a link
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — The tab cannot be selected

### Overview
Put the bar last in a Page: it reserves its height there. Up to three tabs sit in the middle, 94px apart; four and five share the width. The selected tab sits on a pill in the primary text, the others in a lighter gray, as Sidebar items are.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped">
  <Toolbar>
    <ToolbarTitle>Home</ToolbarTitle>
  </Toolbar>
  <PageContent>...</PageContent>
  <TabBar v-model:value="tab">
    <TabBarItem value="home" label="Home">
      <template #icon><Home /></template>
    </TabBarItem>
    <TabBarItem value="calendar" label="Calendar">
      <template #icon><Calendar /></template>
    </TabBarItem>
    <TabBarItem value="photos" label="Photos">
      <template #icon><Images /></template>
    </TabBarItem>
    <TabBarItem value="settings" label="Settings">
      <template #icon><SettingsCog /></template>
    </TabBarItem>
  </TabBar>
</Page>
```

### Search tab
With search the search tab takes a glass circle of its own at the trailing end, as the iOS 26 search tab does. The capsule of the other tabs keeps to the leading end at their width, as in the kit and in Photos: with two tabs it ends well before the circle, with three it reaches 8px from it. It is a tab like the others: show the search screen when its value is selected.
```vue
<TabBar v-model:value="tab" :search="{ value: 'search' }">
  <TabBarItem value="home" label="Home">
    <template #icon><Home /></template>
  </TabBarItem>
  <TabBarItem value="calendar" label="Calendar">
    <template #icon><Calendar /></template>
  </TabBarItem>
  <TabBarItem value="photos" label="Photos">
    <template #icon><Images /></template>
  </TabBarItem>
</TabBar>
```

### Minimize on scroll
With minimize-behavior="onScrollDown" the bar shrinks to the selected tab while the content scrolls down and comes back when it scrolls up, as in iOS 26. Tap the minimized tab to bring the others back.
```vue
<TabBar v-model:value="tab" :search="{ value: 'search' }" minimize-behavior="onScrollDown">
  ...
</TabBar>
```

### Background
background sets how the bar meets the content scrolled under it, as a Toolbar does: soft (the default) fades the canvas color in under the capsule, hard is an opaque band with a line on top, glass a translucent, blurred band with a hairline, and solid an opaque band with no line. Hard and glass show themselves while the content continues under the bar.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped">
  <Toolbar background="hard">...</Toolbar>
  <PageContent>...</PageContent>
  <TabBar v-model:value="tab" background="hard">
    ...
  </TabBar>
</Page>
```

## Table
Styled table primitives for structured data, with header, body, footer, row, cell and caption parts on native table elements.
Import: import { Table, TableHeader, TableBody, TableRow, TableHead, TableCell } from "@plexui/vue/components/Table"
Import: import { DataTable, DataTableColumnHeader } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/table
  Table Primitives:
    - Table (table) — Root table container with horizontal scroll wrapper; attributes go to the <table>
    - TableHeader (thead) — Table header group
    - TableBody (tbody) — Table body group
    - TableFooter (tfoot) — Table footer group
    - TableRow (tr) — Table row
    - TableHead (th) — Table header cell
    - TableCell (td) — Table data cell
    - TableCaption (caption) — Table caption
  DataTable:
    - columns (ColumnDef<TData>[]) — Column definitions following TanStack Table v8 API; header and cell renderers return VNodes (required)
    - data (TData[]) — Data array to display (required)
    - variant ("default" | "bordered") default: "default" — Visual variant: bordered adds a rounded border and tinted header
    - page-size (number) default: 10 — Rows per page. Set 0 or Infinity to disable pagination. The rows-per-page select offers 10, 20, 50, 100 and this size
    - initial-sorting (SortingState) — Initial column sorting state
    - global-filter (string) — Controlled search/filter string. Bind with v-model:global-filter
    - column-visibility (VisibilityState) — Controlled column visibility state. Bind with v-model:column-visibility
    - enable-row-selection (boolean) default: false — Adds a first column (id select) with a checkbox per row and a header checkbox that selects every row on all pages, mixed when some are selected. A click on a checkbox is not a row click
    - row-selection (RowSelectionState) — Controlled row selection state. Bind with v-model:row-selection
    - get-row-id ((row: TData) => string) — Custom row ID accessor; selection keys use it
    - empty-message (string | number | VNode) default: "No results." — Shown when no rows; the emptyMessage slot takes precedence
    - show-pagination (boolean) default: true — Whether to show pagination controls. They hide on their own when every row fits on one page
    - class (string) — Class applied to outermost wrapper
    - emptyMessage slot (VNode[]) — Rich content shown when no rows
    - @row-click ((row: TData) => void) — Click handler for rows. Makes rows interactive: they take Tab and call it on Enter or Space
    - @update:global-filter ((value: string) => void) — Emitted with the next filter when the table changes it; powers v-model:global-filter
    - @update:column-visibility ((visibility: VisibilityState) => void) — Emitted with the next visibility; powers v-model:column-visibility
    - @update:row-selection ((selection: RowSelectionState) => void) — Emitted with the next selection; powers v-model:row-selection
  DataTableColumnHeader:
    - column (Column<TData, TValue>) — TanStack Table column object (required)
    - title (string) — Display text for the header (required)
    - class (string) — Class applied to the header button (or text when the column cannot sort)
  Column helpers:
    - idCell(accessor) (CellComponent) — Monospace ID with copy-on-click tooltip
    - dateTimeCell(accessor, options?) (CellComponent) — Formatted datetime with Intl.DateTimeFormat
    - statusCell(accessor, colorMap?) (CellComponent) — Badge cell with automatic status-to-color mapping
    - tooltipHeader(label, description) (HeaderComponent) — Header with dashed underline and info tooltip

### Overview
A complete table with header, body, footer, and caption showing invoice data.
```vue
<Table>
  <TableCaption>A list of your recent invoices.</TableCaption>
  <TableHeader>
    <TableRow>
      <TableHead>Invoice</TableHead>
      <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
      <TableHead>Method</TableHead>
      <TableHead class="text-right">Amount</TableHead>
    </TableRow>
  </TableHeader>
  <TableBody>
    <TableRow
      v-for="invoice in [
        { invoice: 'INV001', status: 'Paid', method: 'Credit Card', amount: '$250.00' },
        { invoice: 'INV002', status: 'Pending', method: 'PayPal', amount: '$150.00' },
        { invoice: 'INV003', status: 'Unpaid', method: 'Bank Transfer', amount: '$350.00' },
        { invoice: 'INV004', status: 'Paid', method: 'Credit Card', amount: '$450.00' },
        { invoice: 'INV005', status: 'Paid', method: 'PayPal', amount: '$550.00' },
      ]"
      :key="invoice.invoice"
    >
      <TableCell class="font-medium">{{ invoice.invoice }}</TableCell>
      <TableCell>{{ invoice.status }}</TableCell>
      <TableCell>{{ invoice.method }}</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">{{ invoice.amount }}</TableCell>
    </TableRow>
  </TableBody>
  <TableFooter>
    <TableRow>
      <TableCell colspan="3">Total</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">$1,750.00</TableCell>
    </TableRow>
  </TableFooter>
</Table>
```

### With footer
A table with a footer row displaying subtotal information.
```vue
<Table>
  <TableHeader>
    <TableRow>
      <TableHead>Product</TableHead>
      <TableHead class="text-right">Qty</TableHead>
      <TableHead class="text-right">Price</TableHead>
      <TableHead class="text-right">Total</TableHead>
    </TableRow>
  </TableHeader>
  <TableBody>
    <TableRow>
      <TableCell class="font-medium">Pro Plan</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">3</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">$29.00</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">$87.00</TableCell>
    </TableRow>
    <TableRow>
      <TableCell class="font-medium">Storage Add-on</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">1</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">$9.00</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right">$9.00</TableCell>
    </TableRow>
  </TableBody>
  <TableFooter>
    <TableRow>
      <TableCell colspan="3">Subtotal</TableCell>
      <TableCell class="text-right font-semibold">$96.00</TableCell>
    </TableRow>
  </TableFooter>
</Table>
```

### Minimal
A simple table without caption or footer, showing a list of people.
```vue
<Table>
  <TableHeader>
    <TableRow>
      <TableHead>Name</TableHead>
      <TableHead>Title</TableHead>
      <TableHead>Email</TableHead>
    </TableRow>
  </TableHeader>
  <TableBody>
    <TableRow
      v-for="person in [
        { name: 'Lindsay Walton', title: 'Front-end Developer', email: 'lindsay@example.com' },
        { name: 'Courtney Henry', title: 'Designer', email: 'courtney@example.com' },
        { name: 'Tom Cook', title: 'Director of Product', email: 'tom@example.com' },
      ]"
      :key="person.email"
    >
      <TableCell class="font-medium">{{ person.name }}</TableCell>
      <TableCell>{{ person.title }}</TableCell>
      <TableCell>{{ person.email }}</TableCell>
    </TableRow>
  </TableBody>
</Table>
```

### Variant
Use variant="bordered" for a card-like table with a rounded border and tinted header background. Toggle empty to supply no rows without resetting sorting or changing columns. emptyMessage selects the default message, custom text through empty-message, or rich content through the emptyMessage slot. Filtering and its no-results state are shown separately.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h, ref } from "vue"
import { DataTable, DataTableColumnHeader, statusCell, type ColumnDef } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const variant = ref<"default" | "bordered">("bordered")
const empty = ref(false)
const emptyMessage = ref<"default" | "text" | "rich">("text")
const variantUsers = users.slice(0, 6)
const emptyUsers: User[] = []

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: "name",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Name" }),
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name),
  },
  { accessorKey: "role", header: "Role" },
  { accessorKey: "department", header: "Department" },
  { accessorKey: "status", header: "Status", cell: statusCell<User>((row) => row.status) },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable :columns="columns" :data="empty ? emptyUsers : variantUsers"
    :variant="variant" :page-size="Infinity"
    :empty-message="emptyMessage === 'default' ? undefined : 'No users found.'">
    <template v-if="emptyMessage === 'rich'" #emptyMessage>
      <strong>No users found.</strong>
    </template>
  </DataTable>
</template>
```

### Sorting
Click column headers to toggle sort direction. Columns use DataTableColumnHeader for sort controls.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h } from "vue"
import { DataTable, DataTableColumnHeader, type ColumnDef } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: "name",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Name" }),
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name),
  },
  {
    accessorKey: "role",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Role" }),
  },
  {
    accessorKey: "status",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Status" }),
  },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable
    :columns="columns"
    :data="users"
    :page-size="5"
    :initial-sorting="[{ id: 'name', desc: false }]"
  />
</template>
```

### Pagination
Automatic pagination with rows-per-page selector and navigation controls. Set page-size to control the initial page size; the select offers 10, 20, 50 and 100 plus that size. The controls hide when every row fits on one page.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { DataTable, type ColumnDef } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  { accessorKey: "id", header: "ID" },
  { accessorKey: "name", header: "Name" },
  { accessorKey: "department", header: "Department" },
  { accessorKey: "status", header: "Status" },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable :columns="columns" :data="users" :page-size="5" />
</template>
```

### Row click
Listen to @row-click to make rows clickable. Clickable rows also take Tab and call it on Enter or Space.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h, ref } from "vue"
import { DataTable, DataTableColumnHeader, type ColumnDef } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const selectedUser = ref<User | null>(null)

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: "name",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Name" }),
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name),
  },
  { accessorKey: "email", header: "Email" },
  { accessorKey: "role", header: "Role" },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable
    :columns="columns"
    :data="users.slice(0, 8)"
    :page-size="4"
    @row-click="selectedUser = $event"
  />
  <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-secondary">
    {{
      selectedUser
        ? "Clicked: " + selectedUser.name + " (" + selectedUser.id + ") - " + selectedUser.department
        : "Click a row to see selected user details."
    }}
  </p>
</template>
```

### Row selection
Set enable-row-selection to add a first column of checkboxes. The header checkbox selects every row on all pages and shows a mixed state when only some are selected. Bind v-model:row-selection to read the selection; get-row-id keys it by your own ids.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h, ref } from "vue"
import {
  DataTable,
  DataTableColumnHeader,
  statusCell,
  type ColumnDef,
  type RowSelectionState,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const rowSelection = ref<RowSelectionState>({})
const getRowId = (user: User) => user.id

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: "name",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Name" }),
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name),
  },
  { accessorKey: "role", header: "Role" },
  { accessorKey: "status", header: "Status", cell: statusCell<User>((row) => row.status) },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable
    v-model:row-selection="rowSelection"
    :columns="columns"
    :data="users"
    :page-size="5"
    enable-row-selection
    :get-row-id="getRowId"
  />
  <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-secondary">
    {{ Object.keys(rowSelection).length }} of {{ users.length }} rows selected
  </p>
</template>
```

### Cell variants
Column helpers render common cells: idCell (monospace ID with copy-on-click), statusCell (status badge), dateTimeCell (formatted date) and tooltipHeader (header with an info tooltip). Other formats, like the salary below, are plain cell renderers; meta.align aligns a column.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h } from "vue"
import { DataTable, dateTimeCell, idCell, statusCell, tooltipHeader, type ColumnDef } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const money = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: "USD", maximumFractionDigits: 0 })

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  { accessorKey: "id", header: "ID", cell: idCell<User>((row) => row.id) },
  { accessorKey: "name", header: "Name", cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name) },
  { accessorKey: "status", header: "Status", cell: statusCell<User>((row) => row.status) },
  { accessorKey: "joined", header: "Joined", cell: dateTimeCell<User>((row) => row.joined) },
  {
    accessorKey: "salary",
    header: tooltipHeader("Salary", "Annual base salary in USD."),
    meta: { align: "right" },
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, money.format(row.original.salary)),
  },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable :columns="columns" :data="users" :page-size="6" />
</template>
```

### Filtering
Control search with v-model:global-filter. The filter searches across all columns.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h, ref } from "vue"
import { DataTable, DataTableColumnHeader, statusCell, type ColumnDef } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

// Bind the same ref to a search field: <Input v-model:value="globalFilter" />
const globalFilter = ref("")

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: "name",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Name" }),
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name),
  },
  { accessorKey: "role", header: "Role" },
  { accessorKey: "department", header: "Department" },
  { accessorKey: "status", header: "Status", cell: statusCell<User>((row) => row.status) },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable
    :columns="columns"
    :data="users"
    v-model:global-filter="globalFilter"
    :page-size="5"
  />
</template>
```

### Column visibility
Toggle column visibility with v-model:column-visibility.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { h, ref } from "vue"
import { DataTable, DataTableColumnHeader, statusCell, type ColumnDef, type VisibilityState } from "@plexui/vue/components/DataTable"
import { users, type User } from "./data"

const money = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: "USD", maximumFractionDigits: 0 })

const columnVisibility = ref<VisibilityState>({
  role: true,
  department: true,
  status: true,
  salary: true,
})

const columns: ColumnDef<User>[] = [
  {
    accessorKey: "name",
    header: ({ column }) => h(DataTableColumnHeader, { column, title: "Name" }),
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", { class: "font-medium" }, row.original.name),
  },
  { accessorKey: "role", header: "Role" },
  { accessorKey: "department", header: "Department" },
  { accessorKey: "status", header: "Status", cell: statusCell<User>((row) => row.status) },
  {
    accessorKey: "salary",
    header: "Salary",
    meta: { align: "right" },
    cell: ({ row }) => h("span", money.format(row.original.salary)),
  },
]
</script>

<template>
  <DataTable
    :columns="columns"
    :data="users"
    :page-size="5"
    v-model:column-visibility="columnVisibility"
  />
</template>
```

## Tabs
Switch between grouped views with segmented or underline tabs, icon-only triggers, and badge counts on each tab.
Import: import { Tabs, TabsTab } from "@plexui/vue/components/Tabs"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/tabs
Props:
  - value (string) — Selected tab value. Bind with v-model:value; leave unset for an uncontrolled group
  - variant ("segmented" | "underline") default: "segmented" — Visual style: segmented (background highlight) or underline (line indicator)
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Layout direction of the tab group
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height (3xs through 3xl)
  - gutter-size ("2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl") — Horizontal padding of tabs
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Fully rounded control (segmented variant only)
  - flush (boolean) default: false — Text-width indicator, no border, gap spacing (underline variant only)
  - block (boolean) default: false — Full-width control with equal-width tabs
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable all tabs
  - aria-label (string) — Accessible name (required)
  - overflow ("scroll" | "menu") default: "scroll" — Tabs that do not fit: scroll sideways, or move into a menu at the end (horizontal, not block)
  - overflow-label (string) default: "More" — Text of the menu button with overflow="menu"
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the group element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — TabsTab children
  - @update:value ((value: string) => void) — Emitted when a tab is selected; powers v-model:value
  - @change ((value: string) => void) — Emitted with update:value, named after the React onChange prop
  - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted on any click in the group, even when the selection does not change
  TabsTab:
    - value (string) — Tab value (required)
    - disabled (boolean) — Disable this tab
    - icon (Component | VNode | string) — Icon before the label (sized automatically); the icon slot takes precedence
    - badge (number | string | VNode | { content; color?; variant?; pill?; loading? }) — Badge/count or custom badge config
    - aria-label (string) — Accessible name (required for icon-only tabs)
    - default slot (VNode[]) — Tab label
    - icon slot (VNode[]) — Icon before the label
    - badge slot (VNode[]) — Badge content; styling still comes from the badge object

### Overview
Tabs provides two visual variants: segmented (default) with a sliding background highlight, and underline with an animated line indicator. Both support horizontal and vertical orientations. Bind the selected tab with v-model:value; the examples keep it in a tab variable. disabled affects the whole group; reportsDisabled disables only Reports. Arrow-key navigation skips disabled tabs without resetting the selected value. On a phone horizontal tabs of either variant are the iOS segmented control on glass, as toolbar buttons are: segments of one width in a 44px capsule, the selection a darker pill inside it with the primary label and the other labels secondary, filling the row of a phone screen; vertical tabs are an inset grouped list. So the phone view has no variant, flush, block, size and pill controls.
```vue
<Tabs
  v-model:value="tab"
  :variant="variant"
  :orientation="orientation"
  :size="size"
  :pill="pill"
  :flush="flush"
  :block="block"
  :disabled="disabled"
  aria-label="Demo tabs"
>
  <TabsTab value="overview">Overview</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="analytics">Analytics</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="reports" :disabled="reportsDisabled">Reports</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings">Settings</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Segmented with icons
Icons work with the segmented variant: put the icon in the icon slot of individual tabs, or pass a component to the icon prop. Icons are automatically sized to match the current size.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" :size="size" :pill="pill" aria-label="Navigation">
  <TabsTab value="home"><template #icon><Home /></template>Home</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings"><template #icon><SettingsCog /></template>Settings</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="notifications"><template #icon><Bell /></template>Notifications</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Icon only
Leave out the label and fill only the icon slot for compact icon-only tabs. Always provide an aria-label on each tab for accessibility.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" :variant="variant" :size="size" :pill="pill" aria-label="Navigation">
  <TabsTab value="home" aria-label="Home"><template #icon><Home /></template></TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings" aria-label="Settings"><template #icon><SettingsCog /></template></TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="notifications" aria-label="Notifications"><template #icon><Bell /></template></TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Segmented with badge
Pass a number or an object with content, color, variant, and pill to the badge prop. Badges render inline after the tab label inside the segmented container.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" :size="size" :pill="pill" aria-label="Filter">
  <TabsTab value="all" :badge="{ content: 128, pill: badgePill }">All</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="unread" :badge="{ content: 12, color: 'info', pill: badgePill }">Unread</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="flagged" :badge="{ content: 3, color: 'danger', pill: badgePill }">Flagged</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Underline
Set variant="underline" for a minimal tab style with an animated line indicator under the active tab. No background container is rendered. iOS has no underline tabs: on a phone they are the segmented control of Overview, so this example has no Phone preview.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" :size="size" aria-label="Sections">
  <TabsTab value="overview">Overview</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="analytics">Analytics</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="reports">Reports</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings">Settings</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Underline flush
Add flush with variant="underline" for minimal tabs where the indicator matches the text width exactly. No container border is rendered, and spacing between tabs uses gap instead of padding. On a phone these tabs are the segmented control of Overview, so this example has no Phone preview.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" flush :size="size" aria-label="Sections">
  <TabsTab value="overview">Overview</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="analytics">Analytics</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="reports">Reports</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings">Settings</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Underline block
Use block with the underline variant for full-width tabs that stretch evenly across the container. On a phone these tabs are the segmented control of Overview, so this example has no Phone preview.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" block :size="size" aria-label="Sections">
  <TabsTab value="overview">Overview</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="analytics">Analytics</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="reports">Reports</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Underline with icons
Icons work with the underline variant the same way as with segmented. Use the icon slot on individual tabs. On a phone these tabs are the segmented control of Segmented with icons, so this example has no Phone preview.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" :size="size" aria-label="Navigation">
  <TabsTab value="home"><template #icon><Home /></template>Home</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings"><template #icon><SettingsCog /></template>Settings</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="notifications"><template #icon><Bell /></template>Notifications</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Underline with badge
Badges are supported with the underline variant. Pass a simple value or an object with content, color, variant, and pill. On a phone these tabs are the segmented control of Segmented with badge, so this example has no Phone preview.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" :size="size" aria-label="Filter">
  <TabsTab value="all" :badge="{ content: 128, pill: badgePill }">All</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="unread" :badge="{ content: 12, color: 'info', pill: badgePill }">Unread</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="flagged" :badge="{ content: 3, color: 'danger', pill: badgePill }">Flagged</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Vertical segmented
Set orientation="vertical" to stack tabs vertically. The sliding thumb animates up and down. On a phone vertical tabs are an inset grouped list, as iOS lists a choice: 52px rows with the 17px label and a checkmark on the selected row instead of the thumb.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" orientation="vertical" :size="size" aria-label="Settings">
  <TabsTab value="account">Account</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="password">Password</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="notifications">Notifications</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="display">Display</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Vertical underline
Combine variant="underline" with orientation="vertical" for a sidebar-style navigation pattern. The line indicator appears on the left side. On a phone both vertical variants are the list of Vertical segmented, so this example has no Phone preview.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" orientation="vertical" :size="size" aria-label="Settings">
  <TabsTab value="account">Account</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="password">Password</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="notifications">Notifications</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="display">Display</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Vertical underline with icons
Vertical underline tabs with icons provide a rich navigation experience commonly used in settings or admin panels.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" orientation="vertical" :size="size" aria-label="Navigation">
  <TabsTab value="home"><template #icon><Home /></template>Home</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="settings"><template #icon><SettingsCog /></template>Settings</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="notifications"><template #icon><Bell /></template>Notifications</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Sizing
Both variants support the full 9-step size scale. The size prop controls the height, font size, icon size, and spacing of tabs. On a phone with touch.css horizontal tabs of either variant are the 44px glass segmented control of iOS, and vertical tabs are 52px list rows with the 17px label, at every size. The phone view shows each example at its default size, without the size and pill controls.
```vue
<Tabs v-model:value="tab" :variant="variant" :size="size" aria-label="Demo tabs">
  <TabsTab value="overview">Overview</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="analytics">Analytics</TabsTab>
  <TabsTab value="reports">Reports</TabsTab>
</Tabs>
```

### Scrollable
When tabs overflow their container, they automatically become horizontally scrollable. The active tab scrolls into view smoothly. No extra prop is needed: just constrain the container width. A phone scrolls them the same way inside the glass capsule, as Safari's tab groups and Telegram's chat folders do, and each segment then takes the width of its label.
```vue
<div class="flex w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Tabs v-model:value="tab" :variant="variant" :size="size" aria-label="Scrollable tabs">
    <TabsTab value="1">Overview</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="2">Analytics</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="3">Reports</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="4">Settings</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="5">Members</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="6">Billing</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="7">Security</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="8">Integrations</TabsTab>
  </Tabs>
</div>
```

### Overflow menu
With overflow="menu", the tabs that do not fit stay out of the row and move into a menu at its end, instead of a sideways scroll. When the active tab is a hidden one, the menu button shows its name and carries the selection. A tab's badge moves into the menu with it. overflow-label sets the button's text (default "More"). Works for horizontal tabs without block.
```vue
<div class="flex min-w-0 max-w-full" :style="{ width: width + 'px' }">
  <Tabs v-model:value="tab" variant="underline" overflow="menu" aria-label="Repository">
    <TabsTab value="code">Code</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="issues" :badge="12">Issues</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="pulls" :badge="3">Pull requests</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="actions">Actions</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="projects">Projects</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="insights">Insights</TabsTab>
    <TabsTab value="settings">Settings</TabsTab>
  </Tabs>
</div>
```

## Tag Input
Input for entering multiple unique values as chip-style tokens, with suggestions and keyboard editing.
Import: import { TagInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/TagInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/tag-input
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (Tag[]) — Controlled value; update:value fires on every change
  - default-value (Tag[]) — Uncontrolled initial tags
  - @change ((tags: Tag[]) => void) — Fires when the tags change
  - id (string) — Id of the text input, so a label can target it with for
  - placeholder (string) — Input placeholder
  - validator ((value: string) => boolean) — Validates each tag when it is created; invalid tags shown in red
  - max-tags (number) — Maximum number of tags. At the limit the text input hides and a count shows below; the container then takes the tab stop, so arrow keys and Backspace still select and remove tags
  - rows (number) default: 1 — Min rows
  - size ('sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | '3xl') default: xl — Control height when rows=1; matches Input height of the same size
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully-rounded container and chips; matches Input/SelectControl pill
  - delimiters (string[]) default: [",", " "] — Characters that commit the current text as a tag
  - disabled (boolean) — Disables the input: tags cannot be added or removed, and pending text stays as typed
  - auto-focus (boolean) default: false — Focus on mount

### Overview
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[480px]">
  <TagInput placeholder="Add a tag..." :size="size" :rows="Number(rows)" :pill="pill" />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the tags are the value of a row, under its label. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Tags">
    <TagInput variant="ghost" placeholder="Add a tag..." :rows="Number(rows)" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Inline filter row
When rows=1, TagInput matches the height of an Input or SelectControl of the same size, so it composes cleanly into horizontal filter rows. Toggle pill on both controls to keep the pair visually consistent.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex w-full max-w-2xl items-start gap-2">
  <Field label="Where" class="w-[160px] shrink-0">
    <Menu>
      <MenuTrigger>
        <SelectControl :size="size" :pill="pill" :selected="!!field" class="w-full">
          {{ fieldOptions.find((option) => option.value === field)?.label ?? '' }}
        </SelectControl>
      </MenuTrigger>
      <MenuContent :min-width="160" @close-auto-focus="(event) => event.preventDefault()">
        <MenuCheckboxItem
          v-for="option in fieldOptions"
          :key="option.value"
          :checked="field === option.value"
          indicator-variant="ghost"
          @update:checked="field = option.value"
        >
          {{ option.label }}
        </MenuCheckboxItem>
      </MenuContent>
    </Menu>
  </Field>
  <Field label="Is in" class="flex-1">
    <TagInput :size="size" :pill="pill" v-model:value="tags" placeholder="Add value, press Enter…" />
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Where">
    <template #default="control">
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger>
          <SelectControl v-bind="control" :selected="!!field">
            {{ fieldOptions.find((option) => option.value === field)?.label ?? '' }}
          </SelectControl>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent :min-width="160" align="end" @close-auto-focus="(event) => event.preventDefault()">
          <MenuCheckboxItem
            v-for="option in fieldOptions"
            :key="option.value"
            :checked="field === option.value"
            indicator-position="start"
            indicator-variant="ghost"
            @update:checked="field = option.value"
          >
            {{ option.label }}
          </MenuCheckboxItem>
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Is in">
    <TagInput variant="ghost" v-model:value="tags" placeholder="Add a value" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Suggestions
Combine TagInput with clickable buttons to let users add tags by clicking suggestions or typing manually. Suggestions disappear once selected.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<script setup lang="ts">
import { computed, ref } from "vue"
import { Button } from "@plexui/vue/components/Button"
import { TagInput, type Tag } from "@plexui/vue/components/TagInput"

const SUGGESTIONS = ["React", "TypeScript", "Next.js", "Tailwind CSS", "Node.js", "GraphQL", "PostgreSQL", "Redis", "Docker", "AWS"]

const tags = ref<Tag[]>([
  { value: "React", valid: true },
  { value: "TypeScript", valid: true },
])

const available = computed(() => {
  const selected = new Set(tags.value.map((t) => t.value))
  return SUGGESTIONS.filter((s) => !selected.has(s))
})
</script>

<template>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
    <TagInput v-model:value="tags" placeholder="Add a skill..." size="md" />
    <div v-if="available.length > 0" class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
      <Button
        v-for="s in available"
        :key="s"
        size="xs"
        color="secondary"
        variant="soft"
        :pill="false"
        @click="tags = [...tags, { value: s, valid: true }]"
      >
        {{ s }}
      </Button>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<!-- Phone: each suggestion is a row with its Add button. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Skills">
    <TagInput variant="ghost" v-model:value="tags" placeholder="Add a skill..." />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup v-if="suggestions.some((s) => !tags.some((t) => t.value === s))" title="Suggestions">
  <template v-for="s in suggestions" :key="s">
    <Field v-if="!tags.some((t) => t.value === s)" row :label="s">
      <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" :aria-label="'Add ' + s" @click="tags = [...tags, { value: s, valid: true }]">
        Add
      </Button>
    </Field>
  </template>
</FieldGroup>
```

## Text Link
Text link for inline internal and external links with router integration, color inheritance, underline control, and weight options.
Import: import { TextLink } from "@plexui/vue/components/TextLink"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/text-link
Props:
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Link content
  - href (string) — URL. An http:// or https:// URL is external
  - to (string | Record<string, unknown>) — Router link destination (internal), such as a vue-router location
  - primary (boolean) default: false — Primary color, no default underline
  - underline (boolean) default: true — Underline (default true unless primary)
  - force-external (boolean) — Force external link behavior
  - as (Component) — Link component (e.g. RouterLink, NuxtLink)
  - target (string) — Where to open the link. External links default to _blank
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the link

### Overview
```vue
<div class="flex gap-4">
  <TextLink href="#">Read more</TextLink>
  <TextLink href="#" primary>External link <ArrowUpRight /></TextLink>
</div>
```

### Inline usage
```vue
<p>
  You can use the <TextLink href="#">responses endpoint</TextLink> to generate text. You can
  either use the API directly from an HTTP client of your choice, or use one of the
  <TextLink href="#">official SDKs</TextLink> for your preferred language.
</p>
```

### Default inheritance
By default, TextLink uses currentcolor inheritance and text-decoration: underline to create subtle visual differentiation from surrounding text. The hover effect is a dynamic deemphasis of the currentcolor, with some special cases for text-secondary and text-tertiary contexts.You can see this behavior by changing the text color context of the paragraph the links are in below:
```vue
<p :class="colorClassName">
  You can use the <TextLink href="#">responses endpoint</TextLink> to generate text. You can
  either use the API directly from an HTTP client of your choice, or use one of the
  <TextLink href="#">official SDKs</TextLink> for your preferred language.
</p>
```

### Primary color
You can create a colorful link style with the primary prop, which uses color for visual differentiation.Primary links also remove the default underline treatment: pass underline to re-apply that style.
```vue
<p>
  Sample text with a <TextLink href="#" :primary="primary">primary link</TextLink>
</p>
```

### Underline
Links have an underlined aesthetic by default.Pass {':underline="false"'} to remove the underline from the link.
```vue
<p class="text-secondary">
  Sample text with a <TextLink href="#" :underline="underline">subtle link</TextLink>
</p>
```

### External links
When external paths are provided, {''} will be used with {'target="_blank"'} and {'rel="noopener noreferrer"'} included by default. You can also compose icons along with the text to add visual indication.
```vue
<TextLink href="#" primary underline>
  External link <ArrowUpRight />
</TextLink>
```

### Font weight
Use the class attribute to customize font weight.
```vue
<TextLink href="#" class="font-semibold" :underline="false">
  Contact support <ArrowRight />
</TextLink>
```

## Textarea
Multi-line text input that can grow with its content, with a size scale, autofill extension control, and invalid state styling.
Import: import { Textarea } from "@plexui/vue/components/Textarea"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/textarea
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (string | number) — Controlled value; uncontrolled when omitted
  - default-value (string | number) — Initial value when uncontrolled
  - variant ("outline" | "soft" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Visual style of the textarea; ghost has no border and no fill, for a row or a container that frames it
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height for single-line equivalent
  - gutter-size ("2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl") — Horizontal padding; defaults from size
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the textarea
  - read-only (boolean) default: false — Make the textarea read-only (the native readonly attribute works too)
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark as invalid
  - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: !!name — Allow autofill extensions
  - auto-select (boolean) default: false — Select all contents when mounted
  - rows (number) default: 3 — Initial row count
  - auto-resize (boolean) default: false — Grow/shrink with content
  - max-rows (number) — Max rows when auto-resize; defaults to the larger of rows and 10
  - class (string) — Applied to the container <div>
  - @update:value ((value: string) => void) — Emitted on every input
  - @autofill (() => void) — Emitted when the browser autofills the textarea

### Overview
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea placeholder="Enter text..." auto-resize :rows="3" />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the textarea is the value of a row, under its label. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Notes">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" placeholder="Add a note" auto-resize :rows="2" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the textarea. ghost has no border and no fill, for a field its container already frames. On Phone the row is the frame, so the textarea is ghost under the row's label.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-6">
    <div v-for="variant in ['outline', 'soft']" :key="variant" class="flex items-center">
      <div class="text-right text-sm mr-8 min-w-[4rem]" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ variant }}</div>
      <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Textarea placeholder="Enter text..." class="w-full" :variant="variant" :rows="3" />
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Note for the courier">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" placeholder="Gate code, floor, a safe place to leave it" auto-resize :rows="2" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Auto resize
Enable auto-resize to automatically grow/shrink the textarea as the user types. Control the bounds with rows (initial height) and max-rows. Bind the text with v-model:value. On Phone the row grows with it.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea
    v-model:value="value"
    placeholder="Type to grow..."
    :auto-resize="autoResize"
    :rows="Number(rows)"
    :max-rows="Number(maxRows)"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Notes">
    <Textarea
      v-model:value="value"
      variant="ghost"
      placeholder="Type to grow..."
      :auto-resize="autoResize"
      :rows="Number(rows)"
      :max-rows="Number(maxRows)"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Sizing
Sizing of the textarea is controlled with size and gutter-size props. size sets the textarea to a specific control height for single-line equivalent, and gutter-size sets the horizontal padding.Each size provides default values for gutter-size, as well as font-size and border-radius. Each of these values can be overridden independently.On Phone the row sets the type and insets, so a textarea in a row takes no size or gutter-size; rows sets its height. This example is desktop only, as Input's Sizing is.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea placeholder="Write a message..." :size="size" :gutter-size="gutterSize" />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Message">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" placeholder="Write a message..." :rows="4" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Disabled
Use the disabled prop to disable the textarea, greying it out and preventing user interactions.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea :disabled="disabled" default-value="Jane Doe" aria-label="Name" />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Notes">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" default-value="Leave it at the front desk." auto-resize :rows="2" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Invalid
Use the invalid prop to mark the textarea as invalid. You decide how this state is removed, whether that's through @focus, @input, a submit handler, etc.Default visual styles will be applied, based on the variant. Additionally, [data-invalid] is applied to the container for custom styling.On Phone the row is the frame, so the error is the error-message line under the row.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea :invalid="invalid" placeholder="Invalid textarea" />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Reference" :error-message="invalid ? 'Keep it under 140 characters.' : undefined">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" :default-value="reference" :invalid="invalid" auto-resize :rows="2" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Auto select text
Pass auto-select to select all text in the textarea, useful when pairing with other copy actions. This prop behaves like an imperative action when toggled and allows selection to change.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea default-value="Toggle to auto select" :auto-select="autoSelect" aria-label="Notes" />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row orientation="vertical" label="Notes">
    <Textarea variant="ghost" default-value="Toggle to auto select" :auto-select="autoSelect" auto-resize :rows="2" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Autofill extensions
By default, autofill extensions are prevented by Textarea. For the occasions where you want browser extensions to be present, pass allow-autofill-extensions to remove the disabling.When name is passed this value will default to true. Browser autofill extensions are a desktop matter: on iOS autofill comes from the system above the keyboard, so this example is desktop only.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <Textarea
    :key="String(allowAutofillExtensions)"
```
